  const tierPairs=group=>{
    const a=group.slice().sort((x,y)=>cy(x)-cy(y));
    if(a.length!==6) return null;
    const pair=i=>a.slice(i,i+2).sort((x,y)=>cx(x)-cx(y));
    return [pair(0),pair(2),pair(4)];
  };
  const rearTiers=tierPairs(rear), forwardTiers=tierPairs(forward);
  if(!rearTiers || !forwardTiers) return null;
  // Prompt convention: far-side pieces are slightly left; near-side pieces slightly right.
  // Pair arrays are sorted left-to-right, so reverse them into near (L) then far (R) slots.
  const [frontUpper,frontLower,frontTips]=forwardTiers;
  const [hindUpper,hindLower,hindFeet]=rearTiers;
  const validPairs=[...forwardTiers,...rearTiers];
  if(validPairs.some(p=>p.length!==2)) return null;
  return [
    head, torso,
    frontUpper[1], frontUpper[0],
    frontLower[1], frontTips[1], frontLower[0], frontTips[0],
    hindUpper[1], hindUpper[0],
    hindLower[1], hindLower[0],
    hindFeet[1], hindFeet[0]
  ];
}

// New 15-part species sheets add one isolated, single-piece tail behind the rump.
// Detect the rump and head first, identify the horizontal rear appendage, then reuse the established
// front-wing/front-leg and hind-leg pairing logic for the remaining 14 anatomical pieces.
function sheetOrderSpeciesAnatomy15(parts,w,h,modelType){
  if(!parts || parts.length!==15 || (modelType!=='animal' && modelType!=='bird')) return null;
  const M=sheetFigureMetrics(parts), cx=M.cx, cy=M.cy;
  const horizontal=parts.filter(b=>M.bw(b)>=M.w*.24 && M.bw(b)>M.bh(b)*1.18 && M.yr(b)>.12 && M.yr(b)<.82);
  if(!horizontal.length) return null;
  const torso=horizontal.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
    const score=x=>x.n*1.0 + M.bw(x)*.15 - Math.abs(cx(x)-(M.x0+M.w*.49))*.12 - Math.abs(cy(x)-(M.y0+M.h*.42))*.05;
    return score(b)-score(a);
  })[0];
  let headPool=parts.filter(b=>b!==torso && cx(b)>cx(torso)+M.w*.08 && cy(b)<cy(torso)+M.h*.10 && M.yr(b)<.65);
  if(!headPool.length) headPool=parts.filter(b=>b!==torso && cx(b)>cx(torso)+M.w*.10).sort((a,b)=>cy(a)-cy(b)).slice(0,3);
  if(!headPool.length) return null;
  const head=headPool.sort((a,b)=>{
    const score=x=>(cx(x)-cx(torso))*.75+(cy(torso)-cy(x))*.35+Math.sqrt(Math.max(1,x.n))*.025;
    return score(b)-score(a);
  })[0];
  const tailPool=parts.filter(b=>b!==torso && b!==head).filter(b=>{
    const aspect=M.bw(b)/Math.max(1,M.bh(b));
    const rear=cx(torso)-cx(b);
    const yOffset=Math.abs(cy(b)-cy(torso));
    return rear>M.w*.12 && M.bw(b)>M.w*.055 && aspect>1.02 && yOffset<M.h*.34 && M.yr(b)>.10 && M.yr(b)<.88;
  });
  if(!tailPool.length) return null;
  const tail=tailPool.sort((a,b)=>{
    const score=x=>{
      const rear=(cx(torso)-cx(x))/M.w;
      const aspect=M.bw(x)/Math.max(1,M.bh(x));
      const yPenalty=Math.abs(cy(x)-cy(torso))/M.h;
      return Math.log(1+Math.max(1,x.n))*.35 + M.bw(x)/M.w*.8 + Math.min(3,aspect)*.12 + rear*.45 - yPenalty*.55;
    };
    return score(b)-score(a);
  })[0];
  const base=sheetOrderSpeciesAnatomy14(parts.filter(b=>b!==tail),w,h,modelType);
  if(!base || base.length!==14 || base[0]===tail || base[1]===tail) return null;
  return [base[0],base[1],tail,...base.slice(2)];
}

// A fused forearm+hand should be split at the narrow wrist transition, not at an arbitrary
// fixed percentage. Use the source alpha mask to find the narrowest stable row around the likely
// wrist band; this preserves the wrist cuff/base when generated sheets have different proportions.
function sheetFindWristCut(mask,w,part){
  const partH=part.y1-part.y0+1;
  if(!mask || !mask.length || !w || !partH) return Math.round(part.y0+partH*.68);
  const fullH=Math.floor(mask.length/w);
  if(fullH<=0) return Math.round(part.y0+partH*.68);
  const lo=Math.max(part.y0+1,part.y0+Math.round(partH*.50));
  const hi=Math.min(part.y1-1,part.y0+Math.round(partH*.82));
  if(hi<=lo) return Math.round(part.y0+partH*.68);
  const widths=[];
  for(let y=Math.max(0,lo-2);y<=Math.min(fullH-1,hi+2);y++){
    let n=0;
    for(let x=Math.max(0,part.x0);x<=Math.min(w-1,part.x1);x++) if(mask[y*w+x]) n++;
    widths.push({y,n});
  }
  const widthAt=y=>{const a=widths.filter(v=>Math.abs(v.y-y)<=1);return a.length?a.reduce((sum,v)=>sum+v.n,0)/a.length:Infinity;};
  let bestY=Math.round(part.y0+partH*.68),bestScore=Infinity;
  for(let y=lo;y<=hi;y++){
    const here=widthAt(y),before=widthAt(y-3),after=widthAt(y+3);
    // Prefer a real local pinch (forearm narrows into the wrist before the hand widens).
    const pinch=(here<=before && here<=after)?0:Math.max(1,Math.min(before,after)-here+1)*.35;
    const target=Math.abs(y-(part.y0+partH*.68))*0.12;
    const score=here+pinch+target;
    if(score<bestScore){bestScore=score;bestY=y;}
  }
  return Math.max(part.y0+1,Math.min(part.y1,bestY));
}

// IMPORTANT: Do not trim or recolour the recovered torso/waist automatically.
// The dark-blue triangular waistband/upper-trouser section is real character artwork
// and must be preserved exactly as it appears on the uploaded model sheet.

function sheetRecoverAnatomy14From12(parts,w,h,mask){
  if(!parts || parts.length!==12) return null;
  const M=sheetFigureMetrics(parts);
  if(M.h < M.w*1.2) return null;
  // A top-centre component spanning the head, neck and torso is the tell-tale merged piece.
  const corePool=parts.filter(b=>b.y0<=M.y0+M.h*.035 && M.bh(b)>M.h*.36 && M.dx(b)<.20 && M.bw(b)>M.w*.22);
  if(!corePool.length) return null;
  const core=corePool.sort((a,b)=>M.bh(b)-M.bh(a))[0];
  const upper=parts.filter(b=>b!==core && M.yr(b)>.12 && M.yr(b)<.42 && M.dx(b)>.18).sort((a,b)=>M.cx(a)-M.cx(b));
  if(upper.length!==2) return null;
  const shoes=parts.filter(b=>b!==core && !upper.includes(b) && M.yr(b)>.89 && b.y0>M.y0+M.h*.83).sort((a,b)=>M.cx(a)-M.cx(b));
  if(shoes.length!==2) return null;
  const excluded=new Set([core,...upper,...shoes]);
  const shins=parts.filter(b=>!excluded.has(b) && M.yr(b)>.66 && M.yr(b)<.91 && M.dx(b)<.31 && b.y0>M.y0+M.h*.58).sort((a,b)=>M.cx(a)-M.cx(b));
  if(shins.length!==2) return null; shins.forEach(b=>excluded.add(b));
  const thighs=parts.filter(b=>!excluded.has(b) && M.yr(b)>.42 && M.yr(b)<.70 && M.dx(b)<.25 && b.y0>M.y0+M.h*.35).sort((a,b)=>M.cx(a)-M.cx(b));
  if(thighs.length!==2) return null; thighs.forEach(b=>excluded.add(b));
  const lower=parts.filter(b=>!excluded.has(b) && M.yr(b)>.31 && M.yr(b)<.68 && M.dx(b)>.17);
  if(lower.length!==3) return null;
  const left=lower.filter(b=>M.cx(b)<M.mid).sort((a,b)=>M.cy(a)-M.cy(b));
  const right=lower.filter(b=>M.cx(b)>=M.mid).sort((a,b)=>M.cy(a)-M.cy(b));
  if(!((left.length===2&&right.length===1)||(left.length===1&&right.length===2))) return null;

  // Split at the jaw/neck root, not halfway through the head+torso blob. In the 14-part
  // anatomical sheet, hair/face occupies about the upper third of this merged central region;
  // the throat, shirt and the original pants-start/waist section all belong to Body.
  // Prefer the narrowest occupied row around the lower face, which is a useful jaw-to-neck cue,
  // with a 35% fallback for sheets whose antialiasing obscures that contour.
  const coreH=M.bh(core);
  let coreCut=Math.round(core.y0+coreH*.35);
  if(mask && mask.length===w*h){
    const lo=Math.max(core.y0+1,Math.round(core.y0+coreH*.27));
    const hi=Math.min(core.y1-1,Math.round(core.y0+coreH*.41));
    let bestScore=Infinity, bestY=coreCut;
    for(let yy=lo;yy<=hi;yy++){
      let occupied=0;
      for(let xx=core.x0;xx<=core.x1;xx++) if(mask[yy*w+xx]) occupied++;
      if(!occupied) continue; // never cut in an entirely transparent gap above the neck
      // Light bias toward the jaw/neck boundary around 35%, while favouring the narrowest row.
      const score=occupied + Math.abs(yy-(core.y0+coreH*.35))*.18;
      if(score<bestScore){ bestScore=score; bestY=yy; }
    }
    coreCut=bestY;
  }
  coreCut=Math.max(core.y0+Math.round(coreH*.29),Math.min(core.y0+Math.round(coreH*.40),coreCut));
  coreCut=Math.max(core.y0+1,Math.min(core.y1,coreCut));
  const head={...core,y1:coreCut-1,cropClipY1:coreCut-1,cropExcludeBlueFrom:Math.max(core.y0,coreCut-Math.round(coreH*.035)),n:Math.max(1,core.n*.35),recoveredSplit:true};
  const torso={...core,y0:coreCut,cropClipY0:coreCut,n:Math.max(1,core.n*.65),recoveredSplit:true};
  let forearmL,handL,forearmR,handR;
  if(left.length===2){ forearmL=left[0]; handL=left[1]; const fused=right[0]; const cut=sheetFindWristCut(mask,w,fused); forearmR={...fused,y1:cut,cropClipY1:cut,n:fused.n*.60,recoveredSplit:true}; handR={...fused,y0:cut,cropClipY0:cut,n:fused.n*.40,recoveredSplit:true}; }
  else { forearmR=right[0]; handR=right[1]; const fused=left[0]; const cut=sheetFindWristCut(mask,w,fused); forearmL={...fused,y1:cut,cropClipY1:cut,n:fused.n*.60,recoveredSplit:true}; handL={...fused,y0:cut,cropClipY0:cut,n:fused.n*.40,recoveredSplit:true}; }
  return [head,torso,upper[0],upper[1],forearmL,handL,forearmR,handR,thighs[0],thighs[1],shins[0],shins[1],shoes[0],shoes[1]];
}

function sheetParse(img, modelType){
  const sc = Math.min(1, 2200/Math.max(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight)), W = Math.round(img.naturalWidth*sc), H = Math.round(img.naturalHeight*sc), N = W*H;
  const cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = W; cv.height = H;
  const g = cv.getContext('2d', {willReadFrequently:true}); g.drawImage(img, 0, 0, W, H);
  const id = g.getImageData(0,0,W,H), d = id.data;
  // opaque sheet (JPG / flat background): flood-remove the border-connected background colour.
  // Real transparent PNGs (which already have plenty of see-through pixels) skip this whole step,
  // so the safest fix for the "background eats part of the model" problem is just exporting a true
  // alpha PNG in the first place -- this heuristic is only a fallback for flat-colour uploads.
  let clear = 0; for(let i=3;i<d.length;i+=4) if(d[i]<200) clear++;
  if(clear < N*0.01){
    // Robust background estimate: sample many points along the border (not just the 4 corners --
    // a single corner can land on an arm/leg that was drawn too close to the edge) and take the
    // median of each channel, which shrugs off a handful of corner/edge samples that are actually
    // character pixels rather than true background.
    const samples = [], step = Math.max(1, Math.floor((2*W+2*H)/400));
    for(let x=0;x<W;x+=step){ samples.push((x)*4, ((H-1)*W+x)*4); }
    for(let y=0;y<H;y+=step){ samples.push((y*W)*4, (y*W+W-1)*4); }
    const median = k=>{ const v = samples.map(i=>d[i+k]).sort((a,b)=>a-b); return v[Math.floor(v.length/2)]; };
    const bg = [0,1,2].map(median);
    // Tighter tolerance than before: wide enough to clear anti-aliased edge pixels blending into the
    // background, tight enough that dark-but-not-black clothing/hair (navy, charcoal) doesn't read as
    // "background" just because it's also dark.
    const near = p=>Math.abs(d[p*4]-bg[0])<24 && Math.abs(d[p*4+1]-bg[1])<24 && Math.abs(d[p*4+2]-bg[2])<24;
    const seen = new Uint8Array(N), st = [], push = p=>{ if(!seen[p] && near(p)){ seen[p]=1; st.push(p); } };
    for(let x=0;x<W;x++){ push(x); push((H-1)*W+x); } for(let y=0;y<H;y++){ push(y*W); push(y*W+W-1); }
    while(st.length){ const p = st.pop(), x = p%W; d[p*4+3] = 0; if(x>0) push(p-1); if(x<W-1) push(p+1); if(p>=W) push(p-W); if(p<N-W) push(p+W); }
  }
  // split into separate parts: label connected blobs on a small, slightly dilated mask
  const s = Math.min(1, 600/Math.max(W,H)), w = Math.max(1, Math.round(W*s)), h = Math.max(1, Math.round(H*s)), R = 0; // no dilation: preserve narrow transparent gaps so neighbouring parts never merge
  const cx = x=>Math.min(w-1, Math.floor(x*s)), cy = y=>Math.min(h-1, Math.floor(y*s)), m = new Uint8Array(w*h), dm = new Uint8Array(w*h);
  for(let y=0;y<H;y++) for(let x=0;x<W;x++) if(d[(y*W+x)*4+3]>30) m[cy(y)*w+cx(x)] = 1;
  for(let y=0;y<h;y++) for(let x=0;x<w;x++) if(m[y*w+x]) for(let j=-R;j<=R;j++) for(let i=-R;i<=R;i++){ const X=x+i, Y=y+j; if(X>=0&&X<w&&Y>=0&&Y<h) dm[Y*w+X] = 1; }
  const lab = new Int32Array(w*h), blobs = [];
  for(let p=0;p<w*h;p++){
    if(!dm[p] || lab[p]) continue;
    const b = { id:blobs.length+1, x0:w, y0:h, x1:0, y1:0, n:0 }, st = [p]; lab[p] = b.id;
    while(st.length){
      const q = st.pop(), x = q%w, y = (q-x)/w; b.n++; b.x0 = Math.min(b.x0,x); b.x1 = Math.max(b.x1,x); b.y0 = Math.min(b.y0,y); b.y1 = Math.max(b.y1,y);
      [x>0&&q-1, x<w-1&&q+1, y>0&&q-w, y<h-1&&q+w].forEach(r=>{ if(r!==false && dm[r] && !lab[r]){ lab[r] = b.id; st.push(r); } });
    }
    blobs.push(b);
  }
  let sorted = blobs.filter(b=>b.n >= w*h*0.00035).sort((a,b)=>b.n-a.n); // retain small mouth/gesture parts
  // Some generators let the three neighbouring expression heads touch at their hair outlines. They
  // then become ONE connected blob even though the sheet still contains all 27 intended pieces.
  // Recover those side-by-side heads only when the total is short and a tall, unusually-wide blob
  // clearly has room for multiple head-sized pieces. Each recovered slice keeps the original label,
  // but cropClipX restricts its pixels so the same merged blob is not copied into every slice.
  let nParts = sorted.length;
  if(nParts >= 22 && nParts < 27){
    const missing = 27-nParts;
    const wideCandidates = sorted.map(b=>{
      const bw=b.x1-b.x0+1, bh=b.y1-b.y0+1;
      return { b, bw, bh, aspect:bw/Math.max(1,bh) };
    }).filter(c=>c.bw > c.bh*1.55 && c.bh >= h*0.07)
      .sort((a,b)=>b.aspect-a.aspect);
    for(const c of wideCandidates){
      const currentMissing = 27-sorted.length;
      if(currentMissing<=0) break;
      const guess = Math.max(2, Math.min(4, Math.round(c.bw/(c.bh*0.72))));
      // Do not over-count: the split must explain exactly the remaining missing items.
      if(guess-1 !== currentMissing) continue;
      const parts=[];
      for(let j=0;j<guess;j++){
        const x0=Math.floor(c.b.x0+j*c.bw/guess);
        const x1=j===guess-1?c.b.x1:Math.floor(c.b.x0+(j+1)*c.bw/guess)-1;
        if(x1<x0) continue;
        parts.push({...c.b, x0, x1, cropClipX0:x0, cropClipX1:x1, n:c.b.n/guess, recoveredSplit:true});
      }
      if(parts.length===guess){
        sorted=sorted.flatMap(b=>b.id===c.b.id?parts:[b]);
      }
    }
    nParts=sorted.length;
  }
  // New humanoid uploads use the anatomy-position 14-part layout (with legacy 12/27 formats kept
  // for compatibility). New animal and bird uploads use the corresponding species-specific 14-part layout.
  const type = modelType || 'humanoid';
  let layout, COLS, layoutMode = 'legacy27', ordered = null;
  const top = sorted.slice(0, nParts);
  if(type === 'humanoid' && nParts === 12){
    layout = SHEET_KEYS_12;
    const isGrid = sheetLooksLikeRegularPartGrid(top,w,h);
    // Only run the anatomy/merged-region heuristics on a genuine standing-character layout.
    // A grid with 12 components must keep its row-major part order.
    const recovered14 = !isGrid ? sheetRecoverAnatomy14From12(top, w, h, m) : null;
    if(recovered14){ layout = SHEET_KEYS_14; ordered = recovered14; layoutMode = 'anatomy14'; }
    else {
      const strictAnatomy = !isGrid && sheetIsAnatomy12(top, w, h) ? sheetOrderAnatomy12(top, w, h) : null;
      const anatomy = !isGrid ? (strictAnatomy || sheetOrderAnatomy12ByPosition(top,w,h)) : null;
      if(anatomy){ ordered = anatomy; layoutMode = 'anatomy12'; }
      else { COLS = 6; layoutMode = 'legacy12'; }
    }
  }else if(type === 'humanoid' && nParts === 14){
    layout = SHEET_KEYS_14;
    const isGrid = sheetLooksLikeRegularPartGrid(top,w,h);
    const strictAnatomy = !isGrid ? sheetOrderAnatomy14(top, w, h) : null;
    const anatomy = !isGrid ? (strictAnatomy || sheetOrderAnatomy14ByPosition(top,w,h)) : null;
    if(anatomy){ ordered = anatomy; layoutMode = 'anatomy14'; }
    else { COLS = 4; layoutMode = 'base14'; }
  }
  else if((type === 'animal' || type === 'bird') && nParts === 15){
    layout = SHEET_KEYS_SPECIES_15;
    ordered = sheetOrderSpeciesAnatomy15(top, w, h, type);
    if(ordered) layoutMode = 'species15';
    else {
      return { error:'Could not recognise the 15-piece '+type+' anatomy layout. Use the matching prompt: head/front at the right, horizontal body in the centre, one separate tail at the rear/left, six front-leg/wing pieces and six hind-leg/foot pieces, all separated by transparent gaps.', detectedRegions:nParts };
    }
  }
  else if((type === 'animal' || type === 'bird') && nParts === 14){
    layout = SHEET_KEYS_14;
    ordered = sheetOrderSpeciesAnatomy14(top, w, h, type);
    if(ordered) layoutMode = 'species14';
    else {
      return { error:'Could not recognise the 14-piece legacy '+type+' anatomy layout. Existing 14-piece uploads are supported; new sheets should use the 15-piece prompt with a separate tail.', detectedRegions:nParts };
    }
  }
  else if(type === 'humanoid-variants' && nParts === 16){
    layout = SHEET_KEYS_VARIANTS_16; COLS = 4; layoutMode = 'variants16';
  }
  else if(type === 'humanoid-variants' && nParts === 13){
    layout = SHEET_KEYS_VARIANTS_13; COLS = 3; layoutMode = 'variants13';
  }
  else if(type === 'species-variants' && nParts === 9){
    layout = SHEET_KEYS_SPECIES_VARIANTS; COLS = 3; layoutMode = 'speciesVariants9';
  }
  else if(nParts === 27){ layout = SHEET_KEYS_27; COLS = 6; layoutMode = 'legacy27'; }
  else {
    const err = { error: type === 'humanoid-variants'
      ? 'Found '+nParts+' separate regions. The new expression/gesture sheet must contain exactly 16 isolated pieces: closed-eye head, open-eye head, two mouth overlays, four emotion heads, and eight paired hand-gesture parts. Older 13-part sheets are also accepted. Use a transparent background and leave clear gaps between every piece.'
      : type === 'species-variants'
        ? 'Found '+nParts+' separate regions. The animal/bird face sheet must contain exactly 9 isolated pieces in a 3-column by 3-row layout: blink head, open-eye head, closed mouth, open mouth, four emotion heads, and a mouth/beak holding-grip pose.'
        : 'Found '+nParts+' separate regions. The current humanoid workflow expects exactly 27 isolated parts (four rows of six plus a final row of three), including facial expressions and hand gestures. Use a real transparent background and leave clear gaps between every part. Older 12/14-part uploads remain supported for compatibility.', detectedRegions:nParts };
    const bx0 = sorted.length ? Math.min(...sorted.map(b=>b.x0)) : 0;
    const by0 = sorted.length ? Math.min(...sorted.map(b=>b.y0)) : 0;
    const bx1 = sorted.length ? Math.max(...sorted.map(b=>b.x1)) : 0;
    const by1 = sorted.length ? Math.max(...sorted.map(b=>b.y1)) : 0;
    const looksLikeUprightHumanoid = sorted.length > 0 && (by1-by0+1) > (bx1-bx0+1)*1.18;
    if(type === 'humanoid' && nParts > 0 && nParts < 14 && looksLikeUprightHumanoid){
      // Keep the background-removed pixels. In upright cut-out images, sleeves, wrists or the hip
      // seam may touch, so connected-component counting under-counts the intended parts. Switch to
      // the whole-image rig preview rather than rejecting the upload; wide/legacy grids still use
      // the regular 12/14/27-part parser paths above.
      g.putImageData(id,0,0);
      err.fallbackAutoRig = true;
      err.cleanedCanvas = cv;
    }
    return err;
  }
  const need = layout.length;
  if(!ordered){
    const rows = Math.ceil(layout.length/COLS), byTop = top.slice().sort((a,b)=>(((a.y0+a.y1)-(b.y0+b.y1)) || a.x0-b.x0));
    ordered = []; for(let r=0;r<rows;r++) ordered.push(...byTop.slice(r*COLS,(r+1)*COLS).sort((a,b)=>a.x0-b.x0));
  }
  const arts = {};
  ordered.forEach((b,i)=>{
    const key = layout[i], pad = Math.ceil(R/s)+1;
    const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(b.x0/s)-pad), y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(b.y0/s)-pad), x1 = Math.min(W-1, Math.ceil((b.x1+1)/s)+pad), y1 = Math.min(H-1, Math.ceil((b.y1+1)/s)+pad);
    const cw = x1-x0+1, ch = y1-y0+1, out = g.createImageData(cw, ch); let mnx = cw, mny = ch, mxx = -1, mxy = -1;
    for(let y=0;y<ch;y++) for(let x=0;x<cw;x++){
      const si = ((y0+y)*W+x0+x)*4, gx=cx(x0+x), gy=cy(y0+y);
      if(d[si+3]<=30 || lab[gy*w+gx]!==b.id ||
         (b.cropClipX0!=null && (gx<b.cropClipX0 || gx>b.cropClipX1)) ||
         (b.cropClipY0!=null && gy<b.cropClipY0) || (b.cropClipY1!=null && gy>b.cropClipY1)) continue;
      // In a fused head/torso crop, shoulder-blue pixels can extend slightly above the neckline.
      // Keep skin/neck pixels with the head, but leave those blue shirt pixels to the Body part.
      if(b.cropExcludeBlueFrom!=null && gy>=b.cropExcludeBlueFrom){
        const rr=d[si], gg=d[si+1], bb=d[si+2];
        if(bb>rr*1.08 && bb>=gg*.99 && bb-gg>8) continue;
      }
      const di = (y*cw+x)*4; out.data[di]=d[si]; out.data[di+1]=d[si+1]; out.data[di+2]=d[si+2]; out.data[di+3]=d[si+3];
      mnx = Math.min(mnx,x); mxx = Math.max(mxx,x); mny = Math.min(mny,y); mxy = Math.max(mxy,y);
    }
    let c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = mxx-mnx+1; c.height = mxy-mny+1; c.getContext('2d').putImageData(out, -mnx, -mny);
    // Preserve every source pixel in recovered torso art, including the original waist/pants-start shape.
    const art=edMakeArt(c, key, modelType || 'humanoid');
    // The fused 14-part recovery intentionally assigns the complete throat + shirt + original
    // pants-start section to Body, while Head contains only hair/face. Preserve that meaning for
    // the initial assembly proportions below without changing the artwork itself.
    if(b.recoveredSplit && (modelType || 'humanoid') === 'humanoid'){
      if(key==='head') art.recoveredHeadWithoutNeck = true;
      if(key==='torso') art.bodyIncludesNeckAndWaist = true;
    }
    arts[key] = art;
  });
  return { arts, layout: layoutMode };
}
// Down-samples a part's alpha silhouette onto a small fixed grid so two parts of slightly different
// pixel sizes can still be compared shape-for-shape.
function edSiloGrid(art, gw, gh){
  const g = new Float32Array(gw*gh);
  for(let gy=0; gy<gh; gy++) for(let gx=0; gx<gw; gx++){
    const x0 = Math.floor(gx*art.w/gw), x1 = Math.max(x0+1, Math.floor((gx+1)*art.w/gw));
    const y0 = Math.floor(gy*art.h/gh), y1 = Math.max(y0+1, Math.floor((gy+1)*art.h/gh));
    let s = 0, n = 0;
    for(let y=y0; y<y1; y++) for(let x=x0; x<x1; x++){ s += art.alpha[y*art.w+x]; n++; }
    g[gy*gw+gx] = n ? s/n : 0;
  }
  return g;
}
// True when the RIGHT-side piece looks like the same drawing as the LEFT-side piece just pasted
// again (same orientation) rather than a genuine mirror image of it -- the classic "both hands drawn
// with the thumb on the same side" mistake that makes one arm look anatomically backwards. Compares
// silhouettes only (not colour/shading), both as-is and with the right side flipped; if flipping would
// clearly line the two shapes up better, the art was never mirrored to begin with.
function edLooksUnmirrored(artL, artR){
  const gw = 16, gh = 28, gL = edSiloGrid(artL, gw, gh), gR = edSiloGrid(artR, gw, gh), gRf = new Float32Array(gw*gh);
  for(let y=0; y<gh; y++) for(let x=0; x<gw; x++) gRf[y*gw+x] = gR[y*gw+(gw-1-x)];
  const diff = (a,b)=>{ let s = 0; for(let i=0;i<gw*gh;i++) s += Math.abs(a[i]-b[i]); return s; };
  const direct = diff(gL, gR), flipped = diff(gL, gRf);
  // Only act when the pair is clearly a near-duplicate in the first place, and mirroring it would
  // unambiguously line the shapes up better -- avoids "fixing" two legitimately different poses.
  return direct < gw*gh*0.06 && direct < flipped*0.7;
}
// Default rest layout: pelvis at the origin, every part attached to its parent at a natural joint,
// with the forearm's sleeve tucked under the upper arm's cuff so the two pieces read as one arm.
function edDefaultLayout(A, view, modelType){
  const side = view === 'side', species = modelType === 'animal' || modelType === 'bird';
  const T = A.torso, base = a=>({ pvx:a.dpx, pvy:a.dpy, s:1, wx:1, hy:1, fx:1, fy:1, rot:0 }), st = {};
  st.torso = { ax:0, ay:0, ...base(T) };
  if(species && A.tail){
    // The side-profile animal/bird faces right, so the tail root attaches near the torso's left/rear edge.
    // Tail art uses a right-edge pivot, allowing the whole tail to rotate from a single rump joint.
    st.tail = { ax:T.w*0.035, ay:T.h*0.52, ...base(A.tail) };
  }
  // Species sheets use a horizontal right-facing body, with the head at the front and limbs/wings at anatomy-specific anchors.
  st.head = { ax:species ? T.w*0.86 : T.dpx, ay:species ? T.h*0.9 : (T.bodyIncludesNeckAndWaist ? 0.025*T.h : 0.10*T.h), ...base(A.head) };
  // Auto-correct head size: AI-generated sheets sometimes crop the head (and its expression swaps) at
  // a different effective zoom than the torso even though the prompt asks for one consistent scale
  // (torso ~1.2x head height). Rescale the whole head group to match that expected ratio instead of
  // trusting the sheet's raw pixel size as-is -- this is the main cause of "face too big / body too
  // small" imports. Clamped so a genuinely unusual design only gets a mild nudge, not fought outright.
  const headFix = A.head.h > 0 ? (species ? Math.max(0.72, Math.min(1.35, T.h/A.head.h)) : T.bodyIncludesNeckAndWaist ? Math.max(0.85, Math.min(1.15, (T.h/1.85)/A.head.h)) : Math.max(0.6, Math.min(1.6, (T.h/1.2)/A.head.h))) : 1;
  st.head.s = headFix;
  // The supplied 13/14-part sheets use separate lower-face crops for talking. Fit those
  // crops to a mouth-sized window within the head instead of drawing each one at raw 1:1 size;
  // the same helper preserves each source crop's aspect ratio and handles different sheet sizes.
  const mouthFitScale = part => part ? Math.max(0.12, Math.min(1,
    (A.head.w*0.34)/Math.max(1,part.w),
    (A.head.h*0.18)/Math.max(1,part.h))) : 1;
  if(A.mouth_closed) st.mouth_closed = { ax:A.head.w*0.5, ay:A.head.h*0.88, ...base(A.mouth_closed), s:mouthFitScale(A.mouth_closed) };
  if(A.mouth_open) st.mouth_open = { ax:A.head.w*0.5, ay:A.head.h*0.88, ...base(A.mouth_open), s:mouthFitScale(A.mouth_open) };
  if(A.mouth_hold) st.mouth_hold = { ax:A.head.w*0.5, ay:A.head.h*0.88, ...base(A.mouth_hold), s:mouthFitScale(A.mouth_hold) };
  [['L',-1],['R',1]].forEach(([S,sg])=>{
    const U = A['arm'+S+'_upper'], F = A['arm'+S+'_lower'], Ha = A['hand'+S], Th = A['leg'+S+'_upper'], Sh = A['leg'+S+'_lower'];
    // Side view: both shoulders sit at the same spot on the body (the far arm just a touch behind the near one).
    const armX = modelType === 'animal' ? T.w*(S === 'L' ? 0.75 : 0.70) : modelType === 'bird' ? T.w*(S === 'L' ? 0.56 : 0.48) : (side ? T.w/2 + sg*0.18*T.w : T.w/2 + sg*(T.w/2 - U.w*0.42));
    const armY = modelType === 'animal' ? T.h*0.78 : modelType === 'bird' ? T.h*0.24 : 0.17*T.h;
    st['arm'+S+'_upper'] = { ax:armX, ay:armY, ...base(U) };
    const overlap = F.skinTop >= 0.12 ? F.skinTop*F.h : (F.skinTop >= 0 ? 0.03*F.h : 0.25*F.h);
    st['arm'+S+'_lower'] = { ax:U.botCx, ay:U.h - overlap, ...base(F) };
    // The hand is always bare skin (no sleeve-boundary to detect), so it just tucks a small fixed amount
    // under the forearm's wrist end -- enough to hide the seam without the hand looking swallowed.
    if(Ha) st['hand'+S] = { ax:F.botCx, ay:F.h - 0.08*F.h, ...base(Ha) };
    const legX = modelType === 'animal' ? T.w*(S === 'L' ? 0.28 : 0.23) : modelType === 'bird' ? T.w*(S === 'L' ? 0.54 : 0.46) : (side ? T.w/2 - sg*0.14*Th.w : T.w/2 + sg*Th.w*0.49);
    const legY = species ? T.h*0.82 : 0.87*T.h;
    st['leg'+S+'_upper'] = { ax:legX, ay:legY, ...base(Th) };
    const longTrouser = Th.h > T.h*1.15;
    st['leg'+S+'_lower'] = { ax:Th.botCx, ay:(longTrouser ? 0.94 : 0.86)*Th.h, ...base(Sh) };
    const shoe = A['shoe'+S];
    if(shoe){
      // A separate shoe is a child of the shin, with its top opening attached near the shin's ankle.
      st['shoe'+S] = { ax:Sh.botCx, ay:Sh.h*0.94, ...base(shoe) };
    }
  });
  // Every alternate texture (blink, expression heads, fists, pointing, thumbs-up, open palm) starts exactly where its base
  // part sits, so it swaps in place. Mouths are placed by hand (see the default guess above), so they are skipped here.
  // Expression heads inherit the same size correction as the main head so a swapped-in angry/shocked/etc.
  // face doesn't suddenly jump to a different (uncorrected) size.
  Object.keys(SHEET_ALT_OF).forEach(a=>{
    const b = SHEET_ALT_OF[a];
    if(A[a] && st[b] && !st[a] && b !== 'mouth_closed'){
      st[a] = { ax:st[b].ax, ay:st[b].ay, ...base(A[a]) };
      if(b === 'head'){
        // AI sheets often crop each expression at a different zoom. Scale each face independently
        // to the neutral head's visible height, while preserving its own neck pivot/crop coordinates.
        const variantRatio = A[a].h > 0 ? A.head.h/A[a].h : 1;
        st[a].s = headFix * Math.max(.65,Math.min(1.7,variantRatio));
      }
    }
  });
  // Hand scale correction: the reference sheet's hands/gestures are oversized against the head and torso.
  // Apply one consistent reduction to the base hands and every alternate gesture, preserving each wrist pivot.
  ['handL','handR','handL_fist','handR_fist','handL_point','handR_point','handL_thumb','handR_thumb','handL_palm','handR_palm'].forEach(k=>{
    if(st[k]) st[k].s *= 0.92;
  });
  // Pointing pieces in generated sheets are often drawn horizontally even though their wrist is a
  // side edge. Rotate them into the same local limb frame as the plain hand so the wrist joins cleanly.
  if(!species){
    if(st.handL_point) st.handL_point.rot += 90;
    if(st.handR_point) st.handR_point.rot -= 90;
  }
  // If the source art drew the same pose twice for a left/right pair instead of mirroring it (very
  // common when pieces come from an AI image generator), auto-flip the right-side piece so a fresh
  // upload already reads as a correct pair of hands/feet without the person having to notice and fix
  // it by hand.
  // Front view only: in a side view the two limbs of a pair are not mirror images, so never flip them.
  if(!side) SHEET_FLIP_PAIRS.forEach(([lk,rk])=>{
    if(A[lk] && A[rk] && st[lk] && st[rk] && edLooksUnmirrored(A[lk], A[rk])) st[rk].fx = -1;
  });
  return st;
}

/* ---------- 2) editor state + geometry ---------- */
/* EDIT STANCE: every model opens in the editor with the same T-pose shown in the joint-overlay reference
   (arms straight out to the sides, legs a little apart). The stance is an offset over each limb's own rotation
   (st.rot stays 0 = "no change"). When Done is pressed, edCompile() keeps this stance as the saved default rig pose
   so the model lines up with the visible joints in the main video editor as well. Values are degrees; L parts are on the viewer's left. */
const ED_STANCE = { armL_upper:90, armR_upper:-90, legL_upper:8, legR_upper:-8 };
// The T-pose display stance is for front-facing sheets only. In side view every limb is shown hanging straight down.
function edStanceOf(k){ return ED.view === 'side' ? 0 : (ED_STANCE[k]||0); }
const ED = { view:'front', facing:1, modelType:'humanoid', open:false, arts:{}, assign:{}, st:{}, order:[], sel:'torso', cam:{z:1,x:0,y:0}, undo:[], redo:[], bones:true, lock:false, mode:'size', kind:'new', editIdx:null, name:'Model', init:null, cv:null, g:null, dpr:1, ready:false, extraVariants:{headParts:[],expressions:[],gestures:[]}, pendingVariantType:null };
const edArt = k=>ED.arts[ED.assign[k]];
// World (figure-space) placement of every part: { x, y (pivot), rot (rad, incl. parents), sx, sy (signed scale) }.
function edWorld(rest){
  const W = {};
  const go = k=>{
    if(W[k] !== undefined) return W[k];
    const st = ED.st[k];
    if(!st) return W[k] = null;   // this model doesn't have this (optional) part -- skip it, don't crash
    const p = SHEET_PARENT[k], sx = st.s*st.wx*st.fx, sy = st.s*st.hy*st.fy;
    let x, y, rot = (st.rot + (rest ? 0 : edStanceOf(k)))*Math.PI/180;
    if(!p){ x = st.ax; y = st.ay; }
    else {
      const P = go(p); if(!P) return W[k] = null;
      const ps = ED.st[p], c = Math.cos(P.rot), s = Math.sin(P.rot), vx = (st.ax-ps.pvx)*P.sx, vy = (st.ay-ps.pvy)*P.sy;
      x = P.x + vx*c - vy*s; y = P.y + vx*s + vy*c; rot += P.rot;
    }
    return W[k] = { x, y, rot, sx, sy };
  };
  SHEET_KEYS.forEach(go);
  return W;
}
function edL2W(W, k, lx, ly){ const w = W[k], st = ED.st[k], vx = (lx-st.pvx)*w.sx, vy = (ly-st.pvy)*w.sy, c = Math.cos(w.rot), s = Math.sin(w.rot); return { x:w.x+vx*c-vy*s, y:w.y+vx*s+vy*c }; }
function edW2L(W, k, px, py){ const w = W[k], st = ED.st[k], dx = px-w.x, dy = py-w.y, c = Math.cos(w.rot), s = Math.sin(w.rot); return { x:st.pvx + (dx*c+dy*s)/w.sx, y:st.pvy + (-dx*s+dy*c)/w.sy }; }
// Moves part k so its pivot lands on world point (x,y) by rewriting its attach point (children follow automatically).
function edSetPos(k, x, y){
  const st = ED.st[k], p = SHEET_PARENT[k];
  if(!p){ st.ax = x; st.ay = y; return; }
  const W = edWorld(), P = W[p], ps = ED.st[p], c = Math.cos(P.rot), s = Math.sin(P.rot), dx = x-P.x, dy = y-P.y;
  st.ax = ps.pvx + (dx*c+dy*s)/P.sx; st.ay = ps.pvy + (-dx*s+dy*c)/P.sy;
}
// LOCK MODE helper: the bone that ends at joint `k` belongs to k's parent part. Rotates + stretches that parent part so the
// joint lands on world point (tx,ty); k and everything below it are carried along by the hierarchy.
function edBoneDrive(k, tx, ty){
  const p = SHEET_PARENT[k], ps = ED.st[p], cs = ED.st[k], stance = edStanceOf(p), P = edWorld()[p];
  const dx = cs.ax-ps.pvx, dy = cs.ay-ps.pvy, Tx = tx-P.x, Ty = ty-P.y, sxv = dx*P.sx;
  let sy = P.sy;
  if(Math.abs(dy) > 1){
    const d2 = Tx*Tx+Ty*Ty-sxv*sxv;
    if(d2 > 0){ ps.hy = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(4, Math.sqrt(d2)/(Math.abs(dy)*ps.s))); sy = ps.s*ps.hy*ps.fy; }
  }
  const world = Math.atan2(Ty,Tx) - Math.atan2(dy*sy, sxv), inherited = P.rot - (ps.rot+stance)*Math.PI/180;
  let r = (world-inherited)*180/Math.PI - stance; ps.rot = ((r+180)%360+360)%360-180;
}
function edBoneSegs(W){
  const pv = k=>W[k] ? ({x:W[k].x,y:W[k].y}) : null;
  const far = (k, lx, ly)=>W[k] && ED.st[k] ? edL2W(W,k,lx,ly) : null;
  const segs = [], st = ED.st;
  const add=(a,b)=>{ if(a && b && Number.isFinite(a.x) && Number.isFinite(a.y) && Number.isFinite(b.x) && Number.isFinite(b.y)) segs.push([a,b]); };
  if(W.torso && W.head){
    const pelvis=pv('torso'), neck=pv('head');
    const chest={x:pelvis.x+(neck.x-pelvis.x)*0.86,y:pelvis.y+(neck.y-pelvis.y)*0.86};
    add(pelvis,neck);
    add(neck,far('head',st.head.pvx,0));
    if(W.tail && ED.st.tail) add(pv('tail'),far('tail',0,edArt('tail').h*0.5));
    ['L','R'].forEach(S=>{
      const up='arm'+S+'_upper', lo='arm'+S+'_lower', hand='hand'+S;
      const th='leg'+S+'_upper', sh='leg'+S+'_lower', shoe='shoe'+S;
      add(chest,pv(up));
      add(pv(up),pv(lo));
      if(W[hand]){
        add(pv(lo),pv(hand));
        add(pv(hand),far(hand,st[hand].pvx,edArt(hand).h));
      }else add(pv(lo),far(lo,st[lo].pvx,edArt(lo).h));
      add(pv(th),pv(sh));
      if(W[shoe]){
        add(pv(sh),pv(shoe));
        add(pv(shoe),far(shoe,st[shoe].pvx,edArt(shoe).h));
      }else add(pv(sh),far(sh,st[sh].pvx,edArt(sh).h));
    });
  }
  return segs;
}
// Selection handles of part k, in SCREEN pixels: rot = bottom-right corner (scale + rotate), len = stretch length, wid = stretch width.
function edHandles(k, W){
  const a = edArt(k), st = ED.st[k], Z = ED.cam.z, S = p=>({ x:ED.cam.x+p.x*Z, y:ED.cam.y+p.y*Z }), L = (x,y)=>S(edL2W(W,k,x,y));
  // handles sit on the side / end FARTHEST from the joint (stable ratios) and are pushed a little outside the box (never stacked on each other)
  const farY = st.pvy < a.h/2 ? a.h : 0, sideX = st.pvx <= a.w/2 ? a.w : 0, ctr = L(a.w/2, a.h/2);
  const out = (p, d)=>{ const dx = p.x-ctr.x, dy = p.y-ctr.y, l = Math.hypot(dx,dy) || 1; return { x:p.x+dx/l*d, y:p.y+dy/l*d }; };
  return { corners:[L(0,0),L(a.w,0),L(a.w,a.h),L(0,a.h)], rot:out(L(sideX,farY),15), len:out(L(st.pvx,farY),19), wid:out(L(sideX,a.h/2),19) };
}
// True when key k should actually be shown/tappable right now. An alt expression texture (head_blink,
// mouth_open, handL_fist, handR_fist) is invisible unless it's the one currently selected for editing;
// its base hides right back while that's happening, so the two never draw stacked on each other.
function edKeyVisible(k){
  if(SHEET_ALT_OF[k]) return k === ED.sel;
  const alts = SHEET_ALTS[k]; if(alts && alts.includes(ED.sel)) return false;
  return true;
}
// Legacy editor oval-joint blend helpers removed; do not repaint over imported part artwork.
function edPick(p, W){
  if(ED.lock && ED.bones){
    // Mouth crops are nested face artwork, not bones: keep their pixels draggable even while
    // every structural joint is locked to its connected artwork. Their pivot dots are hidden
    // in edDraw(), so exclude them from bone-point hit-testing as well.
    const jointKeys = ED.order.filter(k=>W[k] && edKeyVisible(k) && k!=='mouth_closed' && k!=='mouth_open' && k!=='mouth_hold').sort((a,b)=>(a===ED.sel?-1:0)-(b===ED.sel?-1:0));
    for(const k of jointKeys){
      const q={x:ED.cam.x+W[k].x*ED.cam.z,y:ED.cam.y+W[k].y*ED.cam.z};
      if(Math.hypot(p.x-q.x,p.y-q.y)<20) return {type:'pivot',key:k};
    }
    const fx=(p.x-ED.cam.x)/ED.cam.z, fy=(p.y-ED.cam.y)/ED.cam.z;
    for(let i=ED.order.length-1;i>=0;i--){
      const k=ED.order[i];
      if((k!=='mouth_closed' && k!=='mouth_open' && k!=='mouth_hold') || !W[k] || !edKeyVisible(k)) continue;
      const a=edArt(k), l=edW2L(W,k,fx,fy), lx=Math.round(l.x), ly=Math.round(l.y);
      for(const [ox,oy] of [[0,0],[2,0],[-2,0],[0,2],[0,-2]]){
        const X=lx+ox,Y=ly+oy;
        if(X>=0 && Y>=0 && X<a.w && Y<a.h && a.alpha[Y*a.w+X]) return {type:'move',key:k};
      }
    }
    return {type:'locked',key:ED.sel};
  }
  if(ED.sel){ const h = edHandles(ED.sel, W); for(const n of ['rot','len','wid']) if(Math.hypot(p.x-h[n].x, p.y-h[n].y) < 20) return { type:n, key:ED.sel }; }
  if(ED.bones){
    const keys = ED.order.filter(k=>W[k] && edKeyVisible(k)).sort((a,b)=>(a===ED.sel?-1:0)-(b===ED.sel?-1:0));
    for(const k of keys){ const q = { x:ED.cam.x+W[k].x*ED.cam.z, y:ED.cam.y+W[k].y*ED.cam.z }; if(Math.hypot(p.x-q.x, p.y-q.y) < 17) return { type:'pivot', key:k }; }
  }
  const fx = (p.x-ED.cam.x)/ED.cam.z, fy = (p.y-ED.cam.y)/ED.cam.z;
  for(let i = ED.order.length-1; i >= 0; i--){
    const k = ED.order[i]; if(!W[k] || !edKeyVisible(k)) continue;
    const a = edArt(k), l = edW2L(W, k, fx, fy), lx = Math.round(l.x), ly = Math.round(l.y);
    for(const [ox,oy] of [[0,0],[2,0],[-2,0],[0,2],[0,-2]]){
      const X = lx+ox, Y = ly+oy; if(X >= 0 && Y >= 0 && X < a.w && Y < a.h && a.alpha[Y*a.w+X]) return { type:'move', key:k };
    }
  }
  return { type:'pan' };
}

/* ---------- editor drawing ---------- */
function edDraw(){
  if(!ED.open || !ED.g) return;
  const g = ED.g, cv = ED.cv, dpr = ED.dpr, Z = ED.cam.z, W = edWorld();
  g.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0); g.clearRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
  g.setTransform(dpr*Z,0,0,dpr*Z,dpr*ED.cam.x,dpr*ED.cam.y); g.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high';
  ED.order.forEach(k=>{
    if(!W[k] || !edKeyVisible(k)) return;
    const w = W[k], st = ED.st[k]; g.save(); g.translate(w.x,w.y); g.rotate(w.rot); g.scale(w.sx,w.sy); g.drawImage(edArt(k).canvas, -st.pvx, -st.pvy);
    const preview=ED.previewVariant;
    if(preview&&preview.targetKey===k&&preview.src){
      const cache=window.__ethvelleModelLabVariantPreviewCache||(window.__ethvelleModelLabVariantPreviewCache=new Map());
      let previewImage=cache.get(preview.src);
      if(!previewImage){previewImage=new Image();previewImage.onload=()=>{if(ED.previewVariant&&ED.previewVariant.src===preview.src)edDraw();};previewImage.onerror=()=>{};cache.set(preview.src,previewImage);previewImage.src=preview.src;}
      if(previewImage.complete&&previewImage.naturalWidth){const art=edArt(k);g.drawImage(previewImage,-st.pvx,-st.pvy,art.w,art.h);}
    }
    g.restore();
  });
  g.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
  const S = p=>({ x:ED.cam.x+p.x*Z, y:ED.cam.y+p.y*Z });
  // Joint-bridge painting intentionally removed: it generated oval/crescent artifacts over source art.
  if(ED.bones){
    const segs = edBoneSegs(W); g.lineCap = 'round';
    [['rgba(255,255,255,.95)',ED.lock?7:6],[ED.lock?'#12B886':'#2F5CE8',ED.lock?4:3]].forEach(([c,lw])=>{ g.strokeStyle = c; g.lineWidth = lw; g.beginPath(); segs.forEach(([a,b])=>{ const A = S(a), B = S(b); g.moveTo(A.x,A.y); g.lineTo(B.x,B.y); }); g.stroke(); });
  }
  if(ED.sel && !ED.lock){
    const h = edHandles(ED.sel, W), c = h.corners;
    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(c[0].x,c[0].y); for(let i=1;i<4;i++) g.lineTo(c[i].x,c[i].y); g.closePath();
    g.lineJoin = 'round'; g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.5)'; g.lineWidth = 4; g.stroke(); g.strokeStyle = '#fff'; g.lineWidth = 2; g.stroke();
    const rot = W[ED.sel].rot;
    const knob = (p, ang, glyph)=>{
      g.save(); g.translate(p.x,p.y); g.rotate(ang);
      g.beginPath(); g.arc(0,0,11,0,Math.PI*2); g.fillStyle = '#fff'; g.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,.45)'; g.shadowBlur = 6; g.fill(); g.shadowBlur = 0;
      g.strokeStyle = '#2F5CE8'; g.fillStyle = '#2F5CE8'; g.lineWidth = 2; g.lineCap = 'round'; glyph(); g.restore();
    };
    const arrows = ()=>{ g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0,-5); g.lineTo(0,5); g.stroke(); [[-1,-5],[1,5]].forEach(([d,y])=>{ g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(-3,y-d*3); g.lineTo(0,y); g.lineTo(3,y-d*3); g.stroke(); }); };
    knob(h.wid, rot + Math.PI/2, arrows);
    knob(h.len, rot, arrows);
    knob(h.rot, 0, ()=>{ g.beginPath(); g.arc(0,0,5,Math.PI*0.15,Math.PI*1.55); g.stroke(); g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(-1,-6.5); g.lineTo(2.5,-4.2); g.lineTo(-1.5,-2.4); g.closePath(); g.fill(); });
  }
  if(ED.bones) ED.order.forEach(k=>{
    if(!W[k] || !edKeyVisible(k)) return;
    // Mouth crops are positioned inside the head artwork, not skeleton joints. Their pivot
    // dots looked like a stray blue target beside the face and were never bone controls.
    if(k==='mouth_closed' || k==='mouth_open' || k==='mouth_hold') return;
    const p = S(W[k]), on = k === ED.sel;
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(p.x,p.y,on?9:7,0,Math.PI*2); g.fillStyle = '#fff'; g.fill();
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(p.x,p.y,on?6.5:4.5,0,Math.PI*2); g.fillStyle = ED.lock ? (on ? '#12B886' : '#3fd6a6') : (on ? '#2F5CE8' : '#5b7cf0'); g.fill();
  });
}
function edResize(){
  const st = document.getElementById('edStage'), r = st.getBoundingClientRect(); if(r.width < 10 || r.height < 10) return;
  ED.dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1; ED.cv.width = Math.round(r.width*ED.dpr); ED.cv.height = Math.round(r.height*ED.dpr); ED.cw = r.width; ED.ch = r.height;
  if(ED.needFit){ ED.needFit = false; edFit(); return; }
  edDraw();
}
function edFit(){
  const W = edWorld(); let x0 = 1e9, y0 = 1e9, x1 = -1e9, y1 = -1e9;
  ED.order.forEach(k=>{ const a = edArt(k); if(!a) return; [[0,0],[a.w,0],[a.w,a.h],[0,a.h]].forEach(([lx,ly])=>{ const p = edL2W(W,k,lx,ly); x0 = Math.min(x0,p.x); x1 = Math.max(x1,p.x); y0 = Math.min(y0,p.y); y1 = Math.max(y1,p.y); }); });
  const bw = Math.max(1,x1-x0), bh = Math.max(1,y1-y0), z = Math.min((ED.cw-36)/bw, (ED.ch-36)/bh);
  ED.cam.z = Math.max(0.05, z); ED.cam.x = ED.cw/2 - (x0+x1)/2*ED.cam.z; ED.cam.y = ED.ch/2 - (y0+y1)/2*ED.cam.z;
  edSyncZoom(); edDraw();
}
function edSyncZoom(){ const e = document.getElementById('edZoomVal'); if(e) e.textContent = Math.round(ED.cam.z*100)+'%'; }
function edZoomAt(f, cx, cy){ const z = Math.max(0.08, Math.min(8, ED.cam.z*f)), k = z/ED.cam.z; ED.cam.x = cx-(cx-ED.cam.x)*k; ED.cam.y = cy-(cy-ED.cam.y)*k; ED.cam.z = z; edSyncZoom(); edDraw(); }
function edZoomBy(f){ edZoomAt(f, ED.cw/2, ED.ch/2); }

/* ---------- undo / redo ---------- */
const edSnap = ()=>JSON.stringify({ st:ED.st, order:ED.order, assign:ED.assign, facing:ED.facing });
function edRestore(j){ const o = JSON.parse(j); ED.st = o.st; ED.order = o.order; ED.assign = o.assign; ED.facing = o.facing === -1 ? -1 : 1; }
function edPush(){ ED.undo.push(edSnap()); if(ED.undo.length > 80) ED.undo.shift(); ED.redo.length = 0; edSyncHistory(); }
function edUndo(){ if(!ED.undo.length) return; ED.redo.push(edSnap()); edRestore(ED.undo.pop()); edAfterChange(true); }
function edRedo(){ if(!ED.redo.length) return; ED.undo.push(edSnap()); edRestore(ED.redo.pop()); edAfterChange(true); }
function edSyncHistory(){ document.getElementById('edUndoBtn').disabled = !ED.undo.length; document.getElementById('edRedoBtn').disabled = !ED.redo.length; }
function edAfterChange(full){ edSyncHistory(); if(full) edSyncUI(); else edSyncVals(); edDraw(); }

/* ---------- selection + toolbar ---------- */
function edThumb(a){
  if(!a.thumb){ const k = 60/Math.max(a.w,a.h), c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = Math.max(1,Math.round(a.w*k)); c.height = Math.max(1,Math.round(a.h*k)); c.getContext('2d').drawImage(a.canvas,0,0,c.width,c.height); a.thumb = c.toDataURL('image/png'); }
  return a.thumb;
}
function edSelect(k){ ED.sel = k; edSyncUI(); edDraw(); }
// Facial reactions, mouths and each side's hand gestures are interchangeable variants. Paired
// arms/legs share size and rotation edits without destroying their different anatomical positions.
function edSimilarKeys(k){
  if(/^head(?:_blink|_sad|_angry|_shock|_laugh)?$/.test(k)) return SHEET_KEYS.filter(x=>/^head(?:_blink|_sad|_angry|_shock|_laugh)?$/.test(x));
  if(/^mouth_(?:closed|open|hold)$/.test(k)) return ['mouth_closed','mouth_open','mouth_hold'];
  const hm=k.match(/^hand([LR])(?:_(?:fist|point|thumb|palm))?$/);
  // Hand transforms are a paired character-level edit, not side-specific: include both base hands
  // and every matching gesture on both sides. Per-part defaults still preserve each gesture's own
  // wrist pivot and the left/right model's original mirror direction.
  if(hm) return SHEET_KEYS.filter(x=>/^hand[LR](?:_(?:fist|point|thumb|palm))?$/.test(x));
  const lm=k.match(/^(arm|leg)([LR])_(upper|lower)$/);
  if(lm){ const other=lm[2]==='L'?'R':'L'; return [k,lm[1]+other+'_'+lm[3]]; }
  return [k];
}
function edApplyToSimilarParts(){
  const k=ED.sel;
  if(!k || !ED.st[k]){ showToast('Select a part first',{duration:1300}); return; }
  let init;
  try{ init = typeof ED.init === 'string' ? JSON.parse(ED.init).st : ED.init.st; }
  catch(e){ showToast('Could not read the starting layout',{duration:1500}); return; }
  const headGroup=/^head(?:_blink|_sad|_angry|_shock|_laugh)?$/.test(k);
  const keys=edSimilarKeys(k);
  const group=keys.filter(x=>x!==k && ED.st[x] && init[x] && ED.arts[ED.assign[x]]);
  if(!group.length){ showToast('No other matching parts are present in this sheet',{duration:1500}); return; }
  const src=ED.st[k], src0=init[k], srcArt=edArt(k);
  if(!src0 || !srcArt){ showToast('Could not read the selected part',{duration:1500}); return; }
  const ratio=(v,b)=>Math.abs(b)>1e-6?v/b:1;
  const variants=headGroup || /^mouth_(?:closed|open)$/.test(k) || /^hand[LR](?:_(?:fist|point|thumb|palm))?$/.test(k);
  let applied=0;
  edPush();
  group.forEach(q=>{
    const dst=ED.st[q], d0=init[q], art=edArt(q);
    if(!dst || !d0 || !art) return;
    // Transfer edit DELTAS from the selected piece, relative to its opening state. This preserves
    // the destination's own detected proportions while copying every intentional transform edit.
    dst.s=Math.max(.3,Math.min(3,d0.s*ratio(src.s,src0.s)));
    dst.wx=Math.max(.2,Math.min(4,d0.wx*ratio(src.wx,src0.wx)));
    dst.hy=Math.max(.2,Math.min(4,d0.hy*ratio(src.hy,src0.hy)));
    dst.rot=d0.rot+(src.rot-src0.rot);
    dst.fx=src.fx===src0.fx ? d0.fx : -d0.fx;
    dst.fy=src.fy===src0.fy ? d0.fy : -d0.fy;
    if(variants){
      const srcParent=SHEET_PARENT[k], dstParent=SHEET_PARENT[q];
      const pa=srcParent&&edArt(srcParent), pb=dstParent&&edArt(dstParent);
      const sx=pa&&pb ? pb.w/Math.max(1,pa.w) : 1;
      const sy=pa&&pb ? pb.h/Math.max(1,pa.h) : 1;
      const sp0=srcParent&&init[srcParent], dp0=dstParent&&init[dstParent];
      const sign=v=>v ? Math.sign(v.s*v.wx*v.fx)||1 : 1;
      const flipLocalX=ED.view!=='side' && sign(sp0)!==sign(dp0) ? -1 : 1;
      dst.ax=d0.ax+(src.ax-src0.ax)*sx*flipLocalX;
      dst.ay=d0.ay+(src.ay-src0.ay)*sy;
    } else {
      const mirror=ED.view!=='side';
      dst.ax=d0.ax+(mirror?-(src.ax-src0.ax):(src.ax-src0.ax));
      dst.ay=d0.ay+(src.ay-src0.ay);
    }
    // Keep each part's own crop pivot but copy the selected pivot adjustment as a relative offset.
    dst.pvx=art.dpx+(src.pvx-srcArt.dpx)*(art.w/Math.max(1,srcArt.w));
    dst.pvy=art.dpy+(src.pvy-srcArt.dpy)*(art.h/Math.max(1,srcArt.h));
    applied++;
  });
  if(!applied){ ED.undo.pop(); showToast('No matching parts could be updated',{duration:1500}); return; }
  edAfterChange(true);
  showToast(headGroup ? 'Applied head edits to '+applied+' head variants' : 'Applied edits to '+applied+' matching parts',{duration:1800});
}
function edSyncUI(){
  const box = document.getElementById('edLayers');
  box.innerHTML = ED.order.slice().reverse().map(k=>`<button class="ed-chip ${k===ED.sel?'is-on':''}" data-k="${k}" onclick="edSelect('${k}')"><span class="th"><img src="${edThumb(edArt(k))}" alt=""></span><span class="n">${sheetLabel(k)}</span></button>`).join('');
  const on = box.querySelector('.is-on'); if(on) on.scrollIntoView({ inline:'nearest', block:'nearest' });
  document.getElementById('edSubtitle').textContent = ED.sel ? sheetLabel(ED.sel) + ' selected' : 'Tap a part to edit it';
  document.getElementById('edSwapBtn').disabled = !ED.sel || !/[LR]_/.test(ED.sel);
  const fmb = document.getElementById('edFlipModelBtn'); if(fmb) fmb.style.display = ED.view === 'side' ? '' : 'none';
  document.querySelectorAll('#edModes button').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('is-on', b.dataset.m === ED.mode));
  const bb = document.getElementById('edBonesBtn'); bb.classList.toggle('is-on', ED.bones); bb.classList.toggle('is-off', !ED.bones); bb.innerHTML = edIcon(ED.bones ? 'eye' : 'eyeOff');
  const bg = document.getElementById('edLockBadge'); if(bg){ bg.classList.remove('show'); bg.textContent = ''; }
  const lb = document.getElementById('edLockBtn'); lb.classList.toggle('is-lock', ED.lock); lb.innerHTML = edIcon(ED.lock ? 'lock' : 'unlock'); lb.setAttribute('aria-pressed', ED.lock ? 'true' : 'false');
  edSyncHistory(); edSyncVals();
}
const ED_RANGE = { size:[30,300,'%'], width:[30,300,'%'], height:[30,300,'%'], rotate:[-180,180,'°'], position:[-150,150,'%'] };
// The part's auto-placed position when the sheet was first opened -- the 0% reference point for the
// Move sliders, and what "Reset part" already returns ax/ay to, so the two stay conceptually in sync.
function edInitSt(k){ try{ return JSON.parse(ED.init).st[k]; }catch(e){ return null; } }
// Position is expressed as a percentage of the part's OWN width/height (not raw pixels) so the same
// slider range feels right whether you're nudging a tiny mouth or the whole torso.
function edPosVal(axis){
  const st = ED.st[ED.sel], init = edInitSt(ED.sel), a = edArt(ED.sel); if(!st || !init || !a) return 0;
  const span = axis==='x' ? a.w : a.h; if(!span) return 0;
  const cur = axis==='x' ? st.ax : st.ay, base = axis==='x' ? init.ax : init.ay;
  return Math.round((cur-base)/span*100);
}
function edApplyPos(axis, v){
  const st = ED.st[ED.sel], init = edInitSt(ED.sel), a = edArt(ED.sel); if(!st || !init || !a) return;
  const span = axis==='x' ? a.w : a.h;
  if(axis==='x'){ st.ax = init.ax + v/100*span; document.getElementById('edVal').textContent = 'X ' + v + '%'; }
  else { st.ay = init.ay + v/100*span; document.getElementById('edValY').textContent = 'Y ' + v + '%'; }
  edDraw();
}
function edGetVal(){
  const st = ED.st[ED.sel]; if(!st) return 0;
  if(ED.mode === 'size') return Math.round(st.s*100);
  if(ED.mode === 'width') return Math.round(st.wx*100);
  if(ED.mode === 'height') return Math.round(st.hy*100);
  if(ED.mode === 'rotate') return Math.round(st.rot);
  return edPosVal('x');   // position mode: the main (first) slider is X
}
function edSyncVals(){
  const rowY = document.getElementById('edSliderRowY');
  if(ED.mode === 'position'){
    rowY.style.display = '';
    const r = ED_RANGE.position;
    const slX = document.getElementById('edSlider'), vX = edPosVal('x');
    slX.min = r[0]; slX.max = r[1]; slX.value = vX; document.getElementById('edVal').textContent = 'X ' + vX + '%';
    const slY = document.getElementById('edSliderY'), vY = edPosVal('y');
    slY.min = r[0]; slY.max = r[1]; slY.value = vY; document.getElementById('edValY').textContent = 'Y ' + vY + '%';
  } else {
    rowY.style.display = 'none';
    const r = ED_RANGE[ED.mode], sl = document.getElementById('edSlider'), v = edGetVal();
    sl.min = r[0]; sl.max = r[1]; sl.value = v; document.getElementById('edVal').textContent = v + r[2];
  }
}
function edSetMode(m){ ED.mode = m; edSyncUI(); }
let edSliderPushed = false;
function edSliderStart(){ if(!edSliderPushed){ edPush(); edSliderPushed = true; } }
function edSliderEnd(){ edSliderPushed = false; }
function edSliderInput(v){
  const st = ED.st[ED.sel]; if(!st) return; v = +v;
  if(ED.mode === 'position'){ edApplyPos('x', v); return; }
  if(ED.mode==='size') st.s = v/100; else if(ED.mode==='width') st.wx = v/100; else if(ED.mode==='height') st.hy = v/100; else st.rot = v;
  document.getElementById('edVal').textContent = v + ED_RANGE[ED.mode][2]; edDraw();
}
function edSliderInputY(v){ if(ED.mode !== 'position' || !ED.sel) return; edApplyPos('y', +v); }
function edNudge(d){ if(!ED.sel) return; edPush(); const r = ED_RANGE[ED.mode]; edSliderInput(Math.max(r[0], Math.min(r[1], edGetVal()+d))); edSyncVals(); }
function edNudgeY(d){ if(!ED.sel || ED.mode !== 'position') return; edPush(); const r = ED_RANGE.position; edSliderInputY(Math.max(r[0], Math.min(r[1], edPosVal('y')+d))); edSyncVals(); }
function edFlip(axis){ if(!ED.sel) return; edPush(); const st = ED.st[ED.sel]; if(axis==='x') st.fx *= -1; else st.fy *= -1; edDraw(); }
// NEAR/FAR limb pairs in a side-view sheet: two pieces with the same silhouette (the far one a touch darker,
// see SHEET_PROMPT_SIDE's "NO MIRRORING IN SIDE VIEW" rule). When the model turns around, the near and far
// limbs trade places, so the far leg becomes the near one and vice versa.
const SHEET_FLIP_PAIRS = [['armL_upper','armR_upper'], ['armL_lower','armR_lower'], ['handL','handR'], ['legL_upper','legR_upper'], ['legL_lower','legR_lower'], ['shoeL','shoeR'],
  ['handL_fist','handR_fist'], ['handL_point','handR_point'], ['handL_thumb','handR_thumb'], ['handL_palm','handR_palm']];
// Turns the WHOLE side-view model around so it faces the other way. Every piece turns, limbs and hands included:
// its art is reflected (fx), its joint is mirrored across the body's centre line and its angle is negated, so a foot
// or hand that pointed forward now points the new way. The near and far limbs first trade places together with their
// own state (art, size, stretch, rotation and joint edits), so nothing the person tuned is lost. Parents go first so
// children are placed against their new parent transforms. Side view only.
// ED.facing flips as well: the animation presets read it, so arms and legs swing toward the way the model now faces.
function edFlipModel(){
  if(ED.view !== 'side') return;
  edPush();
  SHEET_FLIP_PAIRS.forEach(([l,r])=>{
    if(!ED.arts[ED.assign[l]] || !ED.arts[ED.assign[r]]) return;   // basic sheets may not have the fist pair
    const t = ED.assign[l]; ED.assign[l] = ED.assign[r]; ED.assign[r] = t;
    const s = ED.st[l]; ED.st[l] = ED.st[r]; ED.st[r] = s;
  });
  const W0 = edWorld(true), cx = W0.torso.x, done = {};
  const go = k => {
    if(done[k] || !ED.st[k] || !W0[k]) return; done[k] = true;
    const p = SHEET_PARENT[k]; if(p) go(p);
    const st = ED.st[k], old = W0[k];
    st.fx *= -1;                                   // reflect this piece's art (limbs and hands included)
    if(p){
      const P = edWorld(true)[p];
      st.rot = (-old.rot - P.rot)*180/Math.PI;
      edSetPos(k, 2*cx - old.x, old.y);
    } else st.rot = -old.rot*180/Math.PI;
  };
  SHEET_KEYS.forEach(go);
  ED.facing = -(ED.facing || 1);
  edSyncVals(); edDraw();
  const flipBtn=document.getElementById('edFlipModelBtn');
  if(flipBtn){ flipBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', ED.facing < 0 ? 'true' : 'false'); flipBtn.title='Turn the entire side-profile rig 180 degrees. Near/far limbs swap, and every piece stays editable'; }
  showToast(ED.facing < 0 ? 'Model turned to face left' : 'Model turned to face right', { duration:1500 });
}
function edSwap(){
  const k = ED.sel; if(!k || !/[LR]_/.test(k)) return;
  const o = k.replace(/([LR])_/, (m,s)=>(s==='L'?'R':'L')+'_');
  edPush(); const t = ED.assign[k]; ED.assign[k] = ED.assign[o]; ED.assign[o] = t;
  [k,o].forEach(x=>{ const a = edArt(x); ED.st[x].pvx = a.dpx; ED.st[x].pvy = a.dpy; });
  edSyncUI(); edDraw(); showToast('Swapped '+sheetLabel(k).toLowerCase()+' and '+sheetLabel(o).toLowerCase(), { duration:1500 });
}
function edResetPart(){
  if(!ED.sel) return; const init = JSON.parse(ED.init), k = ED.sel; edPush();
  const a = edArt(k); ED.st[k] = { ...init.st[k], pvx:a.dpx, pvy:a.dpy }; edAfterChange(true);
}
function edResetAll(){ edPush(); edRestore(ED.init); edAfterChange(true); edFit(); showToast('Reset to the starting layout', { duration:1300 }); }
// Only joint attachment coordinates and bone rotations are restored. Artwork scale, width, length,
// flips and the rest of the part edits remain untouched. The "default" is the pose/layout this
// specific model had when this editing session opened.
function edSkeletonAtDefault(){
  if(!ED.init || !ED.st) return false;
  let base; try{ base=JSON.parse(ED.init).st||{}; }catch(e){ return false; }
  for(const k of Object.keys(ED.st)){
    // Mouth crops are independent face overlays nested inside the head, not skeleton joints.
    // Their ax/ay/rot are part of the model's saved facial layout and must not block the
    // skeleton-default check after a user deliberately repositions them.
    if(k==='mouth_closed' || k==='mouth_open' || k==='mouth_hold') continue;
    const cur=ED.st[k], ref=base[k]; if(!cur || !ref) continue;
    for(const prop of ['ax','ay','rot']){
      if(Number.isFinite(ref[prop]) && (!Number.isFinite(cur[prop]) || Math.abs(cur[prop]-ref[prop])>1e-4)) return false;
    }
  }
  return true;
}
function edResetSkeletonToDefault(silent){
  if(!ED.open || !ED.init || !ED.st) return false;
  let base; try{ base=JSON.parse(ED.init).st||{}; }catch(e){ return false; }
  const keys=Object.keys(ED.st).filter(k=>{
    // Reset only structural joint attachments. Keep mouth overlay transforms as edited by
    // the user; they are nested in the head and are not part of the skeleton pose.
    if(k==='mouth_closed' || k==='mouth_open' || k==='mouth_hold') return false;
    const cur=ED.st[k], ref=base[k];
    return !!(cur && ref && ['ax','ay','rot'].some(prop=>Number.isFinite(ref[prop]) && (!Number.isFinite(cur[prop]) || Math.abs(cur[prop]-ref[prop])>1e-4)));
  });
  if(keys.length){
    edPush();
    keys.forEach(k=>['ax','ay','rot'].forEach(prop=>{
      const cur=ED.st[k], ref=base[k];
      if(cur && ref && Number.isFinite(ref[prop])) cur[prop]=ref[prop];
    }));
    if(!ED.st[ED.sel]) ED.sel=ED.st.torso?'torso':Object.keys(ED.st)[0]||null;
    edAfterChange(true);
  }
  const ok=edSkeletonAtDefault();
  if(!silent) showToast(ok ? (keys.length?'Skeleton reset to its default position':'Skeleton is already in its default position') : 'Could not reset this skeleton', {duration:1600});
  return ok;
}
function edToggleBones(){ ED.bones = !ED.bones; edSyncUI(); edDraw(); showToast(ED.bones ? 'Bones shown' : 'Bones hidden' + (ED.lock ? ' (still locked)' : ''), { duration:1100 }); }
// Bone <-> body-part lock. OFF: a joint dot moves on its own (the art stays where it is, so you can line the bones up with the art).
// ON: the body part is locked to its bone, so dragging a joint moves the part with it; an elbow / knee dot swings and stretches
// the whole arm / leg (upper part + lower part) so the dot stays under your finger.
function edToggleLock(){
  ED.lock = !ED.lock; if(ED.lock && !ED.bones) ED.bones = true;
  edSyncUI(); edDraw();
  // No floating green/black lock notification: the lock button state and aligned green joints
  // are the visual feedback, and locked mode now accepts only joint-point drags.
}

/* ---------- gestures ---------- */
function edSetupPointer(){
  const cv = ED.cv, ptrs = new Map(); let drag = null, moved = false;
  const pos = e=>{ const r = cv.getBoundingClientRect(); return { x:e.clientX-r.left, y:e.clientY-r.top }; };
  const toFig = p=>({ x:(p.x-ED.cam.x)/ED.cam.z, y:(p.y-ED.cam.y)/ED.cam.z });
  const begin = ()=>{ if(!moved){ moved = true; edPush(); } };
  cv.onpointerdown = e=>{
    cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); const p = pos(e); ptrs.set(e.pointerId, p); moved = false;
    document.getElementById('edHint').classList.add('hide');
    if(ptrs.size === 2){ const [a,b] = [...ptrs.values()]; drag = { type:'pinch', d:Math.hypot(a.x-b.x,a.y-b.y)||1, z:ED.cam.z, c:{x:(a.x+b.x)/2,y:(a.y+b.y)/2}, vx:ED.cam.x, vy:ED.cam.y }; return; }
    const W = edWorld(), hit = edPick(p, W), fp = toFig(p);
    if(hit.type === 'locked'){ drag = null; return; }
    if(hit.type === 'pan'){ drag = { type:'pan', p0:p, vx:ED.cam.x, vy:ED.cam.y, dist:0 }; return; }
    if(hit.key !== ED.sel){ ED.sel = hit.key; edSyncUI(); }
    const k = hit.key, w = W[k], st = ED.st[k];
    const isFaceOverlay=k==='mouth_closed' || k==='mouth_open' || k==='mouth_hold';
    if(hit.type === 'move' && ED.lock && k !== 'torso' && !isFaceOverlay) drag = { type:'prot', k, cx:w.x, cy:w.y, a0:Math.atan2(fp.y-w.y, fp.x-w.x), r0:st.rot };
    else if(hit.type === 'move') drag = { type:'move', k, fp0:fp, x0:w.x, y0:w.y };
    else if(hit.type === 'pivot' && ED.lock){
      const off = { x:w.x-fp.x, y:w.y-fp.y };                                   // grab offset, so the dot doesn't jump under the finger
      if(/_lower$/.test(k) || /^hand[LR](?:_(?:fist|point|thumb|palm))?$/.test(k) || /^shoe[LR]$/.test(k)){
        const driver = SHEET_ALT_OF[k] || k;
        if(driver !== k && /^hand[LR](?:_(?:fist|point|thumb|palm))?$/.test(k)) drag = { type:'bone', k, driver, off };
        else drag = { type:'bone', k, driver, off };
        ED.sel = k; edSyncUI();
      } else drag = { type:'pmove', k, off };                                     // root/shoulder/hip/neck joint: move attached chain
    }
    else if(hit.type === 'pivot'){ const c = Math.cos(w.rot), s = Math.sin(w.rot); drag = { type:'pivot', k, ax:w.x-(st.pvx*w.sx*c-st.pvy*w.sy*s), ay:w.y-(st.pvx*w.sx*s+st.pvy*w.sy*c) }; }
    else if(hit.type === 'rot') drag = { type:'rot', k, x:w.x, y:w.y, v0:{ x:fp.x-w.x, y:fp.y-w.y }, s0:st.s, r0:st.rot };
    else if(hit.type === 'len'){ const dir = { x:-Math.sin(w.rot)*Math.sign(w.sy), y:Math.cos(w.rot)*Math.sign(w.sy) }; drag = { type:'len', k, x:w.x, y:w.y, dir, u0:(fp.x-w.x)*dir.x+(fp.y-w.y)*dir.y, h0:st.hy }; }
    else if(hit.type === 'wid'){ const dir = { x:Math.cos(w.rot)*Math.sign(w.sx), y:Math.sin(w.rot)*Math.sign(w.sx) }; drag = { type:'wid', k, x:w.x, y:w.y, dir, u0:(fp.x-w.x)*dir.x+(fp.y-w.y)*dir.y, w0:st.wx }; }
    edDraw();
  };
  cv.onpointermove = e=>{
    if(!ptrs.has(e.pointerId)) return; const p = pos(e); ptrs.set(e.pointerId, p); if(!drag) return;
    if(drag.type === 'pinch'){
      if(ptrs.size < 2) return; const [a,b] = [...ptrs.values()], d = Math.hypot(a.x-b.x,a.y-b.y), c = {x:(a.x+b.x)/2,y:(a.y+b.y)/2};
      const z = Math.max(0.08, Math.min(8, drag.z*d/drag.d)), k = z/drag.z; ED.cam.z = z; ED.cam.x = c.x-(drag.c.x-drag.vx)*k; ED.cam.y = c.y-(drag.c.y-drag.vy)*k; edSyncZoom(); edDraw(); return;
    }
    if(drag.type === 'pan'){ ED.cam.x = drag.vx+p.x-drag.p0.x; ED.cam.y = drag.vy+p.y-drag.p0.y; drag.dist = Math.max(drag.dist, Math.hypot(p.x-drag.p0.x, p.y-drag.p0.y)); edDraw(); return; }
    const fp = toFig(p), st = ED.st[drag.k];
    if(drag.type === 'move'){ begin(); edSetPos(drag.k, drag.x0+fp.x-drag.fp0.x, drag.y0+fp.y-drag.fp0.y); }
    else if(drag.type === 'prot'){          // locked: the part stays on its joint and swings around it (children follow)
      const vx = fp.x-drag.cx, vy = fp.y-drag.cy; if(Math.hypot(vx,vy) < 6/ED.cam.z) return;
      begin(); let r = drag.r0 + (Math.atan2(vy,vx)-drag.a0)*180/Math.PI; st.rot = ((r+180)%360+360)%360-180;
    }
    else if(drag.type === 'pmove'){ begin(); edSetPos(drag.k, fp.x+drag.off.x, fp.y+drag.off.y); }
    else if(drag.type === 'bone'){ begin(); edBoneDrive(drag.driver || drag.k, fp.x+drag.off.x, fp.y+drag.off.y); }
    else if(drag.type === 'pivot'){
      begin(); const w = edWorld()[drag.k], c = Math.cos(w.rot), s = Math.sin(w.rot), vx = fp.x-drag.ax, vy = fp.y-drag.ay;
      st.pvx = (vx*c+vy*s)/w.sx; st.pvy = (-vx*s+vy*c)/w.sy; edSetPos(drag.k, fp.x, fp.y);
    }
    else if(drag.type === 'rot'){
      begin(); const v = { x:fp.x-drag.x, y:fp.y-drag.y }, l0 = Math.hypot(drag.v0.x,drag.v0.y)||1, l1 = Math.hypot(v.x,v.y)||1;
      st.s = Math.max(0.3, Math.min(3, drag.s0*l1/l0));
      let r = drag.r0 + (Math.atan2(v.y,v.x)-Math.atan2(drag.v0.y,drag.v0.x))*180/Math.PI; r = ((r+180)%360+360)%360-180; st.rot = r;
    }
    else if(drag.type === 'len' || drag.type === 'wid'){
      begin(); const u = (fp.x-drag.x)*drag.dir.x+(fp.y-drag.y)*drag.dir.y;
      if(Math.abs(drag.u0) > 1){ const f = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(4, (drag.type==='len'?drag.h0:drag.w0)*u/drag.u0)); if(drag.type==='len') st.hy = f; else st.wx = f; }
    }
    edSyncVals(); edDraw();
  };
  cv.onpointerup = cv.onpointercancel = e=>{
    ptrs.delete(e.pointerId);
    if(!ptrs.size){
      if(drag && drag.type === 'pan' && drag.dist < 6 && ED.sel){ ED.sel = null; edSyncUI(); edDraw(); }   // tap on empty space: deselect, hide the border
      drag = null; if(moved) edSyncHistory();
    } else if(drag && drag.type==='pinch' && ptrs.size < 2) drag = null;
  };
  cv.addEventListener('wheel', e=>{ e.preventDefault(); const p = pos(e); edZoomAt(e.deltaY < 0 ? 1.12 : 1/1.12, p.x, p.y); }, { passive:false });
  cv.oncontextmenu = e=>e.preventDefault();
}

/* ---------- open / close ---------- */
function edInit(){
  if(ED.ready) return; ED.ready = true; ED.cv = document.getElementById('edCanvas'); ED.g = ED.cv.getContext('2d');
  edSetupPointer(); new ResizeObserver(()=>edResize()).observe(document.getElementById('edStage'));
}
function edSetModelName(value){ ED.name = String(value == null ? '' : value).slice(0,40); }
function edOpen(o){
  if(isPlaying && typeof pausePlayback === 'function') pausePlayback();
  edInit();
  edFillIcons();
  ED.arts = o.arts; ED.assign = {}; SHEET_KEYS.forEach(k=>{ if(o.arts[k]) ED.assign[k] = k; });
  ED.view = o.view === 'side' || o.view === 'back' ? o.view : 'front';
  ED.facing = o.facing === -1 ? -1 : 1;
  ED.modelType = o.modelType || (o.sheet && o.sheet.modelType) || 'humanoid';
  ED.extraVariants = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify((o.sheet && o.sheet.customVariants) || {headParts:[],expressions:[],gestures:[]}));
  if(!Array.isArray(ED.extraVariants.headParts)) ED.extraVariants.headParts=[];
  if(!Array.isArray(ED.extraVariants.expressions)) ED.extraVariants.expressions=[];
  if(!Array.isArray(ED.extraVariants.gestures)) ED.extraVariants.gestures=[];
  ED.pendingVariantType = null;
  ED.st = o.st;
  if(o.sheet && Number(o.sheet.v||0)<7) normalizeLegacySheetMouthLayout(o.sheet.parts,o.sheet.draw,ED.st);
  ED.order = o.order.slice(); ED.kind = o.kind; ED.editIdx = o.editIdx == null ? null : o.editIdx; ED.name = o.name || 'Model';
  const nameField=document.getElementById('edModelNameInput'); if(nameField) nameField.value=ED.name;
  ED.init = edSnap(); ED.undo = []; ED.redo = []; ED.sel = 'torso'; ED.mode = 'size'; ED.bones = true; ED.lock = true; ED.assetId = o.assetId || null; ED.returnLib = !!o.returnLib;
  document.getElementById('edHint').classList.remove('hide');
  document.getElementById('sheetEd').classList.add('active'); ED.open = true; ED.needFit = true;
  edSyncUI(); edRenderExtraParts();
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{ edResize(); }));
}
function edExtraCategoryLabel(category){ return category==='headParts' ? 'Head / mouth' : category==='expressions' ? 'Face expression' : 'Hand gesture'; }
function edVariantRemoveFlatBackground(canvas){
  const w=canvas.width,h=canvas.height,n=w*h,ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
  let im; try{ im=ctx.getImageData(0,0,w,h); }catch(e){ return; }
  const d=im.data; let transparent=0;
  for(let i=3;i<d.length;i+=4) if(d[i]<200) transparent++;
  if(transparent>=n*0.01) return; // preserve genuine transparent PNG/WebP alpha
  const samples=[],step=Math.max(1,Math.floor((2*w+2*h)/500));
  for(let x=0;x<w;x+=step){ samples.push((x)*4,((h-1)*w+x)*4); }
  for(let y=0;y<h;y+=step){ samples.push((y*w)*4,(y*w+w-1)*4); }
  const median=k=>{const a=samples.map(i=>d[i+k]).sort((a,b)=>a-b);return a[Math.floor(a.length/2)]||0;};
  const bg=[0,1,2].map(median);
  const near=p=>Math.abs(d[p*4]-bg[0])<27&&Math.abs(d[p*4+1]-bg[1])<27&&Math.abs(d[p*4+2]-bg[2])<27;
  const seen=new Uint8Array(n),queue=new Int32Array(n);let head=0,tail=0;
  const push=p=>{if(p>=0&&p<n&&!seen[p]&&d[p*4+3]>20&&near(p)){seen[p]=1;queue[tail++]=p;}};
  for(let x=0;x<w;x++){push(x);push((h-1)*w+x);}
  for(let y=0;y<h;y++){push(y*w);push(y*w+w-1);}
  while(head<tail){const q=queue[head++],x=q%w;d[q*4+3]=0;if(x>0)push(q-1);if(x<w-1)push(q+1);if(q>=w)push(q-w);if(q<n-w)push(q+w);}
  ctx.putImageData(im,0,0);
}
function edPickExtra(kind){
  if(!ED.open) return;
  if(ED.modelType!=='humanoid'){ showToast('Optional human face and hand parts are only for humanoid models', {duration:2200}); return; }
  let category=kind, subkind='';
  if(kind==='head'){
    const choice=window.prompt('Choose the head part type:\n1 = Open-mouth overlay (talking)\n2 = Closed-mouth overlay\n3 = Blink / closed-eyes full head','1');
    if(choice===null) return;
    const map={'1':'mouth-open','2':'mouth-closed','3':'blink-head','open':'mouth-open','closed':'mouth-closed','blink':'blink-head'};
    subkind=map[String(choice).trim().toLowerCase()];
    if(!subkind){ showToast('Choose 1, 2 or 3', {duration:1600}); return; }
    category='headParts';
  } else if(kind==='expression') category='expressions';
  else if(kind==='gesture') category='gestures';
  ED.pendingVariantType={category,kind:subkind};
  const input=document.getElementById('edExtraFileInput'); input.value=''; input.click();
}
function edHandleExtraFile(input){
  const file=input.files && input.files[0]; input.value=''; if(!file || !ED.pendingVariantType) return;
  const pending=ED.pendingVariantType; ED.pendingVariantType=null;
  const reader=new FileReader();
  reader.onload=()=>{
    const img=new Image(); img.onload=()=>{
      const max=640, scale=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth,img.naturalHeight));
      const cv=document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalWidth*scale)); cv.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalHeight*scale));
      const cx=cv.getContext('2d'); cx.clearRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height); cx.drawImage(img,0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
      // If the image service returned a flat opaque backdrop rather than real alpha, remove only
      // the edge-connected background pixels. Wide margins in the prompt keep the artwork safe.
      edVariantRemoveFlatBackground(cv);
      const src=cv.toDataURL('image/png');
      const category=pending.category, kind=pending.kind || '';
      const defaultName=(kind==='mouth-open'?'Mouth open':kind==='mouth-closed'?'Mouth closed':kind==='blink-head'?'Blink head':category==='expressions'?'Face expression':'Right-hand gesture');
      let name=(file.name||defaultName).replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'').replace(/[_-]+/g,' ').trim();
      name=window.prompt('Give this part a name',name||defaultName);
      if(name===null) name=defaultName;
      name=String(name||defaultName).trim().slice(0,42)||defaultName;
      const item={id:'extra_'+Date.now().toString(36)+'_'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7),name,kind,src,w:cv.width,h:cv.height,createdAt:Date.now()};
      ED.extraVariants[category].push(item);
      edRenderExtraParts();
      showToast('Added '+name, {duration:1500});
    };
    img.onerror=()=>showToast('Could not read that image', {duration:1800}); img.src=reader.result;
  };
  reader.onerror=()=>showToast('Could not read that file', {duration:1800}); reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}
function edRemoveExtraVariant(category,id){
  if(!ED.extraVariants || !Array.isArray(ED.extraVariants[category])) return;
  ED.extraVariants[category]=ED.extraVariants[category].filter(v=>v.id!==id); edRenderExtraParts();
}
function edRenderExtraParts(){
  const count=document.getElementById('edExtraCount'); if(!count||!ED.extraVariants) return;
  const defs=[
    {key:'headParts',target:'edExtraHeadParts',count:'edExtraHeadCount',pick:'head',label:'Add head part'},
    {key:'expressions',target:'edExtraExpressions',count:'edExtraExpressionCount',pick:'expression',label:'Add expression'},
    {key:'gestures',target:'edExtraGestures',count:'edExtraGestureCount',pick:'gesture',label:'Add gesture'}
  ];
  const allCount=defs.reduce((n,d)=>n+(Array.isArray(ED.extraVariants[d.key])?ED.extraVariants[d.key].length:0),0);
  count.textContent=allCount+' added';
  defs.forEach(d=>{
    const row=document.getElementById(d.target), rowCount=document.getElementById(d.count);
    if(!row) return;
    const items=Array.isArray(ED.extraVariants[d.key])?ED.extraVariants[d.key]:[];
    if(rowCount) rowCount.textContent=items.length+' '+(items.length===1?'part':'parts');
    const cards=items.map(v=>{
      const name=escapeHtml(v.name||'Extra part');
      const detail=v.kind?escapeHtml(v.kind.replace(/-/g,' ')):escapeHtml(edExtraCategoryLabel(d.key));
      const id=String(v.id||'').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'');
      return '<div class="ed-extra-card"><button class="ed-extra-remove" type="button" title="Remove" aria-label="Remove '+name+'" onclick="edRemoveExtraVariant(\''+d.key+'\',\''+id+'\')">×</button><img src="'+v.src+'" alt=""><b title="'+name+'">'+name+'</b><small title="'+detail+'">'+detail+'</small></div>';
    }).join('');
    const add='<button type="button" class="ed-extra-add-card" onclick="edPickExtra(\''+d.pick+'\')" aria-label="'+d.label+'"><span class="plus">+</span><span> Add </span></button>';
    row.innerHTML=cards+add;
  });
}
function edClose(){ ED.open = false; document.getElementById('sheetEd').classList.remove('active'); if(ED.returnLib){ ED.returnLib = false; openModelLibrary(); } }
let edCancelArmed = 0;
function edCancel(){
  if(ED.undo.length && Date.now() > edCancelArmed){ edCancelArmed = Date.now() + 2500; showToast('Tap close again to discard changes', { icon:toastIcons.info, duration:2200 }); return; }
  edCancelArmed = 0; edClose();
}
function buildFromSheet(img, fname, modelType){
  const type = modelType || activeSheetType || 'humanoid';
  const r = sheetParse(img, type);
  if(r.error){
    if(type === 'humanoid' && r.fallbackAutoRig && r.cleanedCanvas && autoRigState){
      const detected = runAutoRigDetection(r.cleanedCanvas);
      if(!detected.error){
        Object.assign(autoRigState, detected);
        autoRigState.modelType = 'humanoid';
        autoRigState.originalJoints = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(detected.joints));
        autoRigState.warnings = (detected.warnings || []).concat([
          'The upload contains touching cut-out parts, so it opened in whole-image auto-rig mode. The preview provides 17 draggable joint handles; move the blue points onto the shoulders, elbows, wrists, hips, knees and feet before creating the rig.'
        ]);
        closeAutoRigUploader();
        openAutoRigPreview();
        showToast('Only '+r.detectedRegions+' separate regions were found. Opened the 17-joint rig preview.', { icon:toastIcons.info, duration:2600 });
        return;
      }
    }
    showToast(r.error, { icon:toastIcons.info, duration:3000 });
    document.getElementById('autoRigUploadPreviewImg').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('autoRigUploadIcon').style.display = '';
    setUploadSheetType(type);
    document.getElementById('autoRigUploadPreviewImg').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('autoRigUploadIcon').style.display = '';
    return;
  }
  closeAutoRigUploader();
  // Anatomy-position 12-part and current 14-part humanoid sheets are upright/front-facing. Legacy/species sheets keep their side-view convention.
  const view = (type === 'humanoid' && (r.layout === 'anatomy12' || r.layout === 'anatomy14' || r.layout === 'base14')) ? 'front' : 'side', side = view === 'side';
  const createKind = sheetCreateMode === 'newAsset' ? 'newAsset' : 'new';
  edOpen({ kind:createKind, view, modelType:type, arts:r.arts, st:edDefaultLayout(r.arts, view, type), order:(side?SHEET_DEFAULT_ORDER_SIDE:SHEET_DEFAULT_ORDER).filter(k=>r.arts[k]), name:(fname||'Model').replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'').slice(0,24) });
}
function edShowLoading(message){
  const overlay=document.getElementById('edLoadingOverlay');
  const title=document.getElementById('edLoadingTitle');
  if(title)title.textContent=message||'Opening character editor';
  if(overlay){overlay.classList.add('active');overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');}
}
function edHideLoading(){
  const overlay=document.getElementById('edLoadingOverlay');
  if(overlay){overlay.classList.remove('active');overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}
}
function edHideLoadingAfterPaint(){
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(edHideLoading));
}
// Rebuilds the 10 editable "arts" from a saved sheet (char.sheet / asset.sheet). Resolves to null if any part image fails to load.
function edLoadSheetArts(sh){
  const arts = {}, keys = Object.keys(sh.parts);
  return Promise.all(keys.map(k=>new Promise(res=>{
    const p = sh.parts[k], im = new Image();
    im.onload = ()=>{ const cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = p.w; cv.height = p.h; cv.getContext('2d').drawImage(im,0,0); const a = edMakeArt(cv, k, sh.modelType || 'humanoid'); a.dpx = p.dpx; a.dpy = p.dpy; a.src = null; arts[k] = a; res(); };
    im.onerror = res; im.src = p.src;
  }))).then(()=> keys.some(k=>!arts[k]) ? null : arts);
}
// Re-open an already-built model (Model tab > Edit rig).
function edOpenFromChar(idx){
  const c=characters[idx];if(!c||!c.sheet)return;
  edShowLoading('Opening character editor');
  let opened=false;
  return edLoadSheetArts(c.sheet).then(arts=>{
    if(!arts){showToast("Couldn't load this model's parts",{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    edOpen({kind:'edit',editIdx:idx,view:c.sheet.view,facing:c.sheet.facing,modelType:c.modelType||c.sheet.modelType||'humanoid',sheet:c.sheet,arts,st:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(c.sheet.edit.st)),order:c.sheet.edit.order,name:c.name});
    opened=true;edHideLoadingAfterPaint();
  }).catch(err=>{
    console.error('Ethvelle: character editor could not be opened',err);
    showToast("Couldn't open this character editor",{icon:toastIcons.info});
  }).finally(()=>{if(!opened)edHideLoading();});
}
// UPDATE: "My models" > pencil now opens the FULL editor (same screen as when the model was first added) for that saved model.
// Rename is its own button next to it. Done saves the changes back into the saved model (and any copy of it on stage).
function edOpenFromAsset(id,returnLib){
  let a=modelAssets.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id));
  if(!a){
    const published=Array.isArray(publishedModelAssets)?publishedModelAssets.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id)):null;
    if(published){ a=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(published)); delete a.origin; modelAssets.push(a); saveModelAssetsToStorage(); }
  }
  if(!a)return;
  if(!a.sheet||!a.sheet.parts||!a.sheet.edit){showToast("This older model can't open in the editor. Rename it here, or upload its part sheet again",{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2800});openRenameModelModal(id);return;}
  edShowLoading('Preparing model editor');
  let opened=false;
  return edLoadSheetArts(a.sheet).then(arts=>{
