    if(!arts){showToast("Couldn't load this model's parts",{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    closeModelLibrary();
    edOpen({kind:'asset',assetId:id,returnLib:!!returnLib,view:a.sheet.view,facing:a.sheet.facing,modelType:a.modelType||a.sheet.modelType||'humanoid',sheet:a.sheet,arts,st:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(a.sheet.edit.st)),order:a.sheet.edit.order,name:a.name});
    opened=true;edHideLoadingAfterPaint();
  }).catch(err=>{
    console.error('Ethvelle: saved-model editor could not be opened',err);
    showToast("Couldn't open this model editor",{icon:toastIcons.info});
  }).finally(()=>{if(!opened)edHideLoading();});
}

/* v260 fix: Done preserves authored body-part attachment offsets; never auto-reset ax/ay before compile. */
/* ---------- 3) Done: layout -> rig + puppet ---------- */
function edCompile(){
  // Front-facing rigs are displayed in a temporary T-pose while editing. Compile the neutral/rest
  // pose instead, so Done never saves that visual stance as the character's default project pose.
  const W = edWorld(true), st = ED.st, P = (k,lx,ly)=>edL2W(W,k,lx,ly), pv = k=>({ x:W[k].x, y:W[k].y }), mix = (a,b,t)=>({ x:a.x+(b.x-a.x)*t, y:a.y+(b.y-a.y)*t });
  const J = { pelvis:pv('torso'), neck:pv('head'), headTop:P('head', st.head.pvx, 0) };
  J.chest = mix(J.pelvis, J.neck, 0.86);
  if(st.tail && edArt('tail')){ J.tailBase=pv('tail'); J.tailTip=P('tail',0,edArt('tail').h*0.5); }
  ['L','R'].forEach(S=>{
    const up='arm'+S+'_upper', lo='arm'+S+'_lower', ha='hand'+S, th='leg'+S+'_upper', sh='leg'+S+'_lower';
    J['shoulder'+S] = pv(up); J['elbow'+S] = pv(lo);
    // The wrist and the fingertip are now the hand piece's OWN attach point and bottom edge -- a real
    // independent piece, not a percentage guess along the forearm the way the leg's ankle still is below
    // (legS_lower still covers both the shin AND the foot bone in one image, same trick the arm used to use).
    // st[ha] can be missing on an old project re-opened from before this piece existed, so fall back to
    // that same percentage guess in that one case, exactly as it always worked.
    J['wrist'+S] = st[ha] ? pv(ha) : mix(J['elbow'+S], P(lo, st[lo].pvx, edArt(lo).h), 0.85);
    J['hand'+S] = st[ha] ? P(ha, st[ha].pvx, edArt(ha).h) : P(lo, st[lo].pvx, edArt(lo).h);
    J['hip'+S] = pv(th); J['knee'+S] = pv(sh);
    if(st['shoe'+S]){
      J['ankle'+S] = pv('shoe'+S);
      J['foot'+S] = P('shoe'+S, st['shoe'+S].pvx, edArt('shoe'+S).h);
    }else{
      J['foot'+S] = P(sh, st[sh].pvx, edArt(sh).h);
      J['ankle'+S] = mix(J['knee'+S], J['foot'+S], 0.9);
    }
  });
  const ang = (a,b)=>Math.atan2(b.y-a.y, b.x-a.x), ra = { head:ang(J.neck,J.headTop), torso:ang(J.pelvis,J.neck) };
  if(J.tailBase && J.tailTip) ra.tail=ang(J.tailBase,J.tailTip);
  ['L','R'].forEach(S=>{
    ra['arm'+S+'_upper'] = ang(J['shoulder'+S],J['elbow'+S]); ra['arm'+S+'_lower'] = ang(J['elbow'+S],J['wrist'+S]); ra['hand'+S] = ang(J['wrist'+S],J['hand'+S]);
    ra['leg'+S+'_upper'] = ang(J['hip'+S],J['knee'+S]);
    ra['leg'+S+'_lower'] = st['shoe'+S] ? ang(J['knee'+S],J['ankle'+S]) : ang(J['knee'+S],J['foot'+S]);
    if(st['shoe'+S]) ra['shoe'+S] = ang(J['ankle'+S],J['foot'+S]);
  });
  // The optional expression/gesture parts ride the exact same bone as their base part (see SHEET_BONES), so
  // they inherit that part's rest angle rather than needing one of their own.
  ra.mouth_closed = ra.head; Object.keys(SHEET_ALT_OF).forEach(a=>{ ra[a] = ra[SHEET_ALT_OF[a]]; });
  let x0 = 1e9, y0 = 1e9, x1 = -1e9, y1 = -1e9;
  ED.order.forEach(k=>{ const a = edArt(k); if(!a) return; [[0,0],[a.w,0],[a.w,a.h],[0,a.h]].forEach(([lx,ly])=>{ const p = P(k,lx,ly); x0 = Math.min(x0,p.x); x1 = Math.max(x1,p.x); y0 = Math.min(y0,p.y); y1 = Math.max(y1,p.y); }); });
  const E = Math.max(1, y1-y0), cx = (x0+x1)/2, cy = (y0+y1)/2;
  const all = Object.values(J), mnX = Math.min(...all.map(p=>p.x)), mxX = Math.max(...all.map(p=>p.x)), mnY = Math.min(...all.map(p=>p.y)), mxY = Math.max(...all.map(p=>p.y));
  const pd = 0.1*Math.max(1, mxY-mnY), Wv = Math.max(1,mxX-mnX)+2*pd, Hv = Math.max(1,mxY-mnY)+2*pd, F = {};
  Object.keys(J).forEach(k=>{ F[k] = { x:(J[k].x-mnX+pd)/Wv, y:(J[k].y-mnY+pd)/Hv }; });
  const rigOverride = sheetBuildRigOverrideFromJoints(F, Wv, Hv), Hj = Math.max(1, Math.max(J.footL.y,J.footR.y)-J.headTop.y);
  rigOverride.figFrac = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(1.2, SHEET_FILL*Hj/E));
  const parts = {}, draw = {};
  ED.order.forEach(k=>{
    const a = edArt(k); if(!a) return; if(!a.src) a.src = a.canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
    parts[k] = { src:a.src, w:a.w, h:a.h, dpx:a.dpx, dpy:a.dpy, skinTop:a.skinTop, jointColors:a.jointColors||{} };
    draw[k] = { pvx:st[k].pvx, pvy:st[k].pvy, rot:W[k].rot, sx:W[k].sx, sy:W[k].sy, ra:ra[k] };
    // Mouth pieces are a small facial feature positioned BY HAND inside the head's own artwork
    // (st.ax/ay live in the head's pixel space and are draggable in the editor), unlike head_blink
    // or the fist swaps which share their base part's own bone + pivot formula. Store the mouth's
    // offset/rotation/scale relative to the head's pivot so the runtime renderer can nest it inside
    // the head's live transform (see drawSheetCharacter) instead of mis-anchoring it at the head
    // bone's joint the way a same-bone swap would.
    if(k === 'mouth_closed' || k === 'mouth_open' || k === 'mouth_hold'){
      const hp = st.head;
      draw[k].nestDx = st[k].ax - hp.pvx; draw[k].nestDy = st[k].ay - hp.pvy;
      draw[k].nestRot = (st[k].rot||0)*Math.PI/180;
      draw[k].nestSx = st[k].s*st[k].wx*st[k].fx; draw[k].nestSy = st[k].s*st[k].hy*st[k].fy;
    }
  });
  const th = edArt('head'), tk = 72/Math.max(th.w, th.h), tc = document.createElement('canvas'); tc.width = Math.max(1,Math.round(th.w*tk)); tc.height = Math.max(1,Math.round(th.h*tk)); tc.getContext('2d').drawImage(th.canvas,0,0,tc.width,tc.height);
  const sheet = { v:7, view:ED.view, facing:ED.facing, modelType:ED.modelType || 'humanoid', parts, draw, order:ED.order.slice(), edit:{ st:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(st)), order:ED.order.slice() },
    customVariants:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ED.extraVariants || {headParts:[],expressions:[],gestures:[]})),
    refLen:Math.max(1e-3, Math.hypot(J.neck.x-J.pelvis.x, J.neck.y-J.pelvis.y)), H:Hj, rootOff:{ x:J.pelvis.x-cx, y:J.pelvis.y-cy }, thumb:tc.toDataURL('image/png') };
  sheet.rigJoints = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(F));
  sheet.rigSourceW = Wv; sheet.rigSourceH = Hv;
  sheet.rigOverride = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(rigOverride));
  return { sheet, rigOverride };
}
// Puts a sheet character's pelvis where it makes the whole figure sit centred on the stage.
function sheetPlace(c){
  if(!c || !c.bones || !c.bones.hip) return;
  // The live site uses the older hip-rooted pose rig. The Lab's sheet rig is reconstructed
  // separately by sheetRigForCharacter(), anchored to this hip so transforms and timeline poses work.
  c.hipRestY = c.bones.hip.y;
}
function applySheetToChar(c, sheet, rigOverride){
  if(!c) return;
  if(!c.restTransform && c.modelTransform) c.restTransform = { ...c.modelTransform };
  c.sheet = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(sheet || {}));
  c.sheet.rigOverride = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(rigOverride || c.sheet.rigOverride || {}));
  c.rigOverride = undefined; // the Lab's sheet-rig override (21 bones humanoid / 22 species) must never be applied to the legacy rig
  c.thumb = c.sheet.thumb || c.thumb;
  c.parts = {}; c.type = 'custom'; c.modelType = c.sheet.modelType || 'humanoid'; c.skinMesh = null;
  ensureSpeciesTailBone(c);
  if(!c.expr) c.expr = sheetExprFresh();
  sheetPlace(c);
}
// Clear only temporary bone offsets after Done; keep the user's stage position/scale and clips.
// The newly compiled sheet supplies the screenshot's saved default pose, while the legacy rig
// must return to its own base angles so an old drag/animation offset cannot distort that sheet.
function resetCharacterBonesToRest(c){
  if(!c || !c.bones) return;
  const angles = c.baseAngles || {};
  Object.keys(c.bones).forEach(k=>{
    if(c.bones[k] && Number.isFinite(angles[k])) c.bones[k].angle = angles[k];
  });
  if(c.bones.hip && Number.isFinite(c.hipRestY)) c.bones.hip.y = c.hipRestY;
}
function edDone(){
  const nameField=document.getElementById('edModelNameInput');
  ED.name=String(nameField ? nameField.value : ED.name || 'Model').trim().slice(0,40) || 'Model';
  // Animal/bird body-part attachment positions are authored rig data, not a temporary pose.
  // Resetting ax/ay to ED.init here silently discarded every species body-position adjustment.
  // Preserve their exact edited layout. edCompile() uses edWorld(true), which removes only the
  // temporary display stance while retaining attachment positions, rotations, sizing and pivots.
  // Keep the existing humanoid save/reset behavior unchanged; manual reset remains available.
  const preserveSpeciesLayout = ED.modelType === 'animal' || ED.modelType === 'bird';
  if(!preserveSpeciesLayout && (!edResetSkeletonToDefault(true) || !edSkeletonAtDefault())){
    showToast('Reset the skeleton to its default position before saving this model', {icon:toastIcons.info, duration:2400});
    return;
  }
  let out;
  try{ out = edCompile(); }
  catch(err){ console.error(err); showToast("Couldn't build this model", {icon:toastIcons.info, duration:2400}); return; }
  const sheet = out.sheet, rigOverride = out.rigOverride;
  sheet.rigOverride = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(rigOverride));
  const inProjectEditor = !!(document.getElementById('screen-editor') && document.getElementById('screen-editor').classList.contains('active'));
  if(ED.kind === 'asset'){
    const asset = modelAssets.find(x=>x.id === ED.assetId);
    if(!asset){ showToast('That saved model no longer exists', {icon:toastIcons.info}); edClose(); return; }
    asset.name = ED.name;
    asset.sheet = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(sheet));
    asset.rigOverride = undefined;
    asset.modelType = sheet.modelType || ED.modelType || 'humanoid';
    asset.thumb = sheet.thumb;
    asset.previewThumb = undefined; // rebuild the full-body card image from the newly saved rig
    asset.parts = {};
    asset.updatedAt = Date.now();
    const assetPersisted = saveModelAssetsToStorage();
    characters.forEach(c=>{ if(String(c.assetId) === String(asset.id) && c.type === 'custom'){ c.name=ED.name; applySheetToChar(c, sheet, rigOverride); c.modelType=asset.modelType; resetCharacterBonesToRest(c); } });
    if(inProjectEditor){ syncActiveCharacterRefs(); }
    const returnLib = !!ED.returnLib;
    edClose();
    if(inProjectEditor){ drawStick(); renderCharacterLayers(); syncTransformSliders(); persistProjectState(); }
    if(!returnLib) renderModelsTab();
    showToast(assetPersisted ? 'Model updated' : 'Model updated for this session; storage is full', {duration:2200});
    if(navigator.vibrate) try{ navigator.vibrate(12); }catch(e){}
    return;
  }
  if(ED.kind === 'edit' && characters[ED.editIdx]){
    const c = characters[ED.editIdx];
    c.name = ED.name;
    applySheetToChar(c, sheet, rigOverride); resetCharacterBonesToRest(c); c.modelType = ED.modelType || 'humanoid';
    if(ED.editIdx === activeCharIdx) syncActiveCharacterRefs();
    updateModelAssetFromChar(c);
    edClose();
    if(inProjectEditor){ drawStick(); renderCharacterLayers(); syncTransformSliders(); persistProjectState(); renderTimelineClip(); syncTimelineInnerWidth(); renderRuler(); }
    showToast('Model updated', {duration:1400});
  } else if(ED.kind === 'newAsset'){
    const c = makeCharacter(ED.name);
    applySheetToChar(c, sheet, rigOverride); resetCharacterBonesToRest(c); c.name = ED.name || 'Model'; c.assetId = undefined;
    saveCharacterAsModelAsset(c, {silent:true});
    edClose(); renderModelsTab();
    showToast('Model saved to your library', {duration:1500});
  } else {
    const c = makeCharacter(ED.name);
    applySheetToChar(c, sheet, rigOverride); resetCharacterBonesToRest(c); c.name = ED.name || 'Model'; c.modelType = ED.modelType || 'humanoid';
    c.expr = sheetExprFresh();
    characters.push(c); activeCharIdx = characters.length-1; syncActiveCharacterRefs(); selectedJoint = null;
    saveCharacterAsModelAsset(c, {silent:true});
    edClose();
    if(inProjectEditor){ drawStick(); renderCharacterLayers(); syncTransformSliders(); persistProjectState(); renderTimelineClip(); syncTimelineInnerWidth(); renderRuler(); }
    showToast('Model ready', {duration:1400});
  }
  if(navigator.vibrate) try{ navigator.vibrate(12); }catch(e){}
}



/* ===== ETHVELLE MAIN-SITE BRIDGE FOR THE MODEL LAB'S 21-BONE HUMANOID / 22-BONE SPECIES SHEET RIG ===== */
const ED_ICON_PATHS = {
  x:'<path d="M18 6 6 18M6 6l12 12"/>',
  undo:'<path d="M9 14 4 9l5-5"/><path d="M4 9h10.5a5.5 5.5 0 0 1 0 11H13"/>',
  redo:'<path d="m15 14 5-5-5-5"/><path d="M20 9H9.5a5.5 5.5 0 0 0 0 11H11"/>',
  eye:'<path d="M2 12s3.5-7 10-7 10 7 10 7-3.5 7-10 7S2 12 2 12Z"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"/>',
  eyeOff:'<path d="m3 3 18 18M10.6 10.6a2 2 0 0 0 2.8 2.8M9.9 5.2A10.6 10.6 0 0 1 12 5c6.5 0 10 7 10 7a17 17 0 0 1-3.3 4.1M6.2 6.2C3.5 8 2 12 2 12s3.5 7 10 7a10 10 0 0 0 4-.8"/>',
  lock:'<rect x="4" y="10" width="16" height="11" rx="2"/><path d="M8 10V7a4 4 0 0 1 8 0v3"/>',
  unlock:'<rect x="4" y="10" width="16" height="11" rx="2"/><path d="M8 10V7a4 4 0 0 1 7.6-1.7"/>',
  flipH:'<path d="M12 3v18M4 7l-3 5 3 5M20 7l3 5-3 5"/>',
  flipV:'<path d="M3 12h18M7 4l5-3 5 3M7 20l5 3 5-3"/>',
  copy:'<rect x="8" y="8" width="12" height="12" rx="2"/><path d="M16 8V5a2 2 0 0 0-2-2H5a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v9a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h3"/>',
  reset:'<path d="M3 12a9 9 0 1 0 3-6.7L3 8"/><path d="M3 3v5h5"/>',
  swap:'<path d="M16 3l4 4-4 4M20 7H4M8 21l-4-4 4-4M4 17h16"/>'
};
function edIcon(name){ return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">'+(ED_ICON_PATHS[name]||'')+'</svg>'; }
function edFillIcons(){
  document.querySelectorAll('#sheetEd i[data-ic]').forEach(el=>{ el.innerHTML=edIcon(el.dataset.ic); });
}
const SHEET_RIG_BONE_DEFS = [
  {key:'root',parent:null},
  {key:'pelvis',parent:'root',a:'pelvis',b:'pelvisTop'},
  {key:'spine_lower',parent:'pelvis',a:'pelvisTop',b:'spineA'},
  {key:'spine_upper',parent:'spine_lower',a:'spineA',b:'spineB'},
  {key:'chest',parent:'spine_upper',a:'spineB',b:'chest'},
  {key:'neck',parent:'chest',a:'chest',b:'neck'},
  {key:'head',parent:'neck',a:'neck',b:'headTop'},
  {key:'tail',parent:'pelvis',at:0,a:'tailBase',b:'tailTip'}
].concat(['L','R'].flatMap(S=>[
  {key:'clavicle'+S,parent:'chest',a:'chest',b:'shoulder'+S},
  {key:'upperArm'+S,parent:'clavicle'+S,a:'shoulder'+S,b:'elbow'+S},
  {key:'forearm'+S,parent:'upperArm'+S,a:'elbow'+S,b:'wrist'+S},
  {key:'hand'+S,parent:'forearm'+S,a:'wrist'+S,b:'hand'+S},
  {key:'thigh'+S,parent:'pelvis',at:0,a:'hip'+S,b:'knee'+S},
  {key:'shin'+S,parent:'thigh'+S,a:'knee'+S,b:'ankle'+S},
  {key:'foot'+S,parent:'shin'+S,a:'ankle'+S,b:'foot'+S}
]));
const SHEET_RIG_JOINT_KEYS = ['headTop','neck','chest','pelvis','tailBase','tailTip'].concat(['L','R'].flatMap(S=>['shoulder','elbow','wrist','hand','hip','knee','ankle','foot'].map(n=>n+S)));
const SHEET_RIG_SPINE_SPLIT = [0,.2,.5,.78,1];
const SHEET_RIG_DEFAULT_TEMPLATE = {headTop:[0,.24],neck:[0,.33],chest:[0,.364],pelvis:[0,.5],tailBase:[-.085,.50],tailTip:[-.205,.50],shoulderL:[-.035,.364],elbowL:[-.09,.4],wristL:[-.15,.43],handL:[-.19,.45],hipL:[-.03,.505],kneeL:[-.075,.585],ankleL:[-.1,.66],footL:[-.105,.7]};
function sheetRigWithSpineJoints(J){
  const p=J.pelvis, c=J.chest, at=t=>({x:p.x+(c.x-p.x)*t,y:p.y+(c.y-p.y)*t});
  return {...J,pelvisTop:at(SHEET_RIG_SPINE_SPLIT[1]),spineA:at(SHEET_RIG_SPINE_SPLIT[2]),spineB:at(SHEET_RIG_SPINE_SPLIT[3])};
}
function sheetBuildRigOverrideFromJoints(joints, srcW, srcH){
  const J0={};
  SHEET_RIG_JOINT_KEYS.forEach(k=>{ const p=joints[k]||{x:.5,y:.5}; J0[k]={x:p.x*srcW,y:p.y*srcH}; });
  const J=sheetRigWithSpineJoints(J0), rs={}, lengths={}, angles={}, offsets={};
  const H=Math.max(1,Math.max((J.footL||J.ankleL).y,(J.footR||J.ankleR).y)-J.headTop.y);
  SHEET_RIG_BONE_DEFS.forEach(d=>{
    if(!d.parent){ rs.root={s:J.pelvis,e:J.pelvis}; return; }
    const A=J[d.a], B=J[d.b], P=rs[d.parent], t=d.at===undefined?1:d.at;
    const dx=B.x-A.x, dy=B.y-A.y;
    lengths[d.key]=Math.max(H*.004,Math.hypot(dx,dy))/H;
    angles[d.key]=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
    offsets[d.key]={x:(A.x-(P.s.x+(P.e.x-P.s.x)*t))/H,y:(A.y-(P.s.y+(P.e.y-P.s.y)*t))/H};
    rs[d.key]={s:A,e:B};
  });
  return {ver:2,lengths,angles,offsets};
}
function sheetRigFromJoints(Jin){
  const J=sheetRigWithSpineJoints(Jin), bones={}, rs={};
  SHEET_RIG_BONE_DEFS.forEach(d=>{
    if(!d.parent){ bones.root={x:J.pelvis.x,y:J.pelvis.y,angle:0,parent:null,length:0}; rs.root={s:J.pelvis,e:J.pelvis}; return; }
    const A=J[d.a],B=J[d.b],P=rs[d.parent],t=d.at===undefined?1:d.at,dx=B.x-A.x,dy=B.y-A.y;
    const ox=A.x-(P.s.x+(P.e.x-P.s.x)*t),oy=A.y-(P.s.y+(P.e.y-P.s.y)*t);
    const bone={parent:d.parent,length:Math.max(1e-3,Math.hypot(dx,dy)),angle:Math.atan2(dy,dx)};
    if(d.at!==undefined) bone.attachAt=d.at;
    if(Math.abs(ox)>1e-6||Math.abs(oy)>1e-6) bone.off={x:ox,y:oy};
    if(d.key==='head') bone.isHead=true;
    bones[d.key]=bone; rs[d.key]={s:A,e:B};
  });
  return bones;
}
function sheetSolveRig(B, BA){
  const out={};
  const rec=(k,depth)=>{
    if(out[k]) return out[k];
    const b=B&&B[k]; if(!b||depth>40) return null;
    const p=b.parent?rec(b.parent,depth+1):null;
    let sx=b.x||0,sy=b.y||0,D=0;
    if(p){
      const t=b.attachAt===undefined?1:b.attachAt,c=Math.cos(p.D),sn=Math.sin(p.D),ox=b.off?b.off.x:0,oy=b.off?b.off.y:0;
      sx=p.s.x+(p.e.x-p.s.x)*t+ox*c-oy*sn; sy=p.s.y+(p.e.y-p.s.y)*t+ox*sn+oy*c; D=p.D;
    }
    const ang=Number.isFinite(b.angle)?b.angle:0,len=Number.isFinite(b.length)?b.length:0,rest=(BA&&Number.isFinite(BA[k]))?BA[k]:ang,a=ang+D;
    return out[k]={s:{x:sx,y:sy},e:{x:sx+Math.cos(a)*len,y:sy+Math.sin(a)*len},a,D:D+(ang-rest)};
  };
  Object.keys(B||{}).forEach(k=>rec(k,0));
  return out;
}
function ensureSpeciesTailBone(char){
  if(!char || !char.bones) return;
  const type=char.modelType || (char.sheet && char.sheet.modelType) || 'humanoid';
  if(type!=='animal' && type!=='bird') return;
  if(!char.bones.tail){
    const sh=Number(stageH)||600;
    char.bones.tail={parent:'spine',length:sh*.075,angle:Math.PI,attachAt:.62,attachOffsetX:-sh*.025,attachOffsetY:0};
  }
  if(!char.baseAngles) char.baseAngles={};
  if(!Number.isFinite(char.baseAngles.tail)) char.baseAngles.tail=Number.isFinite(char.bones.tail.angle)?char.bones.tail.angle:Math.PI;
}
function sheetRigForCharacter(char){
  ensureSpeciesTailBone(char);
  const sh=char.sheet, ov=sh.rigOverride||{};
  const cx=stageW/2, J={};
  Object.keys(SHEET_RIG_DEFAULT_TEMPLATE).forEach(k=>{
    const t=SHEET_RIG_DEFAULT_TEMPLATE[k]; J[k]={x:cx+t[0]*stageH,y:t[1]*stageH};
    if(/L$/.test(k)) J[k.slice(0,-1)+'R']={x:cx-t[0]*stageH,y:t[1]*stageH};
  });
  const bones=sheetRigFromJoints(J), baseAngles={};
  // The tail branch belongs only to animal/bird sheets. Keep humanoid sheet rigs unchanged.
  const modelType=char.modelType || (char.sheet && char.sheet.modelType) || 'humanoid';
  if(modelType!=='animal' && modelType!=='bird') delete bones.tail;
  const scale=stageH*(Number(ov.figFrac)||.5);
  Object.keys(ov.lengths||{}).forEach(k=>{if(bones[k]&&Number.isFinite(ov.lengths[k]))bones[k].length=Math.max(1e-3,ov.lengths[k]*scale);});
  Object.keys(ov.angles||{}).forEach(k=>{if(bones[k]&&Number.isFinite(ov.angles[k]))bones[k].angle=ov.angles[k];});
  Object.keys(ov.offsets||{}).forEach(k=>{if(bones[k]&&ov.offsets[k]&&Number.isFinite(ov.offsets[k].x)&&Number.isFinite(ov.offsets[k].y))bones[k].off={x:ov.offsets[k].x*scale,y:ov.offsets[k].y*scale};});
  const rootScale=stageH*(Number(ov.figFrac)||.5)/Math.max(1,Number(sh.H)||1);
  const hip=char.bones&&char.bones.hip?char.bones.hip:{x:stageW/2,y:stageH*.5};
  bones.root.x=hip.x+(Number(sh.rootOff&&sh.rootOff.x)||0)*rootScale;
  bones.root.y=hip.y+(Number(sh.rootOff&&sh.rootOff.y)||0)*rootScale;
  Object.keys(bones).forEach(k=>baseAngles[k]=bones[k].angle);
  // Main-site animation keys are absolute legacy angles. Distribute those deltas across the
  // complete 21-bone humanoid / 22-bone species puppet so extra spine, shoulder, neck, foot and tail joints follow.
  const delta=k=>{const b=char.bones&&char.bones[k],r=char.baseAngles&&char.baseAngles[k];return b&&Number.isFinite(b.angle)&&Number.isFinite(r)?b.angle-r:0;};
  const s=delta('spine'),head=delta('head'),legL=delta('legL'),legR=delta('legR');
  const pelvis=(legL+legR)*.025;
  const p={
    pelvis:pelvis,
    spine_lower:s*.4-pelvis, spine_upper:s*.35, chest:s*.25,
    neck:s*.08,
    head:head-s-s*.08,
    tail:delta('tail')
  };
  ['L','R'].forEach(S=>{
    const a=delta('arm'+S),lo=delta('arm'+S+'_lo'),leg=delta('leg'+S),leglo=delta('leg'+S+'_lo'),hand=delta('hand'+S);
    const clavicle=a*.08-s*.025;
    p['clavicle'+S]=clavicle;
    p['upperArm'+S]=a-s-clavicle;
    p['forearm'+S]=lo-a;
    p['hand'+S]=hand-lo;
    p['thigh'+S]=leg-pelvis;
    p['shin'+S]=leglo-leg;
    // The legacy puppet has no separate toe bone; this small counter-rotation gives the
    // sheet rig's foot/ankle point a natural landing and toe-off response to shin movement.
    p['foot'+S]=-(leglo-leg)*.22;
  });
  Object.keys(bones).forEach(k=>{if(k!=='root')bones[k].angle=baseAngles[k]+(Number(p[k])||0);});
  return {bones,baseAngles};
}

// Main-site upload buttons now always enter the Lab part-sheet creator. The separate legacy
// preview is left available only for previously saved autoRig characters through its edit route.
function openAutoRigUploader(asAsset){ openSheetUploader(!!asAsset, false); }
function startDirectModelUpload(asAsset){ openSheetUploader(!!asAsset, false); }


/* ---------- puppet renderer (used on the animation stage) ---------- */
// Base keys that are a small facial feature positioned BY HAND inside their parent part's own
// artwork (see the nestDx/nestDy comment in edCompile) rather than riding a bone of their own.
// Their alt texture (mouth_open) is already skipped below via SHEET_ALT_OF, so only the base
// key needs to be named here.
const SHEET_FACE_NESTED = ['mouth_closed'];
// Returns the compiled transform needed to draw `key` inside its parent part. Fresh model
// saves (edCompile) write the exact runtime transform and pivot into sh.draw[key]; treat that
// compiled record as canonical so canvas rendering uses exactly what was saved at Done.
// The editor snapshot is a migration fallback for older assets that predate compiled nesting.
function sheetNestInfo(sh,key){
  const part=sh&&sh.parts&&sh.parts[key],d=sh&&sh.draw&&sh.draw[key];
  const stx=sh&&sh.edit&&sh.edit.st,hp=stx&&stx.head,kp=stx&&stx[key];
  const partPivotX=Number(part&&part.dpx)||0,partPivotY=Number(part&&part.dpy)||0;
  const drawPivotX=d&&Number.isFinite(Number(d.pvx))?Number(d.pvx):partPivotX;
  const drawPivotY=d&&Number.isFinite(Number(d.pvy))?Number(d.pvy):partPivotY;
  if(d&&Number.isFinite(Number(d.nestDx))&&Number.isFinite(Number(d.nestDy))){
    return {...d,nestPivotX:drawPivotX,nestPivotY:drawPivotY};
  }
  if(hp&&kp){
    const pivotX=Number.isFinite(Number(kp.pvx))?Number(kp.pvx):drawPivotX;
    const pivotY=Number.isFinite(Number(kp.pvy))?Number(kp.pvy):drawPivotY;
    return {
      nestDx:Number(kp.ax)-Number(hp.pvx||0),
      nestDy:Number(kp.ay)-Number(hp.pvy||0),
      nestRot:(Number(kp.rot)||0)*Math.PI/180,
      nestSx:(Number(kp.s)||1)*(Number(kp.wx)||1)*(Number(kp.fx)||1),
      nestSy:(Number(kp.s)||1)*(Number(kp.hy)||1)*(Number(kp.fy)||1),
      nestPivotX:pivotX,nestPivotY:pivotY
    };
  }
  return null;
}
// Every ordinary part is a rigid image pinned to its own bone start: translate to the joint, rotate by
// how far the bone has turned from rest, then apply the part's own rest rotation / size / flip from the
// editor. The small facial-feature parts in SHEET_FACE_NESTED skip that and instead ride along inside
// their parent part's own transform (captured as parentXform below), offset by the hand-placed spot the
// editor stored for them -- otherwise they'd be drawn at their parent's BONE joint (e.g. the neck) instead
// of wherever on the face they were actually positioned.
function sheetExtraVariant(sh, category, id){
  const list=sh && sh.customVariants && sh.customVariants[category]; if(!Array.isArray(list)||!list.length) return null;
  return id ? (list.find(v=>v.id===id)||null) : null;
}
function sheetLatestHeadPart(sh, kind){
  const list=sh && sh.customVariants && sh.customVariants.headParts; if(!Array.isArray(list)) return null;
  for(let i=list.length-1;i>=0;i--) if(list[i].kind===kind) return list[i];
  return null;
}
function sheetRuntimeHeadVariant(sh, expr){
  if(!sh || !expr) return null;
  // A mood/custom expression stays as the base head during a blink; the renderer overlays
  // the eyelid pixels only. A neutral face may still use the complete closed-eye head swap.
  if((expr.blink || expr.ambientBlink) && !expr.emo && !expr.customExpressionId){
    const v=sheetLatestHeadPart(sh,'blink-head'); if(v) return v;
  }
  if(expr.customExpressionId){ const v=sheetExtraVariant(sh,'expressions',expr.customExpressionId); if(v) return v; }
  return null;
}
function sheetRuntimeMouthVariant(sh, expr){
  if(!sh || !expr) return null;
  return sheetLatestHeadPart(sh, expr.mouth==='open' ? 'mouth-open' : 'mouth-closed');
}
function sheetRuntimeHandVariant(sh, expr, side){
  if(!sh || !expr) return null;
  const id=side==='L'?expr.customGestureLId:expr.customGestureRId;
  return sheetExtraVariant(sh,'gestures',id);
}
function normalizeLegacySheetMouthLayout(parts, draw, state){
  if(!parts || !draw || !state || !parts.head || !state.head) return false;
  const head=parts.head, headState=state.head;
  let changed=false;
  const near=(value,target,tolerance)=>Number.isFinite(Number(value)) && Math.abs(Number(value)-target)<=tolerance;
  ['mouth_closed','mouth_open','mouth_hold'].forEach(key=>{
    const part=parts[key], mouth=state[key];
    if(!part || !mouth) return;
    const fit=Math.max(.12,Math.min(1,(head.w*.34)/Math.max(1,part.w),(head.h*.18)/Math.max(1,part.h)));
    const oldY=near(mouth.ay,head.h*.72,Math.max(1,head.h*.025));
    const previousDefaultY=near(mouth.ay,head.h*.75,Math.max(1,head.h*.025));
    const priorDefaultY=near(mouth.ay,head.h*.82,Math.max(1,head.h*.025));
    const isOldAutoPlacement=
      near(mouth.ax,head.w*.5,Math.max(1,head.w*.015)) &&
      (oldY || previousDefaultY || priorDefaultY) &&
      (near(mouth.s==null?1:mouth.s,1,.035) || near(mouth.s==null?1:mouth.s,fit,.035)) &&
      near(mouth.wx==null?1:mouth.wx,1,.035) &&
      near(mouth.hy==null?1:mouth.hy,1,.035) &&
      near(mouth.fx==null?1:mouth.fx,1,.035) &&
      near(mouth.fy==null?1:mouth.fy,1,.035) &&
      near(mouth.rot==null?0:mouth.rot,0,.5);
    if(!isOldAutoPlacement) return; // never overwrite a user's hand-adjusted mouth
    mouth.ax=head.w*.5;
    mouth.ay=head.h*.88;
    mouth.s=fit;
    const entry=draw[key]||(draw[key]={pvx:part.dpx||0,pvy:part.dpy||0,rot:0,sx:1,sy:1,ra:0});
    entry.nestDx=mouth.ax-(Number(headState.pvx)||0);
    entry.nestDy=mouth.ay-(Number(headState.pvy)||0);
    entry.nestPivotX=Number.isFinite(Number(mouth.pvx))?Number(mouth.pvx):(Number(part.dpx)||0);
    entry.nestPivotY=Number.isFinite(Number(mouth.pvy))?Number(mouth.pvy):(Number(part.dpy)||0);
    entry.pvx=entry.nestPivotX; entry.pvy=entry.nestPivotY;
    entry.nestRot=(Number(mouth.rot)||0)*Math.PI/180;
    entry.nestSx=(Number(mouth.s)||1)*(Number(mouth.wx)||1)*(Number(mouth.fx)||1);
    entry.nestSy=(Number(mouth.s)||1)*(Number(mouth.hy)||1)*(Number(mouth.fy)||1);
    changed=true;
  });
  return changed;
}
function drawSheetCharacter(g, char){
  const sh = char.sheet;
  if(!sh || !sh.parts || !sh.draw || !Array.isArray(sh.order)) return;
  if(Number(sh.v||0)<7){
    if(sh.edit && sh.edit.st) normalizeLegacySheetMouthLayout(sh.parts,sh.draw,sh.edit.st);
    sh.v=7; // one-time lower-face mouth-position migration; never repeats during canvas redraws
  }
  const sheetRig = sheetRigForCharacter(char), B = sheetRig.bones, S = sheetSolveRig(B, sheetRig.baseAngles);
  let L = 0; ['pelvis','spine_lower','spine_upper','chest','neck'].forEach(n=>{ if(B[n]) L += B[n].length; });
  const k = L/Math.max(1e-6, sh.refLen);
  if(!g.__sheetImg) g.__sheetImg = {};
  const getImg = src=>{ let img = g.__sheetImg[src]; if(!img){ img = new Image(); img.src = src; g.__sheetImg[src] = img; } return img; };
  const parentXform = {};   // key -> {x,y,rot,sx,sy} for parts that nested children ride inside of
  sh.order.forEach(key=>{
    if(SHEET_ALT_OF[key] || SHEET_FACE_NESTED.includes(key)) return;   // alt textures & nested face parts are drawn separately below
    const extraHead = key==='head' ? sheetRuntimeHeadVariant(sh,char.expr) : null;
    const extraHand = key==='handL' || key==='handR' ? sheetRuntimeHandVariant(sh,char.expr,key==='handL'?'L':'R') : null;
    const dynamic = extraHead || extraHand;
    const useKey = dynamic ? key : sheetActiveKey(key, char.expr, sh.parts);
    const d = sh.draw[useKey], part = dynamic ? {src:dynamic.src,w:sh.parts[key].w,h:sh.parts[key].h,dpx:sh.parts[key].dpx,dpy:sh.parts[key].dpy} : sh.parts[useKey];
    let sb = SHEET_BONES[useKey];
    if(useKey === 'legL_lower' && sh.parts.shoeL) sb = ['shinL','shinL'];
    if(useKey === 'legR_lower' && sh.parts.shoeR) sb = ['shinR','shinR'];
    const a = sb && S[sb[0]], b = sb && S[sb[1]];
    if(!d || !part || !a || !b) return;
    const img = getImg(part.src);
    if(!img.complete || !img.naturalWidth){ if(!img.__hook){ img.__hook = true; img.addEventListener('load', ()=>{ if(typeof drawStick === 'function') drawStick(); }); } return; }
    const ang = Math.atan2(b.e.y-a.s.y, b.e.x-a.s.x), rot = ang - d.ra + d.rot;
    if(key === 'head') parentXform.head = { x:a.s.x, y:a.s.y, rot, sx:k*d.sx, sy:k*d.sy };
    const mirror = extraHand && key==='handL' && extraHand.side!=='L' ? -1 : 1; // right-hand gestures mirror to the left; left-specific uploads keep their authored orientation
    g.save(); g.translate(a.s.x, a.s.y); g.rotate(rot); g.scale(k*d.sx*mirror, k*d.sy);
    if(dynamic) g.drawImage(img, -d.pvx, -d.pvy, sh.parts[key].w, sh.parts[key].h);
    else g.drawImage(img, -d.pvx, -d.pvy);
    g.restore();
  });
  // Keep eye blinking independent from the full-head emotion swaps. The supplied blink
  // head is the source of truth for eyelid artwork; only the two eye windows are copied,
  // so the sad/angry/shocked/laughing brow and mouth art remains visible underneath.
  if(char.expr && (char.expr.blink || char.expr.ambientBlink || char.expr.eyeState==='closed') && (char.expr.emo || char.expr.customExpressionId) && parentXform.head){
    const blinkPart=sh.parts.head_blink || sheetLatestHeadPart(sh,'blink-head');
    const headKey=sheetActiveKey('head',{...char.expr,blink:false,ambientBlink:false},sh.parts);
    const headPart=sh.parts[headKey] || sh.parts.head, headDraw=sh.draw[headKey] || sh.draw.head;
    if(blinkPart && blinkPart.src && headPart && headDraw){
      const img=getImg(blinkPart.src);
      if(!img.complete || !img.naturalWidth){ if(!img.__hook){img.__hook=true;img.addEventListener('load',()=>{if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();});} }
      else{
        const bw=Number(blinkPart.w)||img.naturalWidth,bh=Number(blinkPart.h)||img.naturalHeight;
        const windows=[{x:.34,y:.565,w:.245,h:.115},{x:.665,y:.565,w:.245,h:.115}];
        const px=parentXform.head;
        g.save();g.translate(px.x,px.y);g.rotate(px.rot);g.scale(px.sx,px.sy);
        windows.forEach(win=>{
          const sx=bw*win.x,sy=bh*win.y,sw=bw*win.w,shh=bh*win.h;
          const dx=-Number(headDraw.pvx||0)+headPart.w*win.x,dy=-Number(headDraw.pvy||0)+headPart.h*win.y;
          g.drawImage(img,sx,sy,sw,shh,dx,dy,headPart.w*win.w,headPart.h*win.h);
        });
        g.restore();
      }
    }
  }
  SHEET_FACE_NESTED.forEach(baseKey=>{
    const pKey = SHEET_PARENT[baseKey], px = parentXform[pKey]; if(!px) return;
    // Every uploaded emotion head shares the same face layout. Keep its own expression
    // art, but allow the independent mouth-open patch to animate over neutral/sad/angry/
    // shocked/laughing heads. Custom one-off full-head expressions stay untouched.
    const activeHeadKey = sheetActiveKey('head', char.expr, sh.parts);
    const supportedHead = ['head','head_sad','head_angry','head_shock','head_laugh'].includes(activeHeadKey);
    if(!supportedHead || (char.expr && char.expr.customExpressionId)) return;
    if(!char.expr || char.expr.mouth !== 'open') return;
    const customMouth = sheetRuntimeMouthVariant(sh,char.expr);
    if(customMouth){
      const img=getImg(customMouth.src), headPart=sh.parts[activeHeadKey] || sh.parts.head;
      if(!img.complete || !img.naturalWidth){ if(!img.__hook){img.__hook=true;img.addEventListener('load',()=>{if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();});} return; }
      const preferredKey = char.expr && char.expr.mouth==='open' && sh.parts.mouth_open ? 'mouth_open' : (sh.parts.mouth_closed ? 'mouth_closed' : (sh.parts.mouth_open ? 'mouth_open' : null));
      const baseMouth = preferredKey && sh.parts[preferredKey];
      const nest = preferredKey && sheetNestInfo(sh, preferredKey);
      const mouthDraw=preferredKey && sh.draw && sh.draw[preferredKey];
      const headDraw=sh.draw && (sh.draw[activeHeadKey] || sh.draw.head);
      const mouthPivotX=nest&&Number.isFinite(Number(nest.nestPivotX))?Number(nest.nestPivotX):(mouthDraw&&Number.isFinite(Number(mouthDraw.pvx))?Number(mouthDraw.pvx):Number(baseMouth&&baseMouth.dpx)||0);
      const mouthPivotY=nest&&Number.isFinite(Number(nest.nestPivotY))?Number(nest.nestPivotY):(mouthDraw&&Number.isFinite(Number(mouthDraw.pvy))?Number(mouthDraw.pvy):Number(baseMouth&&baseMouth.dpy)||0);
      const headPivotX=headDraw&&Number.isFinite(Number(headDraw.pvx))?Number(headDraw.pvx):Number(headPart.dpx)||0;
      const headPivotY=headDraw&&Number.isFinite(Number(headDraw.pvy))?Number(headDraw.pvy):Number(headPart.dpy)||0;
      const looksFullHead = Number(customMouth.w||0) >= headPart.w*.82 && Number(customMouth.h||0) >= headPart.h*.82;
      g.save(); g.translate(px.x,px.y); g.rotate(px.rot); g.scale(px.sx,px.sy);
      if(baseMouth && nest && !looksFullHead){
        // Use the exact editor-authored mouth pivot. Using parts[].dpx/dpy here ignored a
        // changed pivot and made the Talk mouth appear beside the nose after saving.
        g.translate(nest.nestDx,nest.nestDy); g.rotate(nest.nestRot); g.scale(nest.nestSx,nest.nestSy);
        g.drawImage(img,-mouthPivotX,-mouthPivotY,baseMouth.w,baseMouth.h);
      }else if(looksFullHead){
        // Backward compatibility for genuinely full-head transparent expression overlays.
        g.drawImage(img,-headPivotX,-headPivotY,headPart.w,headPart.h);
      }else{
        // Legacy 12/14-part models have no mouth crop to inherit. Place a cropped mouth at a
        // conservative lower-face position, retaining the source aspect ratio.
        const mw=Number(customMouth.w)||img.naturalWidth, mh=Number(customMouth.h)||img.naturalHeight;
        const scale=Math.min(headPart.w*.34/Math.max(1,mw),headPart.h*.18/Math.max(1,mh));
        const dw=mw*scale, dh=mh*scale;
        g.drawImage(img,-headPivotX+headPart.w*.5-dw*.5,-headPivotY+headPart.h*.88-dh*.5,dw,dh);
      }
      g.restore(); return;
    }
    const useKey = sheetActiveKey(baseKey, char.expr, sh.parts), part = sh.parts[useKey];
    const nest = part && sheetNestInfo(sh, useKey); if(!part || !nest) return;
    const img = getImg(part.src);
    if(!img.complete || !img.naturalWidth){ if(!img.__hook){ img.__hook = true; img.addEventListener('load', ()=>{ if(typeof drawStick === 'function') drawStick(); }); } return; }
    g.save(); g.translate(px.x, px.y); g.rotate(px.rot); g.scale(px.sx, px.sy);
    g.translate(nest.nestDx,nest.nestDy); g.rotate(nest.nestRot); g.scale(nest.nestSx,nest.nestSy);
    const mouthPivotX=Number.isFinite(Number(nest.nestPivotX))?Number(nest.nestPivotX):Number((sh.draw[useKey]||{}).pvx??part.dpx)||0;
    const mouthPivotY=Number.isFinite(Number(nest.nestPivotY))?Number(nest.nestPivotY):Number((sh.draw[useKey]||{}).pvy??part.dpy)||0;
    g.drawImage(img,-mouthPivotX,-mouthPivotY); g.restore();
  });
  // Do not paint oval joint patches over source art: they create the unwanted cross/target marks at wrists and knees.
}
// Joint-bridge helper retained for compatibility with old saved models. The renderer no longer
// invokes these oval patches because they can appear as crosshair/crescent artifacts over imported art.
// Legacy runtime oval-joint blend helpers removed to preserve imported artwork at joins.
// Ambient eye-blinking: independent of whichever animation preset is playing (or none at all), each
// custom character with a head_blink part occasionally swaps to it for a brief moment. Characters
// without that part are untouched forever -- the Math.random() check never even fires for them.
function edScheduleBlinks(){
  const tick = ()=>{
    characters.forEach(c=>{
      // Idle blinks use their own flag (expr.ambientBlink), so they never fight the Talk + Blink clip (expr.blink).
      if(c.type === 'custom' && c.sheet && c.sheet.parts && (c.sheet.parts.head_blink || sheetLatestHeadPart(c.sheet,'blink-head')) && !(c.expr && c.expr.disableAmbientBlink)){
        if(!c.expr) c.expr = sheetExprFresh();
        if(!c.expr.ambientBlink && Math.random() < 0.55){
          c.expr.ambientBlink = true; drawStick();
          setTimeout(()=>{ c.expr.ambientBlink = false; drawStick(); }, 110+Math.random()*80);
        }
      }
    });
    setTimeout(tick, 2200+Math.random()*2600);
  };
  setTimeout(tick, 1500+Math.random()*1500);
}
function updateModelAssetFromChar(c){
  if(!c || !c.sheet || !c.assetId) return;
  let a = modelAssets.find(x=>String(x.id) === String(c.assetId));
  // A character may originate from an admin-published model. Promote that asset to the local
  // editable library before saving, so a character-side edit is not silently discarded.
  if(!a && Array.isArray(publishedModelAssets)){
    const published = publishedModelAssets.find(x=>String(x.id) === String(c.assetId));
    if(published){ a=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(published)); delete a.origin; modelAssets.push(a); }
  }
  if(!a) return;
  a.name = c.name || a.name;
  a.updatedAt = Date.now();
  a.sheet = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(c.sheet));
  a.rigOverride = undefined; a.parts = {};
  a.thumb = c.thumb || c.sheet.thumb;
  a.previewThumb = undefined; // the model was edited; the cached full-body preview is now stale
  a.modelType = c.modelType || (c.sheet && c.sheet.modelType) || a.modelType || 'humanoid';
  saveModelAssetsToStorage();
}

// Initialize only the editor's icons and optional ambient blink loop; do not boot a second lab scene or overwrite characters.
(function initEthvelleSheetEditor(){
  const ready = ()=>{ edFillIcons(); edScheduleBlinks(); };
  if(document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', ready, {once:true});
  else ready();
})();
</script>
<script>
/* Ethvelle 21-bone animation refresh, based on the uploaded 14-part Model Lab reference. */
const ETHVELLE_LEGACY_POSE_KEYS_21 = ['spine','armL','armL_lo','armR','armR_lo','legL','legL_lo','legR','legR_lo'];
function ethvelleNormalizeLegacyPoseTo21(p){
  if(!p) return {};
  if(!ETHVELLE_LEGACY_POSE_KEYS_21.some(k=>k in p)) return {...p};
  const o={}, g=k=>Number(p[k])||0, s=g('spine');
  Object.keys(p).forEach(k=>{if(!ETHVELLE_LEGACY_POSE_KEYS_21.includes(k))o[k]=p[k];});
  if('spine' in p){o.spine_lower=(o.spine_lower||0)+s*.4;o.spine_upper=(o.spine_upper||0)+s*.35;o.chest=(o.chest||0)+s*.25;}
  if('head' in p)o.head=(Number(p.head)||0)-s;
  ['L','R'].forEach(S=>{
    const a='arm'+S,al='arm'+S+'_lo',l='leg'+S,ll='leg'+S+'_lo';
    if(a in p||al in p){const u=g(a);o['upperArm'+S]=u-s;o['forearm'+S]=(al in p?g(al):u)-u;}
    if(l in p||ll in p){const u=g(l);o['thigh'+S]=u;o['shin'+S]=(ll in p?g(ll):u)-u;}
  });
  return o;
}
const ethvellePhaseShift21 = p => { const o={}; Object.keys(p).forEach(k=>{ const m=k.match(/^(.*)([LR])$/);
  if(m) o[m[1]+(m[2]==='L'?'R':'L')] = /^clavicle/.test(k) ? -p[k] : p[k]; else o[k] = k==='hipY' ? p[k] : -p[k]; }); return o; };
const ethvelleScalePose21 = (p,f) => { const o={}; Object.keys(p).forEach(k=>o[k]=p[k]*f); return o; };
const ETHVELLE_WALK_A = { pelvis:-0.8*D, spine_lower:1.0*D, spine_upper:2.0*D, chest:2.6*D, neck:-0.8*D, head:-1.2*D,
  // Keep the existing 3/4-body angle. The left arm swings beside the body with a bent elbow,
  // rather than reaching far enough forward to cross the torso.
  clavicleL:2*D, clavicleR:0.8*D, upperArmL:26*D, upperArmR:-4*D, forearmL:-24*D, forearmR:2*D, handL:8*D, handR:0,
  // More visible left knee/toe action makes the planted foot and toe-lift phases read clearly.
  thighL:24*D, thighR:-6*D, shinL:-18*D, shinR:4*D, footL:-11*D, footR:2*D, hipY:-0.012 };
// The second half-cycle stays asymmetric too: the LEFT arm/leg has the larger swing,
// while the RIGHT arm/leg keeps restrained movement. A smaller left-arm reach prevents
// the hand from cutting across or brushing the chest on a 3/4-facing character.
const ETHVELLE_WALK_B = { pelvis:0.4*D, spine_lower:-0.6*D, spine_upper:1.5*D, chest:2.0*D, neck:-0.3*D, head:-0.9*D,
  clavicleL:1.8*D, clavicleR:0.5*D, upperArmL:-10*D, upperArmR:3*D, forearmL:-8*D, forearmR:-1*D, handL:-5*D, handR:0,
  thighL:-20*D, thighR:5*D, shinL:15*D, shinR:-3*D, footL:10*D, footR:-2*D, hipY:-0.022 };
// Passing poses retain the staggered step and gentle 3/4-body lean, with the left elbow
// still flexed so the hand stays clear of the torso between the two stride poses.
const ETHVELLE_WALK_PASS = { pelvis:0, spine_lower:0.1*D, spine_upper:1.0*D, chest:1.3*D, neck:-0.2*D, head:-0.4*D,
  clavicleL:1*D, clavicleR:0.4*D, upperArmL:10*D, upperArmR:-1*D, forearmL:-18*D, forearmR:1*D, handL:4*D, handR:0,
  thighL:6*D, thighR:-2*D, shinL:-7*D, shinR:2*D, footL:-4*D, footR:0, hipY:-0.009 };
const ETHVELLE_WALK_PASS_B = { pelvis:0.1*D, spine_lower:-0.1*D, spine_upper:0.9*D, chest:1.1*D, neck:-0.1*D, head:-0.3*D,
  clavicleL:0.9*D, clavicleR:0.3*D, upperArmL:-3*D, upperArmR:1*D, forearmL:-12*D, forearmR:-0.5*D, handL:-3*D, handR:0,
  thighL:-5*D, thighR:2*D, shinL:7*D, shinR:-2*D, footL:6*D, footR:0, hipY:-0.016 };
// Steady "holding something at waist height with both hands" arm pose used by the walkHold preset below.

/* ---------------- Front-view presets + grouping ----------------
   A front-facing character has mirror-image arms and legs. So each front preset is written for the LEFT
   limb only and ethvelleFrontPair21() mirrors it to the right: a right-side angle is the negative of the left one
   (the same motion reflected across the body). Centre bones (pelvis, spine, neck, head) are written as-is. */
const ethvelleFrontPair21 = L => { const o = {}; Object.keys(L).forEach(k => { if(/L$/.test(k)){ o[k] = L[k]; o[k.slice(0,-1)+'R'] = -L[k]; } else o[k] = L[k]; }); return o; };
const ETHVELLE_FRONT_WALK_A = ethvelleFrontPair21({
  // A restrained front-facing in-place gait. Keep the swing inside each shoulder/hip lane so
  // the hands and knees do not cut across the torso when the animation loops.
  pelvis:0.8*D, spine_lower:0.5*D, spine_upper:-0.5*D, head:-0.4*D,
  clavicleL:1*D, upperArmL:10*D, forearmL:-3*D, handL:1*D,
  thighL:10*D, shinL:-8*D, footL:3*D, hipY:0
});
const ETHVELLE_FRONT_WALK_PASS = ethvelleFrontPair21({
  pelvis:-0.3*D, spine_lower:0.2*D, spine_upper:-0.2*D, head:0,
  clavicleL:0.5*D, upperArmL:3*D, forearmL:-1*D, handL:0,
  thighL:2*D, shinL:-4*D, footL:0, hipY:-0.008
});

/* ---------------- Back-view presets ----------------

// Merge the reference library over the previous 9/11-bone versions. These keys are used by
// the existing animation picker, clip timeline, preview player and export renderer.
Object.assign(animPresets, ethvelleUpdatedPresets);
Object.keys(ethvelleUpdatedPresets).forEach(key=>{
  const preset=animPresets[key];
  if(!preset || !Array.isArray(preset.keyframes)) return;
  preset._ethvelleRigVersion=21;
  preset.keyframes.forEach(kf=>{kf.pose=ethvelleNormalizeLegacyPoseTo21(kf.pose||{});});
});

function ethvelleIs21Pose(p){
  return !!p && ['pelvis','spine_lower','spine_upper','chest','neck','clavicleL','clavicleR','upperArmL','upperArmR','forearmL','forearmR','thighL','thighR','shinL','shinR','footL','footR'].some(k=>Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(p,k));
}
function ethvelleDiscrete21(preset,tFrac,field){
  const kfs=preset&&preset.keyframes;if(!Array.isArray(kfs))return undefined;
  let val;
  for(let i=0;i<kfs.length;i++)if(kfs[i].t<=tFrac&&kfs[i][field]!==undefined)val=kfs[i][field];
  return val;
}
function ethvelleComplete21Pose(preset,tFrac,pose){
  const p=ethvelleNormalizeLegacyPoseTo21(pose||{});
  const fist=ethvelleDiscrete21(preset,tFrac,'fist');
  if(fist!==undefined)p.gestureFist=fist?1:0;
  else p.gestureFist=Number(p.gestureFist)||0;
  p.gesturePoint=Number(p.gesturePoint)||0;
  p.gesturePeace=Number(p.gesturePeace)||0;
  p.gestureThumbsUp=Number(p.gestureThumbsUp)||0;
  const mouth=ethvelleDiscrete21(preset,tFrac,'mouth');
  const blink=ethvelleDiscrete21(preset,tFrac,'blink');
  p.__mouth=mouth===undefined?'closed':mouth;
  p.__blink=blink===undefined?false:!!blink;
  return p;
}

// Updated presets authored for a parent-relative 21-bone rig also need a compatibility pose
// for the original stickman and saved legacy rigs. The sheet renderer uses the full pose below.
function ethvelle21PoseToLegacy(p){
  const out={}, skeleton=new Set(['pelvis','spine_lower','spine_upper','chest','neck','head','clavicleL','clavicleR','upperArmL','upperArmR','forearmL','forearmR','handL','handR','thighL','thighR','shinL','shinR','footL','footR','hipY']);
  Object.keys(p||{}).forEach(k=>{if(!skeleton.has(k)&&!k.startsWith('__ethvelle'))out[k]=p[k];});
  const spine=(Number(p.spine_lower)||0)+(Number(p.spine_upper)||0)+(Number(p.chest)||0);
  out.spine=spine;
  out.head=spine+(Number(p.neck)||0)+(Number(p.head)||0);
  ['L','R'].forEach(S=>{
    const arm=(Number(p['clavicle'+S])||0)+(Number(p['upperArm'+S])||0)+spine;
    out['arm'+S]=arm;
    out['arm'+S+'_lo']=arm+(Number(p['forearm'+S])||0);
    out['hand'+S]=out['arm'+S+'_lo']+(Number(p['hand'+S])||0);
    const leg=(Number(p.pelvis)||0)+(Number(p['thigh'+S])||0);
    out['leg'+S]=leg;
    out['leg'+S+'_lo']=leg+(Number(p['shin'+S])||0)+(Number(p['foot'+S])||0);
  });
  if(p.hipY!==undefined)out.hipY=p.hipY;
  return out;
}

const ethvelleOriginalEvalPreset21 = evalPreset;
evalPreset = function(preset,tFrac){
  const pose=ethvelleOriginalEvalPreset21(preset,tFrac);
  if(preset&&preset._ethvelleRigVersion===21){
    const modern=ethvelleComplete21Pose(preset,tFrac,pose);
    Object.defineProperty(pose,'__ethvelleModernPose',{value:modern,writable:true,configurable:true,enumerable:false});
  }
  return pose;
};

function ethvelleModernPoseForClipAtTime(tSecAbsolute,clipOverride,segmentsOverride){
  const useClip=clipOverride!==undefined?clipOverride:activeClip;
  const useSegments=segmentsOverride!==undefined?segmentsOverride:splitStickmanSegments;
  if(!useClip)return null;
  const hit=stickmanClipAt(tSecAbsolute,useClip,useSegments);if(!hit)return null;
  const clip=hit.clip,preset=animPresets[clip.animKey];
  if(!preset||preset._ethvelleRigVersion!==21)return null;
  const speed=clip.speed||1, localT=Math.max(0,(tSecAbsolute-clip.trimStart)*speed);
  const loopT=(localT%preset.duration)/preset.duration;
  let pose=ethvelleComplete21Pose(preset,loopT,ethvelleOriginalEvalPreset21(preset,loopT));
  if(Array.isArray(clip.keyframes)&&clip.keyframes.length){
    const kfs=[...clip.keyframes].sort((a,b)=>a.t-b.t), WINDOW=.36;
    const holdAndBlend=(kf,base)=>{
      const dist=Math.abs(tSecAbsolute-kf.t);if(dist>=WINDOW)return null;
      const weight=.5*(1+Math.cos(Math.PI*(dist/WINDOW)));
      const kp=ethvelleNormalizeLegacyPoseTo21(kf.pose||{}), blended={};
      const keys=new Set([...Object.keys(base),...Object.keys(kp)]);
      keys.forEach(k=>{
        if(k.startsWith('__'))return;
        const bv=base[k]??0;
        blended[k]=lerp(bv,kp[k]??bv,weight);
      });
      return {...base,...blended};
    };
    if(tSecAbsolute<=kfs[0].t)pose=holdAndBlend(kfs[0],pose)||pose;
    else if(tSecAbsolute>=kfs[kfs.length-1].t)pose=holdAndBlend(kfs[kfs.length-1],pose)||pose;
    else{
      let i=0;while(i<kfs.length-1&&tSecAbsolute>kfs[i+1].t)i++;
      const a=kfs[i],b=kfs[i+1],near=Math.abs(tSecAbsolute-a.t)<=Math.abs(tSecAbsolute-b.t)?a:b,far=near===a?b:a;
      pose=holdAndBlend(near,pose)||holdAndBlend(far,pose)||pose;
    }
    // Preserve preset-level discrete expression fields through the user keyframe blend.
    const mouth=ethvelleDiscrete21(preset,loopT,'mouth'),blink=ethvelleDiscrete21(preset,loopT,'blink'),fist=ethvelleDiscrete21(preset,loopT,'fist');
    pose.__mouth=mouth===undefined?'closed':mouth;pose.__blink=blink===undefined?false:!!blink;
    pose.gestureFist=fist===undefined?(Number(pose.gestureFist)||0):(fist?1:0);
  }
  return pose;
}
const ethvelleOriginalPoseAtClipTime21=poseAtClipTime;
poseAtClipTime=function(tSecAbsolute,clipOverride,segmentsOverride){
  const pose=ethvelleOriginalPoseAtClipTime21(tSecAbsolute,clipOverride,segmentsOverride);
  if(!pose)return pose;
  const modern=ethvelleModernPoseForClipAtTime(tSecAbsolute,clipOverride,segmentsOverride);
  if(modern)Object.defineProperty(pose,'__ethvelleModernPose',{value:modern,writable:true,configurable:true,enumerable:false});
  return pose;
};

const ethvelleOriginalApplyPoseToBones21=applyPoseToBones;
applyPoseToBones=function(pose,targetBones,targetBaseAngles,targetHipRestY){
  const b=targetBones!==undefined?targetBones:bones;
  const character=(Array.isArray(characters)?characters.find(c=>c&&c.bones===b):null)||(b===bones&&typeof activeCharacter==='function'?activeCharacter():null);
  const source=pose||{};
  const modern=source.__ethvelleModernPose||(ethvelleIs21Pose(source)?source:null);
  const legacy=modern?ethvelle21PoseToLegacy(modern):source;
  if(character){
    character._ethvelleModernPose=(character.sheet&&modern)?{...modern}:null;
    if(character.sheet){
      if(!character.expr&&typeof sheetExprFresh==='function')character.expr=sheetExprFresh();
      if(character.expr){
        if(modern){
          character.expr.mouth=modern.__mouth||'closed';
          character.expr.blink=!!modern.__blink;
          const fist=Number(modern.gestureFist)||0,point=Number(modern.gesturePoint)||0,peace=Number(modern.gesturePeace)||0,thumb=Number(modern.gestureThumbsUp)||0;
          character.expr.fistL=character.expr.fistR=fist>.5;
          character.expr.gestL=character.expr.gestR=fist>.5?null:(point>.5?'point':(thumb>.5?'thumb':(peace>.5?'palm':null)));
        }else if(['gestureFist','gesturePoint','gesturePeace','gestureThumbsUp'].some(k=>k in source)){
          const fist=Number(source.gestureFist)||0,point=Number(source.gesturePoint)||0,peace=Number(source.gesturePeace)||0,thumb=Number(source.gestureThumbsUp)||0;
          character.expr.fistL=character.expr.fistR=fist>.5;
          character.expr.gestL=character.expr.gestR=fist>.5?null:(point>.5?'point':(thumb>.5?'thumb':(peace>.5?'palm':null)));
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return ethvelleOriginalApplyPoseToBones21(legacy,targetBones,targetBaseAngles,targetHipRestY);
};

// Apply the full parent-relative pose directly to the 21-bone runtime used by 14-part sheets.
const ethvelleOriginalSheetRigForCharacter21=sheetRigForCharacter;
sheetRigForCharacter=function(char){
  const rig=ethvelleOriginalSheetRigForCharacter21(char);
  const p=char&&char._ethvelleModernPose;
  if(!p||!rig||!rig.bones||!rig.baseAngles)return rig;
  const clamp=(v,lo,hi)=>Math.max(lo,Math.min(hi,v));
  const deg=Math.PI/180;
  const limits={pelvis:28,spine_lower:28,spine_upper:28,chest:30,neck:35,head:42,clavicleL:28,clavicleR:28,upperArmL:165,upperArmR:165,forearmL:145,forearmR:145,handL:95,handR:95,thighL:125,thighR:125,shinL:145,shinR:145,footL:85,footR:85};
  Object.keys(rig.bones).forEach(k=>{
    if(k==='root'||rig.baseAngles[k]===undefined)return;
    const limit=(limits[k]||165)*deg,delta=clamp(Number(p[k])||0,-limit,limit);
    rig.bones[k].angle=rig.baseAngles[k]+delta;
  });
  return rig;
};

const ethvelleOriginalResetTimelineFrame21=resetCharacterForTimelineFrame;
resetCharacterForTimelineFrame=function(char){if(char)char._ethvelleModernPose=null;return ethvelleOriginalResetTimelineFrame21(char);};
const ethvelleOriginalResetBonesToRest21=resetCharacterBonesToRest;
resetCharacterBonesToRest=function(char){if(char)char._ethvelleModernPose=null;return ethvelleOriginalResetBonesToRest21(char);};
const ethvelleOriginalCancelPreview21=cancelAnimationPreview;
cancelAnimationPreview=function(redraw){
  const oldId=previewCharacterId;
  ethvelleOriginalCancelPreview21(redraw);
  const c=oldId&&Array.isArray(characters)?characters.find(ch=>ch&&ch.id===oldId):activeCharacter();
  if(c&&c.sheet){c._ethvelleModernPose=null;if(c.expr){c.expr.mouth='closed';c.expr.blink=false;c.expr.fistL=false;c.expr.fistR=false;c.expr.gestL=null;c.expr.gestR=null;}}
  if(redraw!==false&&c&&c.sheet&&typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();
};

// The sheet renderer's expression data is authoritative for imported cut-out faces; this keeps
// the legacy face overlay's mouth animation in sync for older custom rigs too.
try{
  drawEthvelleFaceMotion = (function(original){
    return function(ctx,char,start,end){return original(ctx,char,start,end);};
  })(drawEthvelleFaceMotion);
}catch(_e){}
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-text-shadow-owner">
/* Single owner of how a text shadow is painted on a text glyph layer and on the composer preview.
   A glyph filled with a gradient/texture uses background-clip:text with a transparent text fill. A CSS
   text-shadow on that same element is painted OVER the clipped background, so the letters turned into a
   shadow-coloured silhouette. For a filled glyph the shadow is therefore drawn with filter:drop-shadow(),
   which is built from the finished gradient/texture letters (bend, perspective and stroke included) and is
   always painted behind them. Plain-colour text keeps the normal text-shadow exactly as before.
   Every writer passes the same shadow string it always built; only the way it is painted differs. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  window.ethvelleApplyTextShadow=function(el,shadow,hasFill,important){
    if(!el||!el.style)return;
    var css=(shadow==null||shadow===''||shadow==='none')?'none':String(shadow);
    var pr=important?'important':'';
    if(hasFill&&css!=='none'){
      el.style.setProperty('text-shadow','none',pr);
      el.style.setProperty('filter','drop-shadow('+css+')',pr);
      if(el.dataset.evShadowFilter!=='1')el.dataset.evShadowFilter='1';
    }else{
      el.style.setProperty('text-shadow',css,pr);
      if(el.dataset.evShadowFilter==='1'){el.style.removeProperty('filter');delete el.dataset.evShadowFilter;}
    }
  };
})();
</script>

  function getSelected(){try{return typeof selectedTextOverlay==='function'?selectedTextOverlay():null}catch(_){return null}}
  function currentTarget(){return root&&root.classList.contains('active')&&draft?draft:getSelected()}
  function safeHex(hex){return /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(String(hex||''))?String(hex):'#FFFFFF'}
  function alphaKeyFor(key){return key==='textColor'?'textOpacity':key==='strokeColor'?'strokeOpacity':key==='shadowColor'?'shadowOpacity':key==='textBackgroundColor'?'textBackgroundOpacity':null;}
  function targetColor(){const t=currentTarget();return t?safeHex(t[activeColorKey]||'#FFFFFF'):'#FFFFFF';}
  function hslToHex(h,s,l){s/=100;l/=100;const k=n=>(n+h/30)%12,a=s*Math.min(l,1-l),f=n=>{const c=k(n);return l-a*Math.max(-1,Math.min(c-3,Math.min(9-c,1)));};return '#'+[f(0),f(8),f(4)].map(v=>Math.round(v*255).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();}
  function hexToHsl(hex){const h=safeHex(hex).slice(1),r=parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16)/255,g=parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16)/255,b=parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16)/255,max=Math.max(r,g,b),min=Math.min(r,g,b),d=max-min;let hue=0,sat=0,light=(max+min)/2;if(d){sat=d/(1-Math.abs(2*light-1));switch(max){case r:hue=((g-b)/d)%6;break;case g:hue=(b-r)/d+2;break;default:hue=(r-g)/d+4;}hue*=60;if(hue<0)hue+=360;}return {h:hue,s:sat*100,l:light*100};}

  window.ethvelleHexToRgba=function(hex,alpha){
    const h=safeHex(hex).slice(1);const r=parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16),g=parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16),b=parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16);
    return `rgba(${r}, ${g}, ${b}, ${Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(alpha)||0))})`;
  };
  function copyStyle(ov){
    const o=ov||{};
    return {editingId:ov?ov.id:null,textContent:o.textContent||'',fontFamily:o.fontFamily||(typeof pendingAdminFontFamily!=='undefined'&&pendingAdminFontFamily)||'Inter',fontSize:Math.max(12,Math.min(120,Number(o.fontSize)||44)),fontWeight:Number(o.fontWeight)||800,fontStyle:o.fontStyle||'normal',textAlign:o.textAlign||'center',textColor:o.textColor||'#FFFFFF',textOpacity:Number(o.textOpacity==null?100:o.textOpacity),strokeWidth:Math.max(0,Number(o.strokeWidth)||0),strokeColor:o.strokeColor||'#16162A',strokeOpacity:Number(o.strokeOpacity==null?100:o.strokeOpacity),shadowEnabled:!!o.shadowEnabled,shadowColor:o.shadowColor||'#000000',shadowOpacity:Number(o.shadowOpacity==null?100:o.shadowOpacity),shadowBlur:Math.max(0,Number(o.shadowBlur)||6),shadowX:Number(o.shadowX)||0,shadowY:Math.max(4,Number(o.shadowY)||6),textBackgroundEnabled:!!o.textBackgroundEnabled,textBackgroundColor:o.textBackgroundColor||'#182A66',textBackgroundOpacity:Number(o.textBackgroundOpacity==null?78:o.textBackgroundOpacity),textBackgroundPadding:Number(o.textBackgroundPadding==null?8:o.textBackgroundPadding),textBackgroundRadius:Number(o.textBackgroundRadius==null?8:o.textBackgroundRadius),textBackgroundShape:o.textBackgroundShape||'rounded',transform:o.transform?Object.assign({},o.transform):{scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0}};
  }
  function ensureRoot(){

  function syncLayout(){
    if(!root||!root.classList.contains('active'))return;
    const input=document.getElementById('evtcStageText');const stage=document.getElementById('canvasStage');
    const vv=window.visualViewport;
    // Once the user starts typing, Android may shrink the visual viewport for the keyboard.
    // Keep the full-screen composer and the writing field at their original geometry instead
    // of repeatedly recalculating the canvas/text size on every keyboard resize/input event.
    const activelyTyping=!!input&&document.activeElement===input;
    // Track the visible viewport even while typing: Android shrinks visualViewport when
    // the soft keyboard opens, so the bottom text palette remains floating above it.
    if(vv){root.style.top=vv.offsetTop+'px';root.style.height=vv.height+'px';root.style.bottom='auto';}
    else {root.style.top='0px';root.style.height='100dvh';root.style.bottom='auto';}
    if(stage&&input&&!activelyTyping){
      const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect();
      // Normally this is the live canvas stage. If the editor is still completing its first
      // layout (or mobile keyboard resize briefly reports a zero-size stage), keep the input
      // centered and fully usable instead of collapsing it to a tiny single-character field.
      const wrap=document.querySelector('.canvas-wrap');const wr=wrap?wrap.getBoundingClientRect():{width:0,height:0};
      const fallbackW=Math.min(Math.max(240,wr.width||0),Math.max(240,window.innerWidth*.92));
      const w=r.width>80?r.width:fallbackW;
      const h=r.height>80?r.height:Math.min(window.innerHeight*.44,w*9/16);
      const left=r.width>80?r.left:(window.innerWidth-w)/2;
      const top=r.height>80?r.top:Math.max(82,Math.min(window.innerHeight*.34,window.innerHeight*.28));
      input.style.left=Math.max(0,left+w*.045)+'px';
      input.style.top=(top+h*.20)+'px';
      input.style.width=Math.max(150,w*.91)+'px';
      input.style.height=Math.max(76,h*.60)+'px';
      input.style.fontSize=Math.max(22,Math.min(56,Number(draft&&draft.fontSize)||44,w*.095))+'px';
    }
  }
  function installLayoutListeners(){
    window.addEventListener('resize',syncLayout,{passive:true});
    if(window.visualViewport)window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',syncLayout,{passive:true});
    if(window.visualViewport)window.visualViewport.addEventListener('scroll',syncLayout,{passive:true});
  }
  installLayoutListeners();
  window.openEthvelleTextComposer=function(id){
    ensureRoot();
    const ov=(id?overlays.find(o=>o.id===id&&o.type==='text'):getSelected())||null;
    draft=copyStyle(ov);
    if(!draft.fontFamily)draft.fontFamily='Inter';
