    const ed=document.getElementById('screen-editor');if(ed)ed.classList.add('ev-text-backdrop-blur');
    document.body.classList.add('evtc-is-open');
    const input=document.getElementById('evtcStageText');
    input.value=draft.textContent||'';input.style.color=window.ethvelleHexToRgba(draft.textColor,Number(draft.textOpacity==null?100:draft.textOpacity)/100);input.style.fontFamily='"'+draft.fontFamily+'",sans-serif';input.style.fontWeight=String(draft.fontWeight||800);input.style.fontStyle=draft.fontStyle||'normal';input.style.textAlign=draft.textAlign||'center';
    input.style.webkitTextStroke=draft.strokeWidth>0?`${draft.strokeWidth}px ${window.ethvelleHexToRgba(draft.strokeColor,Number(draft.strokeOpacity==null?100:draft.strokeOpacity)/100)}`:'0 transparent';
    input.style.textShadow=draft.shadowEnabled?`${Number(draft.shadowX)||0}px ${Math.max(4,Number(draft.shadowY)||6)}px ${Math.max(0,Number(draft.shadowBlur)||0)}px ${window.ethvelleHexToRgba(draft.shadowColor,Number(draft.shadowOpacity==null?100:draft.shadowOpacity)/100)}`:'none';
    input.style.backgroundColor='transparent';
    input.style.padding=draft.textBackgroundEnabled?draft.textBackgroundPadding+'px':'0';input.style.borderRadius=draft.textBackgroundRadius+'px';
    const op=document.getElementById('evtcTextOpacity'),opv=document.getElementById('evtcTextOpacityValue');if(op)op.value=Number(draft.textOpacity==null?100:draft.textOpacity);if(opv)opv.textContent=Math.round(Number(draft.textOpacity==null?100:draft.textOpacity))+'%';
    document.querySelectorAll('#evtcColorStrip .evtc-color-swatch').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',String(b.dataset.color).toLowerCase()===String(draft.textColor||'#FFFFFF').toLowerCase()));
    syncLayout();syncComposerBackplate();
    setTimeout(()=>{syncLayout();syncComposerBackplate();/* Do not auto-focus here: opening the panel should not summon the mobile keyboard or shift the canvas. The user can tap the text field when ready to type. */},80);
  };
  window.closeEthvelleTextComposer=function(save){
    if(root){root.classList.remove('active');root.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}
    const ed=document.getElementById('screen-editor');if(ed)ed.classList.remove('ev-text-backdrop-blur');
    document.body.classList.remove('evtc-is-open');
    if(styleRoot&&styleRoot.classList.contains('evts-compose-subpanel'))closeEthvelleTextStylePanel();
    draft=null;
    if(typeof syncBottomToolsMode==='function')syncBottomToolsMode();
  };
  window.updateEthvelleComposerText=function(){if(!draft)return;const i=document.getElementById('evtcStageText');draft.textContent=i?i.value:'';fitComposerText();};
  function syncComposerBackplate(){
    const input=document.getElementById('evtcStageText'),plate=document.getElementById('evtcStageBackplate'),t=draft;
    if(!input||!plate||!t){if(plate)plate.style.display='none';return;}
    const active=!!t.textBackgroundEnabled;
    if(!active){plate.style.display='none';return;}
    const r=input.getBoundingClientRect();
    plate.style.display='block';plate.style.left=r.left+'px';plate.style.top=r.top+'px';plate.style.width=r.width+'px';plate.style.height=r.height+'px';
    plate.style.backgroundColor=window.ethvelleHexToRgba(t.textBackgroundColor||'#182A66',1);
    plate.style.borderRadius=(Number(t.textBackgroundRadius)||8)+'px';
    const shape=String(t.textBackgroundShape||'rounded');
    const mask=window.ethvelleTextBgMaskCss?window.ethvelleTextBgMaskCss(shape):'none';
    plate.style.webkitMaskImage=mask;plate.style.maskImage=mask;plate.style.webkitMaskSize='100% 100%';plate.style.maskSize='100% 100%';plate.style.webkitMaskRepeat='no-repeat';plate.style.maskRepeat='no-repeat';
    plate.style.opacity=String(Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(t.textBackgroundOpacity==null?78:t.textBackgroundOpacity)))/100);
  }
  function fitComposerText(){
    if(!draft)return;const i=document.getElementById('evtcStageText');if(!i)return;
    const stage=document.getElementById('canvasStage');const r=stage?stage.getBoundingClientRect():{width:window.innerWidth};
    i.style.fontSize=Math.max(22,Math.min(56,Number(draft.fontSize)||44))+'px';i.style.color=window.ethvelleHexToRgba(draft.textColor||'#FFFFFF',Number(draft.textOpacity==null?100:draft.textOpacity)/100);i.style.fontFamily='"'+(draft.fontFamily||'Inter')+'",sans-serif';i.style.fontWeight=String(draft.fontWeight||800);i.style.fontStyle=draft.fontStyle||'normal';i.style.textAlign=draft.textAlign||'center';
    i.style.webkitTextStroke=Number(draft.strokeWidth)>0?`${draft.strokeWidth}px ${window.ethvelleHexToRgba(draft.strokeColor||'#16162A',Number(draft.strokeOpacity==null?100:draft.strokeOpacity)/100)}`:'0 transparent';
    window.ethvelleApplyTextShadow(i,draft.shadowEnabled?`${Number(draft.shadowX)||0}px ${Math.max(4,Number(draft.shadowY)||6)}px ${Math.max(0,Number(draft.shadowBlur)||0)}px ${window.ethvelleHexToRgba(draft.shadowColor||'#000000',Number(draft.shadowOpacity==null?100:draft.shadowOpacity)/100)}`:'none',i.dataset.evFill==='1');
    i.style.backgroundColor='transparent';
    i.style.padding=draft.textBackgroundEnabled?`${Number(draft.textBackgroundPadding)||0}px`:'0';i.style.borderRadius=(Number(draft.textBackgroundRadius)||0)+'px';
    syncLayout();syncComposerBackplate();
  }
  window.setEthvelleComposerColor=function(c){if(!draft)return;draft.textColor=safeHex(c);if(Number(draft.textOpacity)===0)draft.textOpacity=100;activeColorKey='textColor';document.querySelectorAll('#evtcColorStrip .evtc-color-swatch').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',String(b.dataset.color||'').toLowerCase()===draft.textColor.toLowerCase()));const clear=document.getElementById('evtcTextOpacity');const out=document.getElementById('evtcTextOpacityValue');if(clear)clear.value=draft.textOpacity;if(out)out.textContent=Math.round(draft.textOpacity)+'%';fitComposerText();};
  window.setEthvelleComposerOpacity=function(value){if(!draft)return;draft.textOpacity=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(value)));const out=document.getElementById('evtcTextOpacityValue');if(out)out.textContent=Math.round(draft.textOpacity)+'%';const slider=document.getElementById('evtcTextOpacity');if(slider)slider.value=draft.textOpacity;const clear=document.querySelector('.evtc-transparent-swatch');if(clear)clear.classList.toggle('active',draft.textOpacity===0);fitComposerText();};
  window.addEthvelleCustomPaletteColor=function(){const t=currentTarget();const c=safeHex(t?(t[activeColorKey]||t.textColor):'#FFFFFF');try{customColors=window.__ethvelleGetProjectCustomColors?window.__ethvelleGetProjectCustomColors():customColors;}catch(_e){}if(!customColors.some(x=>x.toLowerCase()===c.toLowerCase()))customColors.push(c);customColors=customColors.slice(-12);try{localStorage.setItem('ethvelleCustomTextColors',JSON.stringify(customColors));}catch(_e){}renderColorStrip();const active=document.querySelector(`#evtcColorStrip [data-color="${c}"]`);if(active)active.classList.add('active');if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Color saved to palette',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1200});};
  window.ethvelleComposerToggleBold=function(){if(!draft)return;draft.fontWeight=Number(draft.fontWeight)>=700?500:800;fitComposerText();};
  window.saveEthvelleTextComposer=function(){
    if(!draft)return;
    const wasEditing=!!draft.editingId;
    const text=String(draft.textContent||'').trim();
    if(!text){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Write some text first',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1500});return;}
    let clip=draft.editingId?overlays.find(o=>o.id===draft.editingId&&o.type==='text'):null;
    if(!clip){
      const len=5,startT=(typeof window.ethvellePlanTextInsertStart==='function'?window.ethvellePlanTextInsertStart(len):Math.max(0,Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0));
      clip={id:'ov'+Date.now()+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,5),name:text.replace(/\s+/g,' ').slice(0,22)||'Text',color:'#454B73',type:'text',sourceDuration:len,trimStart:startT,trimEnd:startT+len,thumbs:[],peaks:[],speed:1,transform:{scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0}};
      overlays.push(clip);
    }
    ['textContent','fontFamily','fontSize','fontWeight','fontStyle','textAlign','textColor','textOpacity','strokeWidth','strokeColor','strokeOpacity','shadowEnabled','shadowColor','shadowOpacity','shadowBlur','shadowX','shadowY','textBackgroundEnabled','textBackgroundColor','textBackgroundOpacity','textBackgroundPadding','textBackgroundRadius','textBackgroundShape'].forEach(k=>{if(draft[k]!==undefined)clip[k]=draft[k];});
    clip.textContent=text;clip.name=text.replace(/\s+/g,' ').slice(0,22)||'Text';clip.color='#605DFF';
    ensureAdminFontLoaded(clip.fontFamily||'Inter');
    timelineDuration=Math.max(timelineDuration,clip.trimEnd);
    if(typeof renderOverlayLayers==='function')renderOverlayLayers();if(typeof syncTimelineInnerWidth==='function')syncTimelineInnerWidth();if(typeof renderRuler==='function')renderRuler();
    if(typeof onClipSelected==='function')onClipSelected(clip,wasEditing?'select':'add');else if(typeof selectClipForChrome==='function')selectClipForChrome(clip.id);
    if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();
    window.closeEthvelleTextComposer(true);
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(wasEditing?'Text updated':'Text added',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1200});
  };
  function addTextTools(){
    const row=document.getElementById('bottomToolsClip');if(!row||document.getElementById('clipTextEditTool'))return;
    const make=(id,label,icon,tab)=>{const b=document.createElement('div');b.className='tool-item ev-text-tool';b.id=id;b.setAttribute('role','button');b.tabIndex=0;b.innerHTML=icon+`<span>${label}</span>`;b.onclick=()=>tab==='edit'?window.openEthvelleTextComposer(selectedClip):window.openEthvelleTextStylePanel(tab);b.onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();b.click();}};b.style.display='none';return b;};
    const items=[
      make('clipTextEditTool','Edit text','<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 7V4h16v3M9 20h6M12 4v16"/></svg>','edit'),
      make('clipTextFontTool','Font','<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 20L10 4h4l6 16M7 14h10"/></svg>','font'),
      make('clipTextStrokeTool','Stroke','<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="8"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4"/></svg>','stroke'),
      make('clipTextShadowTool','Shadow','<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="4" y="4" width="11" height="11" rx="1.5"/><rect x="9" y="9" width="11" height="11" rx="1.5"/></svg>','shadow'),
      make('clipTextColorTool','Color','<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 3a9 9 0 1 0 0 18h1a2 2 0 0 0 1-3.7 1.7 1.7 0 0 1 1.3-3.3H17a4 4 0 0 0 4-4c0-4-4-7-9-7z"/><circle cx="7.5" cy="10" r="1"/><circle cx="11" cy="6.8" r="1"/><circle cx="16" cy="8" r="1"/></svg>','color'),
      make('clipTextBackgroundTool','Text BG','<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="16" rx="3"/><path d="M6 15h12"/></svg>','background')
    ];
    items.reverse().forEach(b=>row.insertBefore(b,row.firstChild));
  }
  function setTextTools(){
    addTextTools();const clip=getSelected();const ids=['clipTextEditTool','clipTextFontTool','clipTextStrokeTool','clipTextShadowTool','clipTextColorTool','clipTextBackgroundTool'];
    const visible=!!clip&&!!selectedClip&&String(clip.id)===String(selectedClip)&&!root?.classList.contains('active');
    ids.forEach(id=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.style.display=visible?'flex':'none';});
    const hideForText=['clipAnimTool','clipTrimTool','clipSpeedTool','clipVolumeTool','clipChromaTool','clipExtractAudioTool','clipAddMediaTool','clipSplitTool'];
    hideForText.forEach(id=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el){if(visible)el.style.display='none';else if(typeof oldSyncBottomToolsMode==='function'&&selectedClip){/* the original sync function repopulates these on every state transition */}}});
    if(!visible&&typeof oldSyncBottomToolsMode==='function'&&selectedClip){
      // Re-run original visibility rules for non-text selections, without recursively invoking this wrapper.
      try{oldSyncBottomToolsMode.call(window)}catch(_e){}
    }
  }
  if(typeof oldSyncBottomToolsMode==='function'){
    window.syncBottomToolsMode=function(){oldSyncBottomToolsMode.apply(this,arguments);addTextTools();const clip=getSelected();const isText=!!clip&&!!selectedClip&&String(clip.id)===String(selectedClip);['clipTextEditTool','clipTextFontTool','clipTextStrokeTool','clipTextShadowTool','clipTextColorTool','clipTextBackgroundTool'].forEach(id=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.style.display=isText?'flex':'none';});if(isText){['clipAnimTool','clipTrimTool','clipSpeedTool','clipVolumeTool','clipChromaTool','clipExtractAudioTool','clipAddMediaTool','clipSplitTool'].forEach(id=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.style.display='none';});}};
  }
  window.openPanel=function(key){
    if(key==='text'){window.openEthvelleTextComposer();return;}
    if(typeof oldOpenPanel==='function')return oldOpenPanel.apply(this,arguments);
  };
  function ensurePickerRoot(){
    if(pickerRoot)return pickerRoot;
    pickerRoot=document.createElement('div');pickerRoot.id='ethvelleColorPicker';pickerRoot.className='evcp-root';
    document.body.appendChild(pickerRoot);
    pickerRoot.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===pickerRoot)pickerRoot.classList.remove('active');});
    return pickerRoot;
  }
  function currentPickedHex(){return hslToHex(pickHue,pickSat,pickLight);}
  function renderPickedColor(){pickHex=currentPickedHex();const wheel=document.getElementById('evcpWheel');if(wheel){const a=(pickHue-90)*Math.PI/180;wheel.style.setProperty('--picked-color',pickHex);wheel.style.setProperty('--picker-x',(50+42*Math.cos(a))+'%');wheel.style.setProperty('--picker-y',(50+42*Math.sin(a))+'%');}const sample=document.getElementById('evcpPreview');if(sample)sample.style.backgroundColor=window.ethvelleHexToRgba(pickHex,pickOpacity/100);const hex=document.getElementById('evcpHex');if(hex)hex.value=pickHex;const alpha=document.getElementById('evcpOpacity');if(alpha)alpha.value=pickOpacity;const out=document.getElementById('evcpOpacityValue');if(out)out.textContent=Math.round(pickOpacity)+'%';const sat=document.getElementById('evcpSat');if(sat)sat.value=pickSat;const light=document.getElementById('evcpLight');if(light)light.value=pickLight;updateColorTarget(activeColorKey,pickHex,pickOpacity);}
  window.openEthvelleColorPicker=function(key){
    const t=currentTarget();if(!t){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select or create text first',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1300});return;}
    activeColorKey=['textColor','strokeColor','shadowColor','textBackgroundColor'].includes(key)?key:'textColor';
    const initial=safeHex(t[activeColorKey]||'#FFFFFF'),hsl=hexToHsl(initial),alphaKey=alphaKeyFor(activeColorKey);
    pickHue=hsl.h;pickSat=hsl.s;pickLight=hsl.l;pickOpacity=alphaKey?Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(t[alphaKey]==null?100:t[alphaKey]))):100;pickHex=initial;
    const r=ensurePickerRoot();
    r.innerHTML=`<div class="evcp-sheet"><div class="evcp-grabber"></div><div class="evcp-head"><div><h3>Custom Color</h3><p>Choose a color, transparency, or sample the canvas</p></div><button type="button" class="evcp-close" onclick="document.getElementById('ethvelleColorPicker').classList.remove('active')" aria-label="Close">×</button></div><div class="evcp-main"><div id="evcpWheel" class="evcp-wheel" style="--picked-color:${initial}" role="slider" aria-label="Rainbow color wheel" tabindex="0"></div><div class="evcp-preview-row"><span id="evcpPreview" class="evcp-preview-chip" style="background-color:${initial}"></span><label for="evcpHex">HEX</label><input id="evcpHex" class="evcp-hex" value="${initial}" maxlength="7" spellcheck="false" aria-label="Hex color" oninput="setEthvellePickerHex(this.value)"></div><div class="evcp-field"><label for="evcpSat">Saturation <output id="evcpSatValue">${Math.round(pickSat)}%</output></label><input id="evcpSat" type="range" min="0" max="100" value="${Math.round(pickSat)}" oninput="setEthvellePickerComponent('s',this.value)"></div><div class="evcp-field"><label for="evcpLight">Brightness <output id="evcpLightValue">${Math.round(pickLight)}%</output></label><input id="evcpLight" type="range" min="5" max="95" value="${Math.round(pickLight)}" oninput="setEthvellePickerComponent('l',this.value)"></div><div class="evcp-field"><label for="evcpOpacity">Opacity <output id="evcpOpacityValue">${Math.round(pickOpacity)}%</output></label><input id="evcpOpacity" type="range" min="0" max="100" value="${Math.round(pickOpacity)}" oninput="setEthvellePickerOpacity(this.value)"></div><div class="evcp-actions"><button type="button" class="evcp-secondary" onclick="ethvelleStartStageColorSample()">⌁ Sample color</button></div></div><button type="button" class="evcp-done" onclick="document.getElementById('ethvelleColorPicker').classList.remove('active')">Apply color</button></div>`;
    r.classList.add('active');
    const wheel=document.getElementById('evcpWheel');let dragging=false;
    renderPickedColor();
    const setHueFromEvent=e=>{const b=wheel.getBoundingClientRect(),dx=e.clientX-(b.left+b.width/2),dy=e.clientY-(b.top+b.height/2);pickHue=(Math.atan2(dy,dx)*180/Math.PI+90+360)%360;renderPickedColor();};
    wheel.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{dragging=true;try{wheel.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}setHueFromEvent(e);});
    wheel.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(dragging)setHueFromEvent(e);});
    wheel.addEventListener('pointerup',()=>dragging=false);wheel.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>dragging=false);
  };
  window.setEthvellePickerComponent=function(which,value){if(which==='s')pickSat=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(value)));else pickLight=Math.max(5,Math.min(95,Number(value)));const a=document.getElementById('evcpSatValue'),b=document.getElementById('evcpLightValue');if(a)a.textContent=Math.round(pickSat)+'%';if(b)b.textContent=Math.round(pickLight)+'%';renderPickedColor();};
  window.setEthvellePickerOpacity=function(value){pickOpacity=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(value)));renderPickedColor();};
  window.setEthvellePickerHex=function(value){if(!/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(String(value||'')))return;const hsl=hexToHsl(value);pickHue=hsl.h;pickSat=hsl.s;pickLight=hsl.l;pickHex=safeHex(value);renderPickedColor();};
  window.ethvelleStartStageColorSample=async function(){
    const wasComposer=!!(root&&root.classList.contains('active')),wasStyle=!!(styleRoot&&styleRoot.classList.contains('active'));
    if(pickerRoot)pickerRoot.classList.remove('active');
    if(wasComposer)root.style.visibility='hidden';
    if(wasStyle)styleRoot.style.visibility='hidden';
    const restorePanels=()=>{if(wasComposer&&root)root.style.visibility='';if(wasStyle&&styleRoot)styleRoot.style.visibility='';};
    if(window.EyeDropper){try{const result=await new EyeDropper().open();const c=safeHex(result.sRGBHex);updateColorTarget(activeColorKey,c,pickOpacity);rememberProjectSampleColor(c);restorePanels();if(wasStyle)window.openEthvelleTextStylePanel(activeColorKey==='strokeColor'?'stroke':activeColorKey==='shadowColor'?'shadow':activeColorKey==='textBackgroundColor'?'background':'color');if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Canvas color applied',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1100});return;}catch(_e){restorePanels();return;}}
    const stage=document.getElementById('canvasStage');if(!stage){restorePanels();if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Canvas color sampling is not supported in this browser',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
    let crosshair=document.getElementById('evcpSampleCrosshair');if(!crosshair){crosshair=document.createElement('div');crosshair.id='evcpSampleCrosshair';crosshair.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');document.body.appendChild(crosshair);}const sr=stage.getBoundingClientRect();crosshair.style.left=(sr.left+sr.width/2)+'px';crosshair.style.top=(sr.top+sr.height/2)+'px';crosshair.style.display='block';
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Tap and release on a canvas color to sample it',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1800});
    const handler=e=>{if(!stage.contains(e.target))return;e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();const el=document.elementFromPoint(e.clientX,e.clientY);let sampled=null;
      if(el&&el.tagName==='CANVAS'){try{const rr=el.getBoundingClientRect(),x=Math.max(0,Math.min(el.width-1,Math.round((e.clientX-rr.left)*el.width/rr.width))),y=Math.max(0,Math.min(el.height-1,Math.round((e.clientY-rr.top)*el.height/rr.height))),d=el.getContext('2d').getImageData(x,y,1,1).data;sampled='#'+[d[0],d[1],d[2]].map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();}catch(_e){}}
      if(!sampled&&el){const match=getComputedStyle(el).color.match(/\d+/g);if(match&&match.length>=3)sampled='#'+match.slice(0,3).map(v=>Math.max(0,Math.min(255,Number(v))).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase();}
      if(sampled){updateColorTarget(activeColorKey,sampled,pickOpacity);rememberProjectSampleColor(sampled);if(wasStyle)window.openEthvelleTextStylePanel(activeColorKey==='strokeColor'?'stroke':activeColorKey==='shadowColor'?'shadow':activeColorKey==='textBackgroundColor'?'background':'color');}else if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not sample this pixel; choose it on the color wheel',{icon:toastIcons.info});
      document.removeEventListener('pointerup',handler,true);if(crosshair)crosshair.style.display='none';restorePanels();
    };
    document.addEventListener('pointerup',handler,true);
  };
  window.openEthvelleTextStylePanel=function(tab){
    let target=currentTarget();if(!target){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select a text layer first',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1300});return;}
    if(!styleRoot){styleRoot=document.createElement('div');styleRoot.id='ethvelleTextStylePanel';styleRoot.className='evts-root';document.body.appendChild(styleRoot);styleRoot.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===styleRoot)window.closeEthvelleTextStylePanel();});}
    styleRoot.classList.add('active');styleRoot.classList.toggle('evts-compose-subpanel',!!(root&&root.classList.contains('active')));
    try{customColors=window.__ethvelleGetProjectCustomColors?window.__ethvelleGetProjectCustomColors():customColors;}catch(_e){}
    const t=currentTarget();const tabs={font:'Font & Layout',stroke:'Stroke / Outline',shadow:'Shadow',color:'Text Color',background:'Text Background'};
    const switchField=(key,label,checked)=>`<div class="evts-field evts-control-line"><label for="evts-${key}">${label}</label><input class="evts-switch" id="evts-${key}" type="checkbox" ${checked?'checked':''} onchange="ethvelleTextStyleChanged('${key}',this.checked);window.ethvelleRefreshTextSwatches&&window.ethvelleRefreshTextSwatches()"></div>`;
    const range=(key,label,min,max,val,step=1)=>`<div class="evts-field"><label for="evts-${key}"><span>${label}</span><output class="evts-value" id="evts-${key}-value">${val}</output></label><input id="evts-${key}" type="range" min="${min}" max="${max}" step="${step}" value="${val}" oninput="ethvelleTextStyleChanged('${key}',this.value);document.getElementById('evts-${key}-value').textContent=this.value"></div>`;
    const color=(key,label,val)=>`<div class="evts-field evts-control-line"><label>${label}</label><div class="evts-color-actions"><span class="evts-current-color" style="background:${safeHex(val)}" aria-label="Current color ${safeHex(val)}"></span><button type="button" class="evts-wheel-btn" title="Open custom color wheel" aria-label="Choose custom color" onclick="openEthvelleColorPicker('${key}')"></button></div></div>`;
    const swatches=(key,val)=>{const alphaKey=alphaKeyFor(key),transparent=alphaKey&&Number(t[alphaKey])===0;return `<div class="evts-swatches" data-swatch-key="${key}"><button type="button" class="evts-transparent-swatch ${transparent?'active':''}" title="Transparent" aria-label="Transparent" onclick="ethvelleTextStyleChanged('${alphaKey}',0);this.parentElement.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));this.classList.add('active');window.ethvelleRefreshTextSwatches&&window.ethvelleRefreshTextSwatches()"></button>${[...new Set([...EV_COLORS,...customColors])].map(c=>`<button type="button" data-color="${c}" class="evts-swatch ${String(val).toLowerCase()===c.toLowerCase()?'active':''}" style="background:${c}" aria-label="${c}" onclick="ethvelleTextStyleChanged('${key}','${c}');this.parentElement.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));this.classList.add('active');window.ethvelleRefreshTextSwatches&&window.ethvelleRefreshTextSwatches()"></button>`).join('')}</div>`;};
    const alphaRange=(key,label,val)=>range(key,label,0,100,Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(val==null?100:val))));
    let body='';
    if(tab==='font'){
      const options=EV_FONTS.map(([v,l])=>`<option value="${v}" ${t.fontFamily===v?'selected':''}>${l}</option>`).join('');
      body=`<div class="evts-field"><label for="evts-fontFamily">Font family</label><select id="evts-fontFamily" onchange="ethvelleTextStyleChanged('fontFamily',this.value)">${options}</select></div>${range('fontSize','Font size',12,120,Math.max(12,Number(t.fontSize)||44))}<div class="evts-field"><label for="evts-fontWeight">Weight</label><select id="evts-fontWeight" onchange="ethvelleTextStyleChanged('fontWeight',this.value)"><option value="400" ${Number(t.fontWeight)===400?'selected':''}>Regular</option><option value="500" ${Number(t.fontWeight)===500?'selected':''}>Medium</option><option value="600" ${Number(t.fontWeight)===600?'selected':''}>Semi-bold</option><option value="700" ${Number(t.fontWeight)===700?'selected':''}>Bold</option><option value="800" ${Number(t.fontWeight)>=800?'selected':''}>Extra bold</option></select></div>${switchField('fontStyle','Italic',t.fontStyle==='italic')}<div class="evts-field"><label>Text alignment</label><div class="evts-align-row"><button class="evts-align-btn ${t.textAlign==='left'?'active':''}" onclick="ethvelleTextStyleChanged('textAlign','left');openEthvelleTextStylePanel('font')">Left</button><button class="evts-align-btn ${(!t.textAlign||t.textAlign==='center')?'active':''}" onclick="ethvelleTextStyleChanged('textAlign','center');openEthvelleTextStylePanel('font')">Center</button><button class="evts-align-btn ${t.textAlign==='right'?'active':''}" onclick="ethvelleTextStyleChanged('textAlign','right');openEthvelleTextStylePanel('font')">Right</button></div></div>`;
    }else if(tab==='stroke'){
      body=`${switchField('strokeEnabled','Enable outline',Number(t.strokeWidth)>0)}${color('strokeColor','Outline color',t.strokeColor||'#16162A')}${swatches('strokeColor',t.strokeColor||'#16162A')}${alphaRange('strokeOpacity','Outline opacity',t.strokeOpacity)}${range('strokeWidth','Outline thickness',0,14,Number(t.strokeWidth)||0,.5)}`;
    }else if(tab==='shadow'){
      body=`${switchField('shadowEnabled','Enable shadow',!!t.shadowEnabled)}${color('shadowColor','Shadow color',t.shadowColor||'#000000')}${swatches('shadowColor',t.shadowColor||'#000000')}${alphaRange('shadowOpacity','Shadow opacity',t.shadowOpacity)}${range('shadowBlur','Blur',0,32,Number(t.shadowBlur)||6)}${range('shadowX','Horizontal offset',-24,24,Number(t.shadowX)||0)}${range('shadowY','Vertical offset',4,24,Math.max(4,Number(t.shadowY)||6))}`;
    }else if(tab==='color'){
      body=`${color('textColor','Text color',t.textColor||'#FFFFFF')}${swatches('textColor',t.textColor||'#FFFFFF')}${alphaRange('textOpacity','Text opacity',t.textOpacity)}`;
    }else{
      body=`${switchField('textBackgroundEnabled','Enable text background',!!t.textBackgroundEnabled)}${color('textBackgroundColor','Background color',t.textBackgroundColor||'#182A66')}${swatches('textBackgroundColor',t.textBackgroundColor||'#182A66')}${range('textBackgroundOpacity','Opacity',0,100,Number(t.textBackgroundOpacity==null?78:t.textBackgroundOpacity))}${range('textBackgroundPadding','Padding',0,30,Number(t.textBackgroundPadding==null?8:t.textBackgroundPadding))}${range('textBackgroundRadius','Corner roundness',0,32,Number(t.textBackgroundRadius==null?8:t.textBackgroundRadius))}${window.ethvelleTextBackgroundShapePicker?window.ethvelleTextBackgroundShapePicker(t):''}`;
    }
    styleRoot.innerHTML=`<div class="evts-sheet"><div class="evts-handle"></div><div class="evts-header"><h3 class="evts-title">${tabs[tab]||'Text Style'}</h3><button type="button" class="evts-close" aria-label="Close" onclick="closeEthvelleTextStylePanel()">×</button></div>${body}<button type="button" class="evts-done" onclick="closeEthvelleTextStylePanel()">Done</button></div>`;
  };
  window.closeEthvelleTextStylePanel=function(){if(styleRoot){styleRoot.classList.remove('active');styleRoot.classList.remove('evts-compose-subpanel');}};
  window.ethvelleTextStyleChanged=function(key,value){
    const t=currentTarget();if(!t)return;
    if(key==='strokeEnabled'){t.strokeWidth=value?(Number(t.strokeWidth)>0?Number(t.strokeWidth):3):0;}
    else if(key==='fontStyle'){t.fontStyle=value?'italic':'normal';}
    else if(key==='shadowEnabled'){t.shadowEnabled=!!value;}
    else if(key==='textBackgroundEnabled'){t.textBackgroundEnabled=!!value;}
    else if(['fontSize','fontWeight','textOpacity','strokeWidth','strokeOpacity','shadowOpacity','shadowBlur','shadowX','shadowY','textBackgroundOpacity','textBackgroundPadding','textBackgroundRadius'].includes(key)){t[key]=Number(value);}
    else if(['fontFamily','textAlign','textColor','strokeColor','shadowColor','textBackgroundColor','textBackgroundShape'].includes(key)){t[key]=value;activeColorKey=key;}
    if(key==='fontFamily'&&typeof ensureAdminFontLoaded==='function')ensureAdminFontLoaded(value);
    if(root&&root.classList.contains('active'))fitComposerText();
    else{if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}
  };
})();
</script>


<script id="ethvelle-audio-start-resume-v7">
(function(){
  const previousStartPlayback=window.startPlayback;
  if(typeof previousStartPlayback==='function'&&!previousStartPlayback.__ethvelleAudioResumeV7){
    const wrapped=function(){
      try{if(typeof ensureAudioOverlayContext==='function'){const ctx=ensureAudioOverlayContext();if(ctx&&ctx.state==='suspended')ctx.resume().catch(()=>{});}}catch(_e){}
      return previousStartPlayback.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__ethvelleAudioResumeV7=true;window.startPlayback=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-picsart-text-system-v9">
/* Ethvelle Picsart-inspired text layer system v9 — live controls, shared color picker and effects. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $ = (id)=>document.getElementById(id);
  const baseColors=['#FFFFFF','#F5F5F7','#D9D9E0','#A3A3A3','#70717A','#414141','#17171C','#000000','#FFD9E2','#FFC5CF','#FF8C9A','#FA847C','#FF4E57','#FF174D','#D70D20','#8B0000','#FFE0B2','#FFB74D','#FF7B24','#FFD43B','#FFF176','#C6E64D','#B5E53B','#66BB6A','#25C98A','#16D9D0','#80DEEA','#13A7FF','#2F5CE8','#605DFF','#875BFA','#D946EF'];
  const colorKeys=new Set(['textColor','strokeColor','shadowColor','textBackgroundColor']);
  let activeTool='font', colorTarget='textColor', dock=null, picker=null, sampling=false, sampleMove=null, samplingCleanup=null;
  let oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode, oldOpenComposer=window.openEthvelleTextComposer, oldSaveComposer=window.saveEthvelleTextComposer, oldStyleChanged=window.ethvelleTextStyleChanged, oldColorPicker=window.openEthvelleColorPicker, oldRenderFrame=window.renderFrame, oldRenderOverlayLayers=window.renderOverlayLayers, oldStylePanel=window.openEthvelleTextStylePanel, oldSelectClip=window.selectClipForChrome;
  let composerExtra=null, savedColors=[], persistTimer=null, rowWasDockHidden=false;
  savedColors=[];
  const svg={
    plus:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14"/></svg>',
    font:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 20L10 4h4l6 16M7 14h10"/></svg>',
    color:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 3a9 9 0 1 0 0 18h1a2 2 0 0 0 1-3.7 1.7 1.7 0 0 1 1.3-3.3H17a4 4 0 0 0 4-4c0-4-4-7-9-7z"/><circle cx="7.5" cy="10" r="1"/><circle cx="11" cy="6.8" r="1"/><circle cx="16" cy="8" r="1"/></svg>',
    highlight:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m8 4 8 8M6 13l5 5M4 20h12M14 3l7 7-8 8-7-7z"/></svg>',
    stroke:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="5"/></svg>',
    spacing:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 6h16M4 12h16M4 18h16M8 3 5 6l3 3M16 15l3 3-3 3"/></svg>',
    opacity:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M12 3v18M3 12h18"/></svg>',
    styles:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m12 3 2.5 5.5L20 11l-5.5 2.5L12 19l-2.5-5.5L4 11l5.5-2.5z"/></svg>',
    perspective:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 5h16l-3 14H7zM4 5l3 14M20 5l-3 14"/></svg>',
    shadow:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect x="3" y="3" width="12" height="12" rx="1.5"/><rect x="9" y="9" width="12" height="12" rx="1.5"/></svg>',
    bend:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M3 17Q12 1 21 17M4 20Q12 7 20 20"/></svg>',
    edit:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 7V4h16v3M9 20h6M12 4v16"/></svg>',
    eyedrop:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m14 6 4 4M5 19l4-4m-6 7 4-1 11-11a2.8 2.8 0 0 0-4-4L3 17z"/></svg>',
    wheel:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 3a9 9 0 1 0 0 18h1a2 2 0 0 0 1-3.7 1.7 1.7 0 0 1 1.3-3.3H17a4 4 0 0 0 4-4c0-4-4-7-9-7z"/></svg>'
  };
  const names={font:'Font',color:'Color',gradient:'Gradient',texture:'Texture',highlight:'Highlight',stroke:'Stroke',spacing:'Spacing',opacity:'Opacity',styles:'Styles',perspective:'Perspective',shadow:'Shadow',bend:'Bend',background:'Background'};
  function selected(){try{return overlays.find(o=>String(o.id)===String(selectedClip)&&o.type==='text')||null;}catch(_){return null;}}
  function isComposer(){return !!$('ethvelleTextComposer')?.classList.contains('active');}
  function target(){return isComposer()?(composerExtra||(composerExtra={})):selected();}
  const STYLE_PRESET_KEYS=new Set(['textColor','strokeColor','strokeWidth','shadowEnabled','shadowColor','shadowBlur','shadowX','shadowY','shadowOpacity','textGradient','textTexture','textTextureSize','textOpacity']);
  function syncStyleCardSelection(name){
    const controls=$('evpaControls');if(!controls)return;
    const selectedName=String(name||'');
    controls.querySelectorAll('.evpa-style-card').forEach(card=>{
      const active=!!selectedName&&card.dataset.evpaStyle===selectedName;
      card.classList.toggle('selected',active);
      card.setAttribute('aria-pressed',active?'true':'false');
    });
  }
  // Public read-only bridge: keeps add-on controls pointed at the same live text object.
  window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget=function(){return target();};
  window.ethvellePicsartGetColorTarget=function(){return colorTarget;};
  window.ethvellePicsartToggle=function(key){
    const t=target()||{};let value;
    if(key==='fontStyle')value=t.fontStyle==='italic'?'normal':'italic';
    else if(key==='textUnderline'||key==='textStrikethrough'||key==='textCurveReverse')value=!Boolean(t[key]);
    else if(key==='shadowEnabled'||key==='textBackgroundEnabled')value=!Boolean(t[key]);
    else value=!Boolean(t[key]);
    if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetValue==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetValue(key,value);
  };
  function hex(v){return /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(String(v||''))?String(v).toUpperCase():'#FFFFFF';}
  function rgba(c,a){const h=hex(c).slice(1),n=[0,2,4].map(i=>parseInt(h.slice(i,i+2),16));return `rgba(${n[0]},${n[1]},${n[2]},${Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(a==null?100:a)))/100})`;}
  function save(){if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')try{persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}catch(_e){try{persistProjectState();}catch(__){}}}
  function redraw(){if(typeof renderFrame==='function')try{renderFrame();}catch(_e){}else if(typeof renderOverlayLayers==='function')try{renderOverlayLayers();}catch(_e){};}
  function styleTarget(key,value,live=false){
    const t=target(); if(!t)return;
    const styleValue=key==='fontStyle'?(value==='italic'||value===true):value;
    const nextStyleValue=key==='fontStyle'?(styleValue?'italic':'normal'):value;
    if(!isComposer()&&STYLE_PRESET_KEYS.has(key)&&t.textStyleName&&String(t[key])!==String(nextStyleValue)){
      delete t.textStyleName;
      syncStyleCardSelection('');
    }
    if(isComposer() && oldStyleChanged && !['textCurve','textGradient','textTexture','textTextureSize','textLetterSpacing','textLineHeight','textPerspectiveX','textPerspectiveY','textMixBlend','fontStyle','textHighlightEnabled','textHighlightColor','textHighlightOpacity','textHighlightPadding','textHighlightRadius','textBackgroundShape','textBold','textUnderline','textStrikethrough','textBendUp','textBendDown','textBendFront','textBendBack'].includes(key)){
      try{oldStyleChanged(key,styleValue);}catch(_e){}
    }
    t[key]=key==='fontStyle'?(styleValue?'italic':'normal'):value;
    if(isComposer()){
      const input=$('evtcStageText');
      if(input){applyComposerPreview(input,t);if(window.ethvelleSyncComposerBackplate)window.ethvelleSyncComposerBackplate(t);}
      return;
    }
    // A live slider drag only changes the selected text node. Running renderFrame() here
    // used to repaint the full composition for every thumb sample and made Android touch
    // sliders stutter. Do the full redraw/save once when the drag commits instead.
    if(live){decorateSelectedText();return;}
    redraw();
    decorateSelectedText();
    save();
    // A font tap never rebuilds the panel: the gallery toggles its own .active card in place, so the
    // colour rows, tabs and the gallery scroll position stay exactly as they are.
    if(key!=='fontFamily'&&!window.__evKeepDock)updateDockControls();
  }
  // Repaint the selected text (canvas + overlay node, or the composer preview) without touching the panel.
  window.ethvellePicsartRepaintText=function(){
    const t=target();if(!t)return;
    if(isComposer()){const input=$('evtcStageText');if(input){applyComposerPreview(input,t);if(window.ethvelleSyncComposerBackplate)window.ethvelleSyncComposerBackplate(t);}return;}
    redraw();decorateSelectedText();
  };
  function applyComposerPreview(input,t){
    /* Treat the dedicated Opacity control as a composite layer alpha in the composer too,
       so the fill, outline, shadow and text backplate all fade together. */
    input.style.opacity=String(Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(t.textOverallOpacity==null?100:t.textOverallOpacity)))/100);
    input.style.color=rgba(t.textColor||'#FFFFFF',t.textOpacity==null?100:t.textOpacity);
    input.style.fontFamily='"'+String(t.fontFamily||'Inter').replace(/"/g,'')+'",sans-serif';
    input.style.fontWeight=String(t.fontWeight||800);input.style.fontStyle=t.fontStyle==='italic'?'italic':'normal';input.style.textAlign=t.textAlign||'center';
    input.style.webkitTextStroke=Number(t.strokeWidth)>0?`${Number(t.strokeWidth)}px ${rgba(t.strokeColor||'#000000',t.strokeOpacity)}`:'0 transparent';
    const _evHasFill=!!String(t.textGradient||t.textTexture||'');if(input.dataset.evFill!==(_evHasFill?'1':'0'))input.dataset.evFill=_evHasFill?'1':'0';
    window.ethvelleApplyTextShadow(input,t.shadowEnabled?`${Number(t.shadowX)||0}px ${Math.max(4,Number(t.shadowY)||6)}px ${Math.max(0,Number(t.shadowBlur)||0)}px ${rgba(t.shadowColor||'#000000',t.shadowOpacity)}`:'none',_evHasFill);
    input.style.backgroundColor=t.textBackgroundEnabled?rgba(t.textBackgroundColor||'#182A66',t.textBackgroundOpacity==null?78:t.textBackgroundOpacity):'transparent';
    input.style.padding=t.textBackgroundEnabled?`${Number(t.textBackgroundPadding)||0}px`:'0';input.style.borderRadius=(Number(t.textBackgroundRadius)||0)+'px';input.style.textDecoration=[t.textUnderline?'underline':'',t.textStrikethrough?'line-through':''].filter(Boolean).join(' ')||'none';const bendVertical=(Number(t.textBendDown)||0)-(Number(t.textBendUp)||0),bendDepth=(Number(t.textBendFront)||0)-(Number(t.textBendBack)||0);input.style.transform=`perspective(700px) rotateX(${bendDepth*.12}deg) rotateY(${bendDepth*.08}deg) skewY(${bendVertical*.015}deg)`;
    const fill=String(t.textGradient||t.textTexture||'');
    if(fill){input.style.backgroundImage=fill;input.style.backgroundClip='text';input.style.webkitBackgroundClip='text';input.style.webkitTextFillColor='transparent';input.style.backgroundSize=String(t.textTextureSize||'100% 100%');input.style.backgroundColor='transparent';}
    else{input.style.backgroundImage='none';input.style.backgroundClip='border-box';input.style.webkitBackgroundClip='border-box';input.style.webkitTextFillColor='';input.style.backgroundSize='auto';}
  }
  function setRange(key,value){
    const numberKeys=['fontSize','fontWeight','strokeWidth','strokeOpacity','shadowOpacity','shadowBlur','shadowX','shadowY','textBackgroundOpacity','textBackgroundPadding','textBackgroundRadius','textOpacity','textOverallOpacity','textCurve','textLetterSpacing','textLineHeight','textPerspectiveX','textPerspectiveY','textHighlightOpacity','textHighlightPadding','textHighlightRadius','textBendUp','textBendDown','textBendFront','textBendBack'];
    if(numberKeys.includes(key))value=Number(value);
    styleTarget(key,value);
  }
  function markHistory(){if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')try{persistProjectState();}catch(_e){}}
  function queueSave(){if(persistTimer)clearTimeout(persistTimer);persistTimer=setTimeout(()=>{persistTimer=null;save();},180);}
  function makeDock(){
    if(dock)return dock;
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip'); if(!row||!row.parentNode)return null;
    dock=document.createElement('section');dock.id='ethvellePicsartTextDock';dock.className='evpa-dock';dock.style.display='none';
    dock.innerHTML=`<div class="evpa-controls" id="evpaControls"></div><div class="evpa-palette" id="evpaPalette"></div><div class="evpa-tabs" id="evpaTabs"></div>`;
    row.parentNode.insertBefore(dock,row);
    const tabs=$('evpaTabs');
    const entries=[['font','Font'],['color','Color'],['gradient','Gradient'],['texture','Texture'],['highlight','Background'],['stroke','Stroke'],['spacing','Spacing'],['opacity','Opacity'],['styles','Styles'],['perspective','Perspective'],['shadow','Shadow'],['bend','Bend'],['background','BG']];
    tabs.innerHTML=entries.map(([k,l])=>`<button class="evpa-tab" type="button" data-tab="${k}" onclick="ethvellePicsartSetTool('${k}')">${svg[k]||svg.font}<span>${l}</span></button>`).join('');
    return dock;
  }
  let dockOriginalParent=null,dockOriginalNext=null;
  function moveDockToComposer(){
    makeDock();const host=$('evtcDock');if(!dock||!host)return;
    if(dock.parentNode!==host){dockOriginalParent=dock.parentNode;dockOriginalNext=dock.nextSibling;host.appendChild(dock);}
    host.querySelectorAll('.evtc-dock-tools,#evtcColorStrip,.evtc-opacity-line').forEach(el=>el.style.display='none');
    dock.classList.add('evpa-in-composer');dock.style.display='flex';activeTool='color';updateDockControls();
  }
  function moveDockBackToEditor(){
    if(!dock||!dockOriginalParent)return;
    dock.classList.remove('evpa-in-composer');
    if(dockOriginalNext&&dockOriginalNext.parentNode===dockOriginalParent)dockOriginalParent.insertBefore(dock,dockOriginalNext);else{const row=$('bottomToolsClip');if(row&&row.parentNode===dockOriginalParent)dockOriginalParent.insertBefore(dock,row);else dockOriginalParent.appendChild(dock);}
    dockOriginalParent=null;dockOriginalNext=null;
  }
  function updateDockVisibility(){
    makeDock();const t=selected();const inCompose=isComposer();/* v231: only an explicit text-edit action may show the main dock. */const mainOn=!!t&&!inCompose&&window.__evTextEditorOpen===true;const composeOn=inCompose&&!!composerExtra;const on=mainOn||composeOn;
    if(dock)dock.style.display=on?'flex':'none';
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip');if(row&&mainOn){rowWasDockHidden=true;row.style.visibility='hidden';row.style.pointerEvents='none';}else if(row&&rowWasDockHidden&&!inCompose){rowWasDockHidden=false;row.style.visibility='';row.style.pointerEvents=''; /* retain the original flex geometry; syncBottomToolsMode controls display */}
    document.querySelectorAll('.ev-text-tool').forEach(el=>el.style.display='none');
    if(on&&dock){dock.style.setProperty('--evpa-active-color',hex((target()||{}).textColor||'#fff'));updateDockControls();}
  }
  function colorsRow(key,val){
    let custom=[];try{custom=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ethvelleCustomTextColors')||'[]').filter(c=>/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(c));}catch(_e){}
    /* v219-fix: colours picked from the canvas (saved per project) sit right beside Transparent, newest first,
       ahead of the fixed base palette. A picked colour that already exists in the base palette is not duplicated. */
    const baseLower=new Set(baseColors.map(c=>String(c).toLowerCase()));
    const customFront=[...new Set(custom.map(c=>c.toUpperCase()).filter(c=>!baseLower.has(c.toLowerCase())))].reverse();
    const all=[...customFront,...baseColors];
    /* v219-fix: emit the REAL canvas-eyedropper button (same markup/classes the eyedropper script creates) in the
       very first render, so the row never paints once without it and then jumps 43px when it appears. */
    return `<div class="evpa-swatches"><button type="button" class="ev-eyedrop-button ev-eyedrop-text" data-ev-eyedrop-button="text" aria-label="Pick color from canvas" aria-pressed="false" title="Pick color from canvas"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m2 22 1-1h3l9-9"/><path d="M3 21v-3l9-9"/><path d="m15 6 3.4-3.4a2.1 2.1 0 1 1 3 3L18 9l.4.4a2.1 2.1 0 1 1-3 3l-3.8-3.8a2.1 2.1 0 1 1 3-3l.4.4Z"/></svg></button><button class="evpa-color-wheel" type="button" title="Custom color" aria-label="Custom color" onclick="openEthvelleColorPicker('${key}')"></button><button class="evpa-transparent" type="button" title="Transparent / none" aria-label="Transparent" onclick="ethvellePicsartSetNone('${key}')"></button>${all.map(c=>`<button class="evpa-swatch ${hex(val)===hex(c)?'active':''}" type="button" data-color="${c}" title="${c}" style="background:${c}" onclick="ethvellePicsartSetColor('${key}','${c}')"></button>`).join('')}</div>`;
  }
  function rangeControl(key,label,min,max,val,step=1,unit=''){
    const v=Number(val)||0;return `<label class="evpa-range"><span>${label}<output id="evpa-v-${key}">${v}${unit}</output></span><input type="range" min="${min}" max="${max}" step="${step}" value="${v}" oninput="ethvellePicsartSetValue('${key}',this.value,'${unit}',true)" onchange="ethvellePicsartSetValue('${key}',this.value,'${unit}',false)" onkeyup="ethvellePicsartSetValue('${key}',this.value,'${unit}',false)"></label>`;
  }

  function safeEffectCss(value){
    const css=String(value||'').trim();
    if(!css || !/gradient\(/i.test(css) || !/^[#a-zA-Z0-9(),.%\s+-]+$/.test(css))return '';
    return css;
  }
  function safeEffectLabel(value,fallback){return String(value||fallback||'Custom').replace(/[&<>"']/g,'').slice(0,24)||fallback||'Custom';}
  function readCustomEffects(kind){
    const key=kind==='texture'?'ethvelleCustomTextTextures':'ethvelleCustomTextGradients';
    try{
      const list=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'[]');
      if(!Array.isArray(list))return [];
      return list.filter(x=>x&&safeEffectCss(x.css)).slice(0,16).map((x,i)=>({name:safeEffectLabel(x.name,`Custom ${i+1}`),css:safeEffectCss(x.css),size:/^(100% 100%|\d{1,3}px \d{1,3}px)$/.test(String(x.size||''))?String(x.size):'100% 100%'}));
    }catch(_e){return [];}
  }
  function toggle(key,label,val){return `<label class="evpa-toggle"><span>${label}</span><input type="checkbox" ${val?'checked':''} onchange="ethvellePicsartSetValue('${key}',this.checked)"><i></i></label>`;}
  function controlButton(label,active,onclick){return `<button type="button" class="evpa-choice ${active?'active':''}" onclick="${onclick}">${label}</button>`;}
  function updateDockControls(){
    if(!dock||dock.style.display==='none')return;const t=target();if(!t)return;
    const palette=$('evpaPalette'),controls=$('evpaControls');if(!palette||!controls)return;
    const controlsScrollTop=controls.scrollTop;
    const oldPaletteScroller=palette.querySelector('.evpa-swatches');
    const paletteScrollLeft=oldPaletteScroller?oldPaletteScroller.scrollLeft:0;
    let key='textColor',color=t.textColor||'#FFFFFF';
    if(activeTool==='stroke'){key='strokeColor';color=t.strokeColor||'#000000';}
    else if(activeTool==='shadow'){key='shadowColor';color=t.shadowColor||'#000000';}
    else if(activeTool==='highlight'||activeTool==='background'){key='textBackgroundColor';color=t.textBackgroundColor||t.textHighlightColor||'#182A66';}
    else if(activeTool==='gradient'||activeTool==='texture'){key='textColor';color=t.textColor||'#FFFFFF';}
    colorTarget=key;
    /* Stroke / Shadow / Background keep their colour row INSIDE the scrolling controls (top of the
       panel) instead of pinned above the tab bar. Rebuilding the controls below would destroy the row,
       so bring the shared #evpaPalette node back to its dock slot first, then re-insert it on top. */
    const tabsEl=$('evpaTabs');
    if(palette.parentNode!==dock&&tabsEl&&tabsEl.parentNode===dock)dock.insertBefore(palette,tabsEl);
    palette.innerHTML=colorsRow(key,color);

  window.ethvellePicsartSetTool=function(tool){activeTool=tool;updateDockControls();};
  window.ethvellePicsartSetValue=function(key,value,unit,live=false){
    if(key==='fontStyle'&&value==='italic')styleTarget(key,value,live);else if(key==='textUnderline'||key==='textStrikethrough'||key==='textCurveReverse')styleTarget(key,!!value,live);else styleTarget(key,value,live);
    if(key==='textUnderline'||key==='textStrikethrough'||key==='textCurveReverse'||key==='fontWeight'||key==='textAlign'||key==='fontStyle'){if(!live)updateDockControls();}
    const out=$('evpa-v-'+key);if(out){const n=Number(value);out.textContent=(Number.isFinite(n)?n:value)+(unit||'');}
  };
  window.ethvellePicsartSetColorTarget=function(key){if(colorKeys.has(key))colorTarget=key;};
  window.ethvellePicsartSetColor=function(key,c){
    colorTarget=key;const value=hex(c);styleTarget(key,value,true);
    if(key==='textBackgroundColor'&&activeTool==='highlight'){const t=target();if(t)t.textHighlightColor=value;}
    // Palette taps do not rebuild the whole panel: keep the swatch scroller stationary and
    // avoid a double render on Android touch devices.
    const palette=$('evpaPalette');if(palette)palette.querySelectorAll('.evpa-swatch[data-color]').forEach(btn=>btn.classList.toggle('active',hex(btn.dataset.color)===value));
    if(!isComposer())save();
  };
  window.ethvellePicsartSetNone=function(key){const alphaKey=({textColor:'textOpacity',strokeColor:'strokeOpacity',shadowColor:'shadowOpacity',textBackgroundColor:'textBackgroundOpacity'})[key];if(!alphaKey)return;/* v223-fix: update in place. Rebuilding the whole dock on every Transparent tap made the full panel blink. */window.__evKeepDock=true;try{styleTarget(alphaKey,0,false);}finally{window.__evKeepDock=false;}try{const dk=document.getElementById('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(dk){dk.querySelectorAll('input[type="range"]').forEach(r=>{const oi=String(r.getAttribute('oninput')||'');if(oi.indexOf("'"+alphaKey+"'")>-1){r.value=0;r.style.setProperty('--ev-range-progress','0%');}});const o=document.getElementById('evpa-v-'+alphaKey);if(o)o.textContent='0%';}}catch(_e){}if(typeof window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches==='function')window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches();};
  function effectCurrent(kind){const t=target();return t?String(kind==='gradient'?(t.textGradient||''):(t.textTexture||'')):'';}
  function effectChipFill(b){try{return decodeURIComponent(b.dataset.evpaFill||'');}catch(_e){return b.dataset.evpaFill||'';}}
  function syncEffectChipSelection(kind){
    const controls=$('evpaControls');if(!controls)return;
    const cur=effectCurrent(kind);
    controls.querySelectorAll('.evpa-gradient-chip[data-evpa-effect][data-evpa-fill]').forEach(b=>{
      const on=b.dataset.evpaEffect===kind&&effectChipFill(b)===cur;
      b.classList.toggle('selected',on);b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',on?'true':'false');
    });
  }
  // A gradient/texture tap only changes the data, repaints the text and moves the selected class.
  // The list is rebuilt (scroll kept) only when the chosen fill has no chip yet, e.g. a brand-new custom preset.
  function finishEffectTap(kind){
    const prevTool=activeTool;activeTool=kind;
    const controls=$('evpaControls');
    const grid=controls&&prevTool===kind?controls.querySelector('.evpa-gradient-presets'):null;
    if(grid){
      const cur=effectCurrent(kind);
      const known=cur===''||Array.from(grid.querySelectorAll('.evpa-gradient-chip[data-evpa-fill]')).some(b=>effectChipFill(b)===cur);
      if(known){syncEffectChipSelection(kind);return;}
    }
    const top=grid?grid.scrollTop:0;
    updateDockControls();
    if(top){const g2=($('evpaControls')||{}).querySelector?$('evpaControls').querySelector('.evpa-gradient-presets'):null;if(g2)g2.scrollTop=top;}
  }
  window.ethvellePicsartSetGradient=function(g){styleTarget('textGradient',String(g||''));styleTarget('textTexture','');styleTarget('textTextureSize','100% 100%');finishEffectTap('gradient');};
  window.ethvellePicsartSetTexture=function(g,size){styleTarget('textTexture',String(g||''));styleTarget('textGradient','');styleTarget('textTextureSize',String(size||'100% 100%'));finishEffectTap('texture');};
